Based on this insight, we propose LeanMem, a lightweight long-term memory framework. LeanMem first filters out low-value content, then stores informative segments as compact profile memory, temporally structured event memory, or source-grounded record memory, depending on the nature of the information. During maintenance, only dynamically evolving event memories are selectively updated, avoiding redundant consolidation of stable profiles and immutable records. During inference, LeanMem dynamically selects memory types and allocates retrieval budgets according to query-specific evidence demands, assembling relevant evidence on demand. On LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S with GPT-4.1-mini and Qwen3-8B, LeanMem improves accuracy over the strongest memory-based baseline in every setting, by up to 15.1 points, at the lowest or near-lowest construction cost, inference tokens, and latency. The code and datasets are included in the supplementary materials.

</details>


### [30] [When Correct Solutions Repeat: Rarity-Aware Credit Redistribution for GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03467)
*Zhe Cao,Miaowen Wen,Fangjiong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 可验证奖励RL中的稀有正确解信用再分配，可迁移到agent代码优化的RL训练。

TL;DR: 提出Cue-GRPO，通过稀有性感知的信用重分配来缓解GRPO中结构级信用集中问题，以低开销提升AIME重复采样性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO中正确完成被均匀对待，导致重复出现的形式获得过多信用，稀有形式信用不足，形成结构级偏差，限制了对多样正确策略的探索。

Method: Cue-GRPO使用确定性策略线索构造验证正确轨迹的rollout局部划分，根据簇稀有性重新分配正优势，无需辅助模型。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Math-7B和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，AIME重复采样性能提升，尤其高采样预算；训练开销仅增加6%。

Conclusion: 结构级信用重分配是RLVR的有效设计维度，策略线索提供低开销实现。

中文摘要：基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）通常将每个正确完成视为独立的学习信号。在GRPO中，这种完成级别的均匀性造成了结构级别的偏差：反复出现的正确解形式会按采样频率成比例地累积正系数质量，而稀有形式获得的信用有限。我们将此行为形式化为多重性诱导的结构级信用集中，并提出一种分区条件规则，根据簇稀有性重新分配正优势。Cue-GRPO通过使用确定性策略线索来构造验证正确轨迹的rollout局部划分，无需辅助模型推理即可实例化该规则。在Qwen2.5-Math-7B和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，Cue-GRPO提高了AIME重复采样性能，在高采样预算下提升最大。法官分区（JP）下的信用重分配（CR）进一步表明，所提出的重分配机制可以配合法官派生的分区工作。Cue-GRPO比GRPO仅增加6%的墙钟训练开销。这些结果支持结构级信用重分配作为RLVR的一个实用设计轴，而策略线索为竞赛数学提供了一种低开销实现。代码可在https://github.com/CzZ12/When-Correct-Solutions-Repeat-Rarity-Aware-Credit-Redistribution-for-GRPO获取。

Original abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) com- monly optimizes each correct completion as an independent learning signal. In GRPO, this completion-level uniformity creates structure-level skew: recurring correct solution forms accumulate positive coefficient mass in proportion to how often they are sampled, while rare forms receive limited credit. We formalize this behavior as multiplicity-induced structure-level credit concentration and introduce a partition- conditioned rule that redistributes positive advantages accord- ing to cluster rarity. Cue-GRPO instantiates this rule with- out auxiliary-model inference by using deterministic Strategy Cues to construct rollout-local partitions of verified-correct traces. Across Qwen2.5-Math-7B and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, Cue-GRPO improves AIME repeated-sampling performance, with the largest gains at high sampling budgets. Credit Re- distribution (CR) under Judge Partitions (JP) further indi- cates that the proposed redistribution mechanism can oper- ate with judge-derived partitions. Cue-GRPO adds only 6% wall-clock training overhead over GRPO. These results sup- port structure-level credit redistribution as a practical design axis for RLVR, with Strategy Cues providing a low-overhead implementation for competition mathematics. Code is avail- able at https://github.com/CzZ12/When-Correct-Solutions- Repeat-Rarity-Aware-Credit-Redistribution-for-GRPO.

</details>


### [31] [ToolLIFT: Lifting Tool-Specific Trajectories into Function-Level Graphs for Generalizable Tool Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03468)
*Xiuhui You,Jiayi Luo,Zichao Shen,Qingyun Sun,Ziwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 将工具轨迹提升为功能级工作流图并解耦规划与工具选择，是通用工具规划与经验复用方法。

TL;DR: 提出ToolLIFT框架，将工具特定轨迹提升为函数级工作流图（FWG），实现跨工具集的泛化工具规划，并在多个基准上超越最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具级图方法依赖特定工具，泛化性差；类似任务共享函数级工作流结构，可作为更可迁移的抽象。

Method: ToolLIFT通过轨迹提升机制构建FWG，采用解耦的工作流规划与工具选择，并结合源门控和技能特定奖励的强化学习来确保可靠数据流。

Result: 在2个ID和3个OOD基准上，ToolLIFT一致优于最先进基线，展现出对未见工具集的强泛化能力。

Conclusion: ToolLIFT验证了函数级工作流抽象的有效性，为跨工具集泛化提供了可行方案。

中文摘要：历史工具使用轨迹为大型语言模型（LLM）智能体规划和协调工具使用提供了宝贵的经验。现有方法直接从这些轨迹构建工具级图，但所得图仍然与特定工具绑定，难以跨工具集泛化。为应对这一挑战，我们发现尽管涉及的工具不同，类似任务通常共享共同的函数级工作流结构，这可以作为工具规划中更具可迁移性的抽象。基于这一洞察，我们提出了ToolLIFT，一种将工具特定轨迹提升为函数级工作流图（FWG）以实现泛化工具规划的框架。具体而言，我们首先提出一种轨迹提升机制，在FWG中编码工作流结构并跨工具共享协作经验。然后，基于FWG的全局结构，我们引入解耦的工作流规划和工具选择，使个体工具选择与整体工作流对齐。最后，为确保可靠的工具数据流，我们采用强化学习（RL）并提出源门控和技能特定奖励，以在工具调用间维持可溯源的信息流。在两个分布内（ID）和三个分布外（OOD）基准上的实验表明，ToolLIFT持续优于最先进的基线，展示了对手未见工具集的强大泛化能力。

Original abstract: Historical tool-use trajectories provide valuable experience for large language model (LLM) agents to plan and coordinate tool usage. Existing approaches directly construct tool-level graphs from these trajectories, but the resulting graphs remain tied to specific tools and are hard to generalize across tool sets. To tackle this challenge, we find that despite differences in the tools involved, analogous tasks often share a common function-level workflow structure, which serves as a potentially more transferable abstraction for tool planning. Based on this insight, we propose ToolLIFT, a framework that lifts tool-specific trajectories into a function-level workflow graph (FWG) for generalizable tool planning. Specifically, we first propose a trajectory-lifting mechanism that encodes workflow structures in the FWG and shares collaboration experience across tools. Then, building on the global structure of the FWG, we introduce decoupled workflow planning and tool selection to align individual tool choices with the overall workflow. Lastly, to ensure reliable tool dataflow, we adopt Reinforcement Learning (RL) and propose source-gated and skill-specific rewards to maintain source-traceable information flow across tool calls. Experiments on two in-distribution (ID) and three out-of-distribution (OOD) benchmarks show that ToolLIFT consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, demonstrating strong generalization to unseen tool sets.

</details>


### [32] [WeClawArena: An Auditable Sandbox and Benchmark for Cross-User Agents Collaboration and Security in Human-Centered Agent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03499)
*Prince Zizhuang Wang,Aojie Yuan,Haiyue Zhang,Xiyang Hu,Yue Zhao,Shuli Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 跨用户多agent协作与安全benchmark，带可审计sandbox，对多agent可靠性与安全控制有价值。

TL;DR: WeClawArena is an auditable benchmark and runtime sandbox for multi-party owned-agent collaboration over personal workspaces, with 124 base tasks expanded into 620 scenario variants including attack vectors, measuring utility and attack success separately.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing agent benchmarks study tool use and collaboration but lack an end-to-end sandbox for verifiable cross-user agent collaboration with realistic user digital workspaces, and do not test how harmful actions travel through human-centered agent networks.

Method: WeClawArena provides a runtime sandbox that records peer messages, tool calls, resource operations, governed decisions, and workspace states. It includes 124 base tasks across six domains, expanded to 620 variants with one benign control and four attack-vector variants per task.

Result: The benchmark reports utility and attack success rate separately, and audits attack success from bounded runtime evidence, enabling diagnosis of task breakdown, privacy leakage, poisoned evidence, and invalid authority paths.

Conclusion: WeClawArena enables auditable evaluation of multi-party agent collaboration and security, supporting realistic personal workspace constraints and attack vector analysis.

中文摘要：近期在持久化个人智能体框架方面的进展，使得以人为中心的智能体网络成为现实部署目标：每个用户都可以由一个代表用户行事的AI智能体提供服务，该智能体维护状态，并通过社交和任务关系与其他智能体通信。在这些网络中，日常工具使用变成了多方拥有的智能体在个人工作空间上的协作，其中文件、记录、工具和策略对所有者的直接可见性受限。现有的智能体基准研究工具使用和协作，但并未提供端到端的沙箱来验证跨用户智能体协作，且缺乏真实的用户数字工作空间，也未测试有害行为如何通过以人为中心的智能体网络传播。我们引入了WeClawArena，一个可审计的基准和运行时沙箱，用于多方拥有的智能体在个人工作空间上的协作。WeClawArena针对协作式工具使用任务，其中个人工作空间既充当操作工具又充当个人约束。该基准包含六个跨用户任务域的124个基础任务，并将其扩展为620个场景变体，每个基础任务有一个良性对照和四个攻击向量变体。沙箱记录对等消息、工具调用、资源操作、治理决策和最终工作空间状态。WeClawArena分别报告效用和攻击成功率，并从有界运行时证据审计攻击成功，支持诊断任务中断、隐私泄露、投毒证据和无效授权路径。

Original abstract: Recent advances in persistent personal-agent frameworks are making human-centered agent networks realistic deployment targets: each user can be served by an AI agent that acts on the user's behalf, maintains state, and communicates with other agents through social and task relations. In these networks, everyday tool use becomes multi-party owned-agent collaboration over personal workspaces, where files, records, tools, and policies are not directly visible across owners. Existing agent benchmarks study tool use and collaboration, but they do not provide an end-to-end sandbox for verifiable cross-user agent collaboration with realistic user digital workspaces or test how harmful actions can travel through the human-centered agent network. We introduce WeClawArena, an auditable benchmark and runtime sandbox for multi-party owned-agent collaboration over personal workspaces. WeClawArena targets collaborative tool-use tasks in which personal workspaces serve as both operational tools and personal constraints. The benchmark contains 124 base tasks across six cross-user task domains and expands them into 620 scenario variants, with one benign control and four attack-vector variants per base task. The sandbox records peer messages, tool calls, resource operations, governed decisions, and final workspace states. WeClawArena reports utility and attack success rate separately and audits attack success from bounded runtime evidence, supporting diagnosis of task breakdown, privacy leakage, poisoned evidence, and invalid authority paths.

</details>


### [33] [Can LLM design high-quality experiments? A Comprehensive and Systematic Benchmark on Autonomous Experimental Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03501)
*Zejun Liu,Jian Wu,Ru Peng,Yuliang Ji,Dongyuan Li,Renhe Jiang,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: SCOPE 基准与 OptED agentic workflow 评估并优化 LLM 的实验设计，是通用 agent 评测与工作流方法，可迁移到 SemOpt。

TL;DR: 提出了SCOPE基准来评估LLM在实验设计中的能力，发现现有LLM存在明显不足，并提出了OptED工作流来优化实验设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI4Research研究忽视实验设计阶段，且缺乏系统评估LLM实验设计能力的基准。

Method: 构建SCOPE基准，从高层规划完整性和低层配置准确性两个维度评估LLM；提出OptED智能体工作流，通过阶段隔离、工具增强和规则约束优化实验设计。

Result: SCOPE评估显示大多数LLM无法直接设计高质量实验，所有LLM在低层配置上存在性能瓶颈，搜索模式不能提升设计质量；OptED有效缓解了配置瓶颈。

Conclusion: 实验设计是AI4Research中挑战性且重要的环节，OptED提供了一种可行方案，但LLM在此任务上仍有较大提升空间。

中文摘要：AI for Research (AI4Research) 利用人工智能来自动化和改进科学工作流程。虽然实验设计是研究过程中的关键阶段，但先前的工作主要集中于代码实现和执行，忽视了这一阶段的重要性，并且没有基准测试来评估人工智能进行系统性实验设计的能力。为了弥补这一空白，我们提出了SCOPE，一个科学综合规划评估基准，该基准由来自顶级会议（如ICML、NeurIPS和ICLR）的19个研究领域的300篇高质量最新论文构建而成，从两个维度评估大型语言模型（LLM）：高层规划的完整性（主要实验、消融实验和分析实验）以及低层配置的准确性与合理性（数据集、基线和指标）。基准测试揭示了三个发现：（1）大多数LLM无法直接设计高质量的实验；（2）所有LLM在低层配置方面都表现出性能瓶颈；（3）搜索模式并不能提高设计质量。此外，为了应对这些挑战，我们提出了OptED，一种新颖的智能体工作流程，用于优化基于LLM的实验设计，通过阶段隔离、工具增强和基于规则的约束来增强基于LLM的实验规划，有效缓解配置瓶颈。

Original abstract: AI for Research (AI4Research) leverages AI to automate and improve scientific workflows. While experimental design is a critical stage of the research process, prior work has focused primarily on code implementation and execution, overlooking the importance of this stage, and no benchmark exists to evaluate AI's ability to conduct systematic experiment design. To bridge this gap, we propose SCOPE, a Scientific COmprehensive Planning Evaluation Benchmark constructed from 300 high-quality latest papers across 19 research domains from top-tier venues (e.g., ICML, NeurIPS, and ICLR),evaluating LLMs on two dimensions: High-Level planning completeness (main, ablation, and analysis experiments) and Low-Level configuration accuracy and rationality (datasets, baselines, and metrics). Benchmarking reveals three findings: (1) most LLMs cannot directly design high-quality experiments; (2) all LLMs exhibit a performance bottleneck in low-level configuration; and (3) search mode does not improve design quality. Furthermore, to address these challenges, we propose OptED, a novel agentic workflow to optimize LLM-based experimental design, that enhances LLM-based experimental planning through stage isolation, tool augmentation, and rule-based constraints, effectively alleviating the configuration bottleneck.

</details>


### [34] [Hybrid LLM-Augmented Reinforcement Learning Agents for Complex Sequential Decision Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03502)
*Christophe D. Hounwanou,John Emeka Eze,Yaé Ulrich Gaba*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出 LLM 规划与 RL 动作优化结合的混合 agent 架构，对长程 sequential 任务的 coding agent 设计有直接启发。

TL;DR: 提出一种LLM增强的强化学习智能体，结合LLM的规划能力和RL的动作优化，在序贯决策任务上优于各自单独的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体在长期序贯决策中缺乏精确动作优化和交互能力，而RL虽擅长序贯控制但缺乏高层抽象和任务分解能力，两者互补，因此需要混合方法。

Method: 设计一种LLM增强的RL智能体架构：LLM负责生成子目标、结构化计划和情境指导，RL智能体通过与环境交互细化底层动作，两者协同完成决策。

Result: 实验显示，与RL-only和LLM-only基线相比，该方法在序贯决策任务上实现了更高的样本效率、更高的成功率和更连贯的动作轨迹。

Conclusion: LLM与RL的混合范式为构建更强大的自主系统提供了有前景的方向。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）近来在推理、规划和使用工具方面展现出强大的能力，从而催生了新形式的自主智能体。然而，基于LLM的智能体在需要精确动作优化和环境交互的长期序贯决策任务中表现不佳。强化学习（RL）虽然对序贯控制有效，但往往缺乏复杂场景所需的高层抽象和任务分解能力。本文介绍了一种LLM增强的强化学习智能体，将LLM驱动的规划与基于RL的动作优化相结合。所提出的架构利用LLM生成子目标、结构化计划和情境指导，而RL智能体通过与环境的交互来优化底层动作。在序贯决策任务上的实验表明，与仅使用RL和仅使用LLM的基线相比，该方法提高了样本效率、成功率和动作轨迹的一致性。这种混合范式为构建更强大的自主系统指明了一个有前景的方向。

Original abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently shown strong capabilities in reasoning, planning, and tool-use, enabling new forms of autonomous agents. However, LLM-based agents struggle with long-horizon sequential decision tasks that require precise action optimization and environment interaction. Reinforcement Learning (RL), while effective for sequential control, often lacks the high-level abstraction and task decomposition abilities needed for complex scenarios. This paper introduces an LLM-Augmented Reinforcement Learning Agent that integrates LLM-driven planning with RL-based action optimization. The proposed architecture leverages the LLM to generate subgoals, structured plans, and contextual guidance, while the RL agent refines low-level actions through interaction with the environment. Experiments on sequential decision tasks demonstrate improved sample efficiency, higher success rates, and more coherent action trajectories compared to RL-only and LLM-only baselines. This hybrid paradigm highlights a promising direction for building more capable autonomous systems.

</details>


### [35] [When Many Answers Are Valid, Voting Fails: Symbolic Verification for Best-of-K Causal Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03506)
*Omatharv Bharat Vaidya,Connor Thomas Jerzak,Zayne Rea Sprague,Fangcong Yin,Nhat Ho*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: CALVER 用符号验证从多个候选推理中选最优，这种验证聚合机制可借鉴到 agent 输出可靠性与正确性筛选。

TL;DR: CALVER 是一个无需训练的符号验证器，通过因果公理对推理轨迹评分，显著提升因果推理中的答案选择准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自一致性方法在因果推理中失效，因为常见错误重复出现且有效答案分散，导致无效答案常被选中。

Method: CALVER 基于 Pearl 因果标准（d-分离、后门调整、干预）对结构化轨迹评分，无需参考答案，选择得分最高的候选。

Result: 在 CLEAR 任务中达到 42.1%，明显优于多数投票等方法（约 30%），并在多个模型中复现，支持因果图从文本构建。

Conclusion: CALVER 证明因果有效性可作为一种可靠的聚合准则，仅需因果结构即可提升推理选择性能。

中文摘要：自一致性假设在采样推理轨迹中出现最频繁的答案是最可靠的，但这在因果推理中可能失效：样本常常重复相同的混杂错误，并且投票分散在多个有效答案之间，使得无效答案获胜，尽管存在有效的少数轨迹。我们引入了 CALVER（因果公理级验证），一个无需训练的符号验证器，它根据 Pearl 的因果标准对结构化轨迹进行评分，包括 d-分离、后门调整和干预，并在不参考参考答案的情况下选择得分最高的候选。在 CLEAR 的 find-one-valid 查询中，该查询允许多个图有效答案，CALVER 达到 42.1%，而多数投票、奖励模型、LLM 裁判和模型置信度在相同的冻结池上均接近 30%。将裁判扩展到 72B 并未缩小差距。在一个经过审计的干净核心子集中，21 个图有效 CALVER 选择中有 11 个与基准列出的答案不同，但仍满足请求的谓词。随着采样预算的增加，优势扩大，并在十个已发表的贝叶斯网络、第二个模型系列以及模型必须从文本构建图的设置中重现。CALVER 还改进了针对精确真实值的阈值化平均处理效应决策，在真值表检查器下推广到逻辑，并在 CPU 上以毫秒级对每个候选进行评分。CALVER 只需要一个因果结构，可以直接提供或从文本构建；只要满足这一点，选择就可以通过因果有效性进行聚合。

Original abstract: Self-consistency assumes the most frequent answer among sampled reasoning traces is the most reliable, but this can fail in causal reasoning: samples often repeat the same confounding error, and votes fragment across multiple valid answers, letting an invalid answer win despite a valid minority trace. We introduce CALVER (Causal Axiom-Level VERification), a training-free symbolic verifier that scores structured traces against Pearl's causal criteria, including -separation, backdoor adjustment, and intervention, and selects the highest-scoring candidate without consulting a reference answer. On CLEAR find-one-valid queries that admit multiple graph-valid answers, CALVER reaches 42.1% where plurality, a reward model, an LLM judge, and model confidence remain near 30% on identical frozen pools. Scaling the judge to 72B does not close the gap. In an audited clean-core subset, 11 of 21 graph-valid CALVER selections differ from the benchmark's listed answer while still satisfying the requested predicate. The advantage widens with the sampling budget and reproduces across ten published Bayesian networks, a second model family, and settings where the model must build the graph from text. CALVER also improves thresholded average-treatment-effect decisions against exact ground truth, generalizes to logic under a truth-table checker, and scores each candidate in milliseconds on CPU. CALVER needs only a causal structure, supplied outright or built from the text; wherever that holds, selection can aggregate via causal validity.

</details>


### [36] [Adversarial Fast-Moving Real-World Domains as Test Beds for Benchmarking AI Scientist Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03569)
*William Bolton,Philip Torr*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 以 F1 与 MTG 这类对抗性真实领域作为 AI 科学家能力测试床，其 idea 生成/筛选评测框架可迁移到 agent 研究。

TL;DR: 提出用复杂真实世界领域（F1和MTG）作为AI科学家新想法生成能力的基准，发现模型能产生合理想法但很少匹配专家，关键差距在筛选和连贯新颖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准依赖合成或回顾性任务，可能受先前暴露干扰，需要能评估推理、新颖性和假设形成的真实世界框架。

Method: 在两个真实世界领域（F1赛车设计和MTG套牌构建）中，让模型提出想法，并与专家实际创新或顶尖选手套牌对比，分析匹配程度和相关性。

Result: F1中最佳模型匹配10/40创新；MTG中最佳套牌找回5/7新卡，且模型卡牌选择与PT套牌采用显著相关（ρ=0.74）。

Conclusion: AI科学家能生成看似合理的想法，但缺乏筛选和聚焦于连贯新颖性的能力，这比想法生成本身更关键。

中文摘要：衡量AI科学家生成新颖想法的能力是出了名的困难。该领域的现有基准在评估科学推理和研究复现方面取得了进展，但通常依赖于合成任务或回顾性目标，这些可能受到先前暴露的混淆影响。我们假设，复杂、对抗性强、快速变化的真实世界领域，其中专家从业者独立生成可观测的输出，可以提供一种实用的解决方案来填补这一空白，并评估AI科学家所需的能力，包括推理、新颖性和假设形成。我们在两个结构不同的领域实例化了这一框架：一级方程式（F1），其中模型围绕2026赛季的赛车设计概念进行构思，而真实的季前创新提供了基准真值；以及《万智牌》（MTG），其中模型从最近更新的卡池中提出套牌，并根据19个职业巡回赛（PT）套牌列表进行评估。在两个领域中，模型都产生了看似合理的输出，但很少有与真实世界专家解决方案一致的。在F1中，最佳模型GPT-5.2在多次运行中提出166个想法，匹配了40个真实创新中的10个。在MTG中，来自Gemini 3 Flash的最佳套牌从排名第三的PT套牌中找回了7张新系列卡牌中的5张，并且在所有108套牌中，模型最常选择的卡牌也是PT套牌中采用最广泛的卡牌（Spearman ρ=0.74，p=0.0003）。这些结果表明，AI科学家的关键能力差距不在于想法生成，而在于过滤、优先级排序和连贯的新颖性。

Original abstract: Benchmarking the ability of AI scientists to generate novel ideas is notoriously difficult. Existing benchmarks in this field have made progress in evaluating scientific reasoning and research replication, but often rely on synthetic tasks or retrospective targets, which may be confounded by prior exposure. We hypothesize that complex, adversarial, fast-moving real-world domains where expert practitioners independently generate observable outputs can provide a practical solution to fill this gap and evaluate the capabilities needed for AI scientists, including reasoning, novelty, and hypothesis formulation. We instantiate this framework in two structurally different domains, Formula 1 (F1), where models ideate around car design concepts for the 2026 season, and real pre-season innovations provide a ground truth, and Magic: The Gathering (MTG), where models propose decks from a recently updated card pool and are evaluated against 19 Pro Tour (PT) decklists. Across both domains, models produce plausible outputs, but few align with real-world expert solutions. In F1, the best model, GPT-5.2 matched 10 of 40 real innovations with 166 ideas proposed across runs. In MTG, the best deck from Gemini 3 Flash recovered 5 of 7 new-set cards from the third-place PT deck, and across all 108 decks, the cards models selected most often were also the cards most widely adopted by PT decks (Spearman $ρ= 0.74$, $p = 0.0003$). These results suggest that a key capability gap for AI scientists is not idea generation, but filtering, prioritization, and coherent novelty.

</details>


### [37] [From Social Coding to Agentic Coding: Productivity and Relational Reconfiguration in Open-Source Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03585)
*Mengying Zhou,Yongjie Yin,Yang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 用多 agent 模拟研究编码 agent 对开源社区的生产力与知识影响，是 coding agent 的重要实证结论。

TL;DR: 编码代理能提升开发效率但削弱公共知识生成，导致生产力与公共知识之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究生成式编码代理对开源社区的影响，特别是其将活动从公共交互转向私有循环，从而可能改变公共知识生成与人际关系网络。

Method: 基于真实GitHub数据（1,084名开发者）构建LLM多智能体模拟，在历史提交热身后分设无CA和有CA两个平行条件，进行4周模拟对比。

Result: CA使计划和完成任务分别增加34.0%和39.0%，中位完成时间从45分钟降至20分钟；但采纳率仅26.0%，收益集中于高活跃高连接开发者；人-人交互从32.4%降至11.6%，CA参与模式升至57.3%；CA语料知识覆盖率仅22.3%，远低于真实人类语料库的81.1%。

Conclusion: 编码代理虽提高技术产出，但造成公共知识流失和后续贡献支持下降，形成生产力与公共知识的矛盾，需要平衡效率与开放性。

中文摘要：开源软件社区是一种数字公共基础设施，不仅产生代码，还通过可见的协作生成公共知识和人际关系。生成式编码代理（CAs）是一种提高开发效率的先进工具，同时将部分活动从公共人际交互转移到私有的人机循环中。我们使用基于LLM的多智能体模拟来研究这种转变，该模拟基于来自1,084名活跃开发者及其仓库关系的真实GitHub数据进行初始化。在历史提交热身后，我们将相同的社区状态分支到并行无CA和有CA的条件下，进行为期4周的模拟。引入CA使计划任务和完成任务分别增加34.0%和39.0%，并将中位完成时间从45分钟缩短至20分钟。然而，采用率仅为26.0%，且收益集中在已经更活跃且连接更广泛的开发者中。CA还重构了任务执行路径。直接的人与人交互从32.4%下降到11.6%，而涉及CA的模式增加到57.3%，其中40.3%通过CA辅助的自我循环完成。CA条件下生成的公共知识对后续任务的支持也较少。在标准化的检索基准上，CA语料库的知识覆盖率为22.3%，远低于真实人类语料库的81.1%，并且需要更多的检索步骤且成功率较低。这些结果揭示了生产力与公共知识之间的张力：编码代理提高了技术产出，但更多工作转向代理中介或私有循环，使公共记录对未来贡献者的用处降低。

Original abstract: Open-source software communities are a form of digital public infrastructure that not only produces code, but also generates public knowledge and interpersonal relationships through visible collaboration. Generative coding agents (CAs) are an advanced tool to improve development efficiency while shifting part of activities from public human interaction to private human-agent loops. We study this shift using an LLM-based multi-agent simulation initialized with real GitHub data from 1,084 active developers and their repository relationships. After a warm-up with historical commits, we branch the same community state into parallel No-CA and CA conditions for 4-week simulations. CA introduction increases planned and completed tasks by 34.0% and 39.0%, respectively, and reduces median completion time from 45 to 20 minutes. However, adoption reaches only 26.0%, and the gains concentrate among developers who are already more active and well connected. CAs also restructure task execution pathways. Direct human-human interaction declines from 32.4% to 11.6%, while CA-involved modes increase to 57.3%, including 40.3% completed through CA-assisted self-loops. Public knowledge generated under CA condition also provides less support for later tasks. On a standardized retrieval benchmark, the CA corpus achieves 22.3% knowledge coverage, far below the 81.1% achieved by the real-human corpus, and requires more retrieval steps with a lower success rate. These results reveal a productivity-public knowledge tension: coding agents increase technical production, but more work shifts to agent-mediated or private loops, leaving public records less useful to future contributors.

</details>


### [38] [Large language models for partial differential equation workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03600)
*Han Wan,Rui Zhang,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 综述 LLM 辅助 PDE 工作流中的代码生成与求解，可视为科学计算领域的 coding agent 界面进展。

TL;DR: 本文综述了大型语言模型在偏微分方程工作流中的三个关键阶段（模型发现、求解器生成、模拟反馈应用）的近期进展，指出现有系统主要作为工作流级接口，但受限于数据稀缺和模拟与现实差距等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏微分方程在科学和工程中的实用性依赖于完整的工作流，而现有工具难以有效整合语言、数学、代码和模拟。作者希望系统性梳理LLM在该领域的进展和局限性，以推动科学AI系统的发展。

Method: 本文以综述方式，将LLM辅助PDE研究划分为三个阶段：发现与公式化、求解器生成与修订、利用模拟反馈支持控制/设计/优化，并分别分析每个阶段的最新进展、系统角色和当前瓶颈。

Result: 当前系统主要充当工作流级接口，但高质量数据集和基准稀缺，尤其是知识发现和实际应用；此外，模拟结果与现实系统之间存在持续差距，阻碍了直接迁移。

Conclusion: LLM辅助PDE工作流是开发科学AI系统的关键试验场，需连接语言、计算、物理约束和现实世界决策，但必须克服数据稀缺和模拟-现实差距等挑战。

中文摘要：偏微分方程（PDE）在科学与工程中之所以具有可操作性，并非作为孤立的公式，而是作为连接建模假设、控制方程、数值求解器、诊断和决策的可执行工作流。大型语言模型（LLM）正开始通过连接自然语言、符号数学、代码、求解器输出和反馈来支持此类工作流。在此，我们考察了LLM辅助PDE研究在三个阶段的近期进展：控制模型的发现与公式化、可执行数值求解器的生成与修订，以及利用模拟反馈支持控制、设计与优化。在这些阶段中，当前系统主要充当工作流级接口。尽管取得了这些进展，该领域仍受限于高质量数据集和基准的稀缺，尤其是在知识发现和实际应用方面，这些领域需要专家标注、可执行问题构造和任务级反馈，而这些都需要大量的领域工作。另一个挑战是模拟结果与现实世界科学与工程系统之间持续存在的差距，这限制了数值模拟、控制策略和优化设计向实际环境的直接迁移。这些挑战使得LLM辅助PDE工作流成为开发科学AI系统的关键试验场，这类系统需能够连接语言、计算、物理约束和现实世界决策。

Original abstract: Partial differential equations (PDEs) become actionable in science and engineering not as isolated formulae, but as executable workflows that connect modelling assumptions, governing equations, numerical solvers, diagnostics, and decisions. Large language models (LLMs) are beginning to support such workflows by linking natural language, symbolic mathematics, code, solver outputs, and feedback. Here we examine recent advances in LLM-assisted PDE research across three stages: the discovery and formulation of governing models, the generation and revision of executable numerical solvers, and the use of simulation feedback to support control, design, and optimization. Across these stages, current systems act primarily as workflow-level interfaces. Despite this progress, the field remains limited by the scarcity of high-quality datasets and benchmarks, especially for knowledge discovery and real-world applications, where expert annotation, executable problem construction, and task-level feedback require substantial domain effort. A further challenge is the persistent gap between simulation-based results and real-world scientific and engineering systems, which limits the direct transfer of numerical simulations, control policies, and optimized designs to practical settings. These challenges make LLM-assisted PDE workflows a critical testbed for developing scientific AI systems that can connect language, computation, physical constraints, and real-world decision-making.

</details>


### [39] [Learning Clinical-Trial Strategy: Offline Policy Training for Decision Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03606)
*William Bolton,Philip Torr*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 从临床决策中离线训练策略 agent，reward-weighted BC 等方法可迁移到 coding agent 的轨迹学习。

TL;DR: 本研究将肿瘤药物开发建模为离线决策问题，构建结构化数据集并比较多种离线学习目标与LLM代理，证明离线学习能有效预测临床试验组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床开发是在不确定性下的顺序决策，现有方法缺乏对异质性证据的整合和规划能力，需要一种能利用历史数据学习规划策略的框架。

Method: 构建包含31.7k条公共记录的时间数据集，形成881个离线决策片段；比较行为克隆、奖励加权行为克隆等四种离线目标与四个LLM代理，使用日期门控检索和多个数据分割进行评估。

Result: 离线训练模型优于未微调基线，奖励加权行为克隆最佳，指示F1达46.2%，严格F1为14.2%，远超最佳工具代理（25.0%和2.1%）。

Conclusion: 结构化离线学习能有效教会代理规划临床实验，为肿瘤药物开发决策提供数据驱动的方法支持。

中文摘要：临床开发是在不确定性下的顺序决策，其中申办者必须规划来自异质性证据的实验组合。我们通过将肿瘤学临床开发构建为一个离线决策问题来研究这一设置，其中代理从决策日期可用的信息预测肿瘤药物项目未来六个月的试验组合。为此，我们构建了一个时间数据集，将31.7k条异质性公共数据记录（包括试验注册、监管审查、申办者申报、使用数据和流行病学）整合到45个历史项目的881个离线决策片段中。我们比较了四种离线目标：行为克隆、奖励加权行为克隆、学习奖励训练和基于价值的隐式Q学习，与共享共同日期门控检索框架的四个前沿LLM代理，跨越留出的药物、申办者、药物类别和时间分割。离线训练的模型优于未微调的基线，特别是在2025年8月后无污染保留中。奖励加权行为克隆表现最佳，在指示F1上获得46.2%，严格F1获得14.2%，而最佳工具代理在每个指标上分别为25.0%和2.1%。这些结果表明，结构化离线学习可以教会代理规划临床实验。

Original abstract: Clinical development is sequential decision-making under uncertainty, where a sponsor must plan a portfolio of experiments from heterogeneous evidence. We study this setting by framing oncology clinical development as an offline decision-making problem in which an agent predicts the next six-month trial portfolio of an oncology drug program from information available at the decision date. To support this, we construct a temporal dataset that combines 31.7k heterogeneous public data records, including trial registries, regulatory reviews, sponsor filings, utilization data, and epidemiology, into 881 offline decision episodes across 45 historical programs. We compare four offline objectives: behavioral cloning, reward-weighted behavioral cloning, learned-reward training, and value-based implicit Q-learning against four frontier LLM agents that share a common date-gated retrieval scaffold across held-out drug, sponsor, drug-class, and temporal splits. Models trained offline outperform the non-fine-tuned baselines, particularly in the post-August 2025 contamination-clean holdout. Reward-weighted behavioral cloning performs the best, obtaining 46.2% indication F1 and 14.2% strict F1 against 25.0% and 2.1%, respectively, for the best-performing tool agent on each metric. These results suggest that structured offline learning can teach agents to plan clinical experiments.

</details>


### [40] [Formal Verification of Agentic Systems over Operational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03609)
*Alejandro J. Mercado,Alessio Lomuscio*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 为 LLM agentic 系统提供形式化验证框架（STEAD/FO-CTL），是 agent 可靠性与安全控制的通用贡献。

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM驱动的智能体系统在关系数据上的验证问题，形式化为STEAD模型，证明了FO-CTL验证不可判定，但在有限域和标识符重命名条件下可判定（PSPACE完全），并提出了规范部署包装器来保证该条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的智能体系统在实际工作流程中处理持久化操作数据，部署前需要验证其满足业务需求。现有方法只关注智能体接口层面的约束或行为分析，无法提供系统级保证（如数据演化和工作流执行的正确性），因此需要新的验证框架。

Method: 将智能体系统形式化为有状态工具启用型智能体部署（STEAD），定义其语义和FO-CTL规范验证问题。分析证明一般情况不可判定，然后通过有限域限制和标识符重命名条件找到可判定子类，并设计规范部署包装器强制满足条件，同时保持等变性。

Result: 验证FO-CTL规范在一般情况下不可判定；在有限域且满足标识符重命名条件下是PSPACE完全的；LLM智能体可能不满足该条件，但规范部署包装器可保证任意基础智能体满足；计算规范表示是图同构困难的。

Conclusion: 本文为LLM智能体系统提供了可判定的验证框架，通过规范部署包装器实现了可验证性，并以案例管理工作流为例展示了实用性，为可靠部署LLM智能体奠定了基础。

中文摘要：由大型语言模型（LLM）驱动的智能体系统正越来越多地部署在现实世界的工作流程中，在这些流程中它们对持久化的操作数据进行操作。在部署之前，这些系统需要根据管理工作流执行和数据演化的业务需求进行验证。然而，现有方法并未提供此类系统级保证，因为它们主要是在智能体的接口层面约束或分析行为。我们在此研究由单个LLM和工具编排框架组成的智能体系统在关系操作数据上的验证问题。我们将其形式化为有状态的工具启用型智能体部署（STEAD），给出其语义，定义针对一阶计算树逻辑（FO-CTL）规范验证它们的问题，并证明该问题不可判定。我们确定了在有限域限制下精确保持FO-CTL规范的充分条件，在该限制下验证是PSPACE完全的。关键要求是，重命名数据中的不透明标识符必须相应地重命名所选的工具调用。我们表明，LLM驱动的智能体可能违反此条件，并引入一种规范的部署包装器，该包装器对于任意基础智能体都能保证此条件，同时保持已有的等变行为。我们证明了此构造所需的规范表示的计算是图同构困难的。最后，我们在一个编排案例管理工作流的LLM智能体上说明了我们的框架。

Original abstract: Agentic systems driven by large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in real-world workflows where they act on persistent operational data. Before deployment, these systems need to be verified against business requirements that govern workflow execution and data evolution. However, existing approaches do not provide such system-level guarantees, as they mainly constrain or analyse behaviour at the agent's interface level. We study here the verification of agentic systems comprising a single LLM and a tool orchestration harness over relational operational data. We formalise them as Stateful Tool-Enabled Agentic Deployments (STEADs), give their semantics, define the problem of verifying them against First-Order Computation Tree Logic (FO-CTL) specifications, and show that it is undecidable. We identify sufficient conditions for exact preservation of FO-CTL specifications under a finite-domain restriction, over which verification is PSPACE-complete. The key requirement is that renaming opaque identifiers in the data must correspondingly rename the selected tool calls. We show that LLM-driven agents can violate this condition and introduce a canonical deployment wrapper that guarantees it for arbitrary base agents while preserving already-equivariant behaviour. We prove that computing canonical representations required by this construction is graph-isomorphism-hard. Finally, we illustrate our framework on an LLM agent orchestrating a case-management workflow.

</details>


### [41] [AutoSND: From Execution Evidence to Structural Policies for Automated Network Dismantling Heuristic Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03653)
*Zhijing Hu,Changjun Fan,Yufan Deng,Zhiguang Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 用LLM自动搜索网络拆解启发式程序，基于执行证据进行树搜索和结构策略引导，可迁移到自动代码/算法优化agent设计。

TL;DR: 提出AutoSND，一种三阶段树搜索框架，利用大型语言模型自动设计网络拆解程序，通过将执行证据转化为结构策略，生成质量和速度优化的候选方案，在真实网络上表现更优且结构可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络拆解启发式方法依赖人工设计，而基于大模型的自动设计方法虽能生成候选，却无法将执行中的失败或质量转化为结构级指导，导致候选质量提升受限。

Method: AutoSND采用三阶段树搜索：阶段一广泛探索简单启发式并归档执行证据；阶段二将候选记录编译为涉及局部信号、邻域访问和状态更新范围的结构化策略；阶段三在策略指导下继续树搜索，最终得到质量优先和速度优先的候选程序。

Result: 在12个真实网络和3个大型网络上，AutoSND相比现有方法取得更好的搜索性能和稳定性，发现更具竞争力且结构可解释的拆解程序，代码已开源。

Conclusion: AutoSND证明了将执行证据转化为结构策略能有效提升自动启发式设计的质量，其最终候选形成以残差度为骨干、受限局部信号调整节点顺序的可解释结构，为复杂网络拆解提供新思路。

中文摘要：网络拆解是分析复杂系统鲁棒性和脆弱性的基础，然而实用的启发式方法必须平衡有效性和计算效率，并且通常由研究人员手动设计。基于大型语言模型的现有自动启发式设计方法可以生成并筛选候选方案，但难以将执行过程中的候选质量或失败状态转化为结构级别的指导，以用于后续生成。我们提出了AutoSND，一个用于完整网络拆解程序的三阶段树搜索框架。第一阶段从简单启发式方法进行广泛探索，并归档执行证据。第二阶段将候选记录编译为关于局部信号、邻域访问和状态更新范围的结构化策略。第三阶段在这些策略的指导下继续树搜索，并获得最终的质量优先和速度优先的候选方案，即AutoSND-Q/S。在12个真实世界网络和3个大型真实世界网络上的实验表明，AutoSND实现了更好的搜索性能和稳定性，并发现了更具竞争力和结构可解释性的网络拆解程序。最终候选方案形成了一种可解释的结构，以残差度为主干，用有界局部信号调整节点顺序，并限制状态更新范围。代码可在https://github.com/MirrorNew/AutoSND获取。

Original abstract: Network dismantling is fundamental to analyzing the robustness and vulnerability of complex systems, yet practical heuristics must balance effectiveness and computational efficiency, and are usually designed manually by researchers. Existing large language model based automatic heuristic design methods can generate and screen candidates, yet they have difficulty further transforming candidate quality or failure states during execution into structural-level guid- ance for subsequent generation. We propose AutoSND, a three stage tree search framework for complete network dismantling pro- grams. Stage I broadly explores from simple heuristics and archives execution evidence. Stage II compiles candidate records into struc- tural policies concerning local signals, neighborhood access, and state update ranges. Stage III continues tree search conditioned on these policies and obtains the final quality prioritized and speed prioritized candidates, AutoSND-Q/S. Experiments on 12 real world networks and 3 large real world networks show that AutoSND achieves better search performance and stability and discovers more competitive and structurally interpretable network disman- tling programs. The final candidates form an interpretable structure that uses residual degree as the backbone, adjusts node order with bounded local signals, and restricts the state update range. Code is available at https://github.com/MirrorNew/AutoSND.

</details>


### [42] [TARL: Transaction-Aware Reliable Ledgers for Executable Memory Management in Long-Term Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03699)
*Han Xiao,Hongjun Xu,Xin Zhang,Yidong Chen,Xiaodong Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出长程agent可执行记忆状态更新框架与基准，区分新增/修订/拒绝等动作，对agent记忆与自我改进设计有迁移价值。

TL;DR: TARL是一个将内存更新细分为五种可执行操作并训练模型选择最优操作的框架，显著提升长期智能体的内存状态恢复并减少污染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持久化内存系统使用二元写/保持决策，无法区分添加、忽略、修正、拒绝或推迟等不同更新意图，导致错误更新反复扭曲未来推理。

Method: TARL框架将每个语句映射为五种执行操作，通过识别受影响内存、解析时间范围、比较来源可靠性并维护三个账本（接受、待定、拒绝），同时利用替代操作产生的内存状态对比进行训练。

Result: 在引入的TARL-Mem基准上，TARL在域内、跨来源、时间、反事实和顺序评估中均提升了动作预测和状态恢复，减少了内存污染，保留了冲突证据，限制了累积损坏。

Conclusion: TARL通过细粒度可执行操作替代二元决策，为长期智能体提供了更可靠的内存更新机制，有效降低错误传播风险。

中文摘要：持久化内存帮助长期智能体保留知识，但一次更新错误可能会反复扭曲未来的检索和推理。现有大多数系统将内存更新简化为二元写/保持（Write/Hold）决策，无法区分新信息是应被添加、忽略、用于修正过时信念、拒绝为不可靠，还是推迟验证。这些选择可能共享相同的二元标签，却产生根本不同的内存状态。我们提出TARL，一种内存状态更新框架，将每个语句映射为五种可执行操作之一。TARL识别受影响的内存，解析其时间范围，比较来源可靠性，并更新已接受、待定和已拒绝的账本。它通过比较替代更新操作产生的内存状态来进一步训练，鼓励模型选择导致正确结果的操作。我们还引入了TARL-Mem，一个具有细粒度操作标签和下一状态目标的基准。在域内、跨来源、时间、反事实和顺序评估中，TARL改善了操作预测和状态恢复，减少了内存污染，保留了冲突证据，并限制了累积损坏。完整的模型实现见补充材料。

Original abstract: Persistent memory helps long-term agents retain knowledge, yet a single update error can repeatedly distort future retrieval and reasoning. Most existing systems reduce memory updating to a binary Write/Hold decision, which cannot distinguish whether new information should be added, ignored, used to revise an outdated belief, rejected as unreliable, or deferred for verification. These choices may share the same binary label while producing fundamentally different memory states. We introduce TARL, a memory state update framework that maps each statement to one of five executable actions. TARL identifies the affected memory, resolves its temporal scope, compares source reliability, and updates accepted, pending, and rejected ledgers. It is further trained by comparing the memory states produced by alternative update operations, encouraging the model to select the operation that leads to the correct result. We also introduce TARL-Mem, a benchmark with fine-grained action labels and next-state targets. Across in-domain, cross-source, temporal, counterfactual, and sequential evaluations, TARL improves action prediction and state recovery, reduces memory pollution, preserves conflicting evidence, and limits cumulative corruption. The complete model implementation is provided in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [43] [When Outputs Disperse, Does Epistemic Revision Follow? A Black-Box Coupling Diagnostic for Machine Collectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03722)
*Molood Arman*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出黑盒诊断评估LLM集体分歧与立场修正的耦合，对多agent协作和可靠性评测有启发。

TL;DR: 提出一个黑盒诊断框架，通过一致性指数和逐轮立场标注来衡量LLM集体中输出离散度与真实认知修正的耦合程度，并在两个模型配置上验证了条件性异议对错误前提恢复的差异影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM集体中看似多样的输出可能仅保留相同结论，导致分歧不再是认知多样性的可靠代理；需要区分真实修正与前提保留的重述。

Method: 提出离散-修正耦合诊断：使用一致性指数(CI)验证输出离散度变化，通过逐轮立场标注测量认知修正；结合元预测清晰系统(MPCS)在过度收敛时插入再差异化协议(RDP)。

Result: 在gpt-4o-mini上条件性异议提升17.7个点，静态角色多样性损害8.1个点；在gemini-2.5-flash上无显著增益(26.1% vs 27.1%, p=.84)，且效果与GPT显著不同；Gemini的94%响应为重新表述而非让步。

Conclusion: 应同时报告立场转移和前提保留率，而不能仅依赖准确率；不同模型对同一干预的认知修正机制可能完全不同。

中文摘要：集体智慧研究将分歧视为认知多样性的证据：如果个体表达不同的观点，群体应保留修正的能力。在LLM集体中，这一代理指标可能失效：智能体可以生成看似多样的论证，同时保留相同的结论。我们将“离散-修正耦合”操作化：一种干预在嵌入空间中确实增加了集体输出的离散度，但伴随着其认知立场真正修正的程度，而非保留前提的重新表述。该诊断是黑盒的：它仅基于生成的文本，不对生成模型的内部表征作任何假设。两个通道被独立测量：输出通道，即一致性指数（CI），验证干预是否改变了输出离散度；认知通道，即逐轮立场标注，衡量集体是否进行了修正。我们提出CI与元预测清晰系统（MPCS）结合，当输出过度收敛时插入再差异化协议（RDP），作为一种估计该耦合机制的可复用方法。我们评估了来自两种配置的五智能体集体（gpt-4o-mini 和 gemini-2.5-flash；每种条件310个配对情节）。在gpt-4o-mini上，条件性异议将错误前提恢复率提高+17.7个百分点（p<1e-6），而静态角色多样性则损害恢复（-8.1，p=.007）。在gemini-2.5-flash上，同样的干预在相当预算下没有带来收益（26.1% vs 27.1%，p=.84），尽管验证了离散度下降；两种处理效应彼此显著不同（z=3.79，p<.001）。机制标记显示，Gemini通过框架内异议保留错误前提：94%的标记后RDP响应是重新表述而非让步（GPT为24%）。我们建议在报告准确性的同时，报告每次干预的立场转移和前提保留率。

Original abstract: Collective intelligence research treats disagreement as evidence of epistemic diversity: if agents express different views, the group should retain capacity to revise. In LLM collectives this proxy can break: agents can produce diverse-looking arguments while preserving the same conclusion. We operationalize dispersion-revision coupling: the degree to which an intervention that verifiably increases the dispersion of a collective's outputs in embedding space is accompanied by genuine revision of its epistemic stance rather than premise-preserving reformulation. The diagnostic is black-box: it operates on generated text alone and makes no claims about the internal representations of the generating models. Two channels are measured independently: an output channel, the Coherence Index (CI), verifies that the intervention changed output dispersion; an epistemic channel, per-turn stance annotation, measures whether the collective revised. We propose CI with the Meta-Predictive Clarity System (MPCS), which inserts a Re-Differentiation Protocol (RDP) when outputs over-converge, as a reusable method for estimating this coupling regime. We evaluate five-agent collectives from two configurations (gpt-4o-mini and gemini-2.5-flash; 310 paired episodes per condition). On gpt-4o-mini, conditional dissent improves false-premise recovery by +17.7 points (p<1e-6) while static persona diversity harms recovery (-8.1, p=.007). On gemini-2.5-flash, the same intervention at a comparable budget yields no gain (26.1% vs 27.1%, p=.84) despite a verified dispersion drop; the two treatment effects differ from each other (z=3.79, p<.001). Mechanism tagging shows Gemini preserves the false premise via intra-framework dissent: 94% of tagged post-RDP responses reformulate rather than concede (vs 24% on GPT). We recommend reporting per-intervention stance shift and premise-preservation rate alongside accuracy.

</details>


### [44] [AgenticECO: An Agentic Framework for ECO on 3D Integrated Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03738)
*Shuo Ren,Yaohui Han,Libo Shen,Zhiqiang Jia,Rongliang Fu,Bei Yu,Tsung-Yi Ho*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 证据门控的工具使用agent工作流用于3D-IC ECO，含验证门和审计工件，对工程类自动化agent及SemOpt有直接借鉴。

TL;DR: 提出一种基于证据门控的智能体工作流和最小干扰布线层，显著提升3D-IC ECO修复成功率与可归因性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D-IC中键合级缺陷无2D对应，后布线ECO手工且依赖专家；现有编辑后完全重布导致修复与布线器变动纠缠，无法归因。

Method: 设计AgenticECO（证据门控工具使用智能体）和EcoRoute（最小干扰ECO布线层），驱动未修改固定布线器，使修复可归因；包含对照与预注册研究。

Result: 9个自然缺陷案例中清除7个（对比全重布和常规修复均为2个），平均干扰0.66%，零时钟网触碰；跨骨干重跑清除全部9个；盲诊断零错误编辑。

Conclusion: 该流程通过布线/时序/DRC/结构等价检查，提供可审计、可归因的3D-IC ECO框架，开源代码和工件支持复现。

中文摘要：随着摩尔定律放缓，业界正转向三维集成；然而在合并的3D-IC流程中，布线后的设计会暴露出没有2D对应物的键合级缺陷，而后布线工程变更单（ECO）仍然是手工、依赖专家的工作。更糟糕的是，标准的“编辑后完全重新布线”做法将修复与布线器变动纠缠在一起，因此签核数字无法归因于促使该数字的编辑。我们提出了AgenticECO，一种在开源TaiWei流程上进行3D-IC ECO的基于证据门控的工具使用智能体工作流，并搭配EcoRoute，一种最小干扰的ECO布线层，它驱动未修改的固定布线器，使修复可归因于其编辑。在相同预算下的九个匹配自然缺陷案例中，AgenticECO清除了七个，而完全重新布线和常规修复均为两个，清除案例的平均干扰为0.66%，且未触及时钟网络；在同一密封合同下的跨骨干重跑清除了全部九个。对照研究表明，在保留条件下修复动作是必要的，占用率感知选择带来合法落地而非修复成功，并且在收紧时钟下最小干扰将接受与拒绝翻转。三项预注册视觉研究将像素仪器的边缘定位到有争议的落地站点，一项预注册盲诊断精确恢复了每个保留的注入缺陷，是唯一一个零错误编辑的组。每个接受的结果都通过布线、重新提取、最大/最小时序、DRC和结构等价门。代码、环境和每集审计工件作为补充材料发布。

Original abstract: As Moore's law slows, the industry is turning to three-dimensional integration; yet in merged 3D-IC flows, routed designs expose bond-level defects with no 2D analogue, and post-route engineering change orders (ECO) remain manual, expertise-bound work. Worse, the standard edit-then-fully-reroute practice entangles a repair with router churn, so a signoff number cannot be attributed to the edit that motivated it. We present AgenticECO, an evidence-gated tool-using agent workflow for 3D-IC ECO on the open-source TaiWei flow, paired with EcoRoute, a minimal-disturbance ECO-routing layer that drives the unmodified pinned router so a repair is attributable to its edit. Across nine matched natural defect cases under identical budgets, AgenticECO clears seven versus two for both full reroute and stock repair, at 0.66\% mean disturbance over cleared cases and zero clock nets touched, and a cross-backbone rerun under the same sealed contract clears all nine. Controlled studies show that the repair moves are necessary under preservation, that occupancy-aware choice buys legal landings rather than repair success, and that under tightened clocks minimal disturbance flips accept versus reject. Three preregistered visual studies localize the pixel instrument's edge to contested landing sites, and a preregistered blind diagnostic exactly restores every held-out injected defect, the only arm with zero wrong edits. Every accepted result passes routing, fresh extraction, max/min timing, DRC, and structural-equivalence gates. Code, environment, and per-episode audit artifacts are released as supplementary material.

</details>


### [45] [Agents Catching Agents: Shortcut Cascades and Benchmark Gaming in Clinical Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03744)
*Sebastián Andrés Cajas Ordóñez,Agastya Munnangi,Aldo Marzullo,Felipe Ocampo Osorio,Quang Bui,Mohammad Shahin,Armaan Grewal,Emmanuel Paul Kwesiga,Anqi Peter Li,Josephine Nanyonjo,Aaditya Panchal,Arshnoor Bhutani,Nikhil Jaiswal,Milit S. Patel,Maximin Lange,Leo Anthony Celi*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 研究临床多agent委员会被捷径和基准博弈欺骗的现象，提出监督与独立复核机制，对agent可靠性和评估有通用启发。

TL;DR: 研究发现，语言模型委员会易受社会合理性捷径的影响，如同伴压力或系统标志，而非纯粹的视觉线索；只有独立于自我报告的裁判代理才能有效检测这种采纳行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策支持系统正采用语言模型代理委员会，但需要验证它们是否会被基准测试中的捷径（如社会线索）所欺骗，而这些线索在临床实践中会被忽略。

Method: 在七个队列（涵盖文本、影像和表格数据）上测试Gemini委员会对孤立线索的抵抗性，并通过引入同伴错误断言、虚假系统标志、监督代理（门控、同源裁判、独立裁判）以及改变视觉显著性和同伴数量来评估社会传染效应。

Result: 委员会抵抗孤立线索（翻转5-16%），但同伴错误断言导致38%的采纳，虚假标志同样有效；同源裁判在文本上有效（精确率100%，召回率93%）但在影像上失效，独立裁判在影像上表现良好（精确率77-88%，假阳性率13-21%）；改变视觉显著性无效，第二个同伴提高50%传染；隐藏评分标准的提及极少。

Conclusion: 委员会博弈的关键在于社会合理性，而非感知显著性；只有独立于自我报告的裁判代理才能检测这种采纳，强调了在临床决策支持中需要独立的监督机制。

中文摘要：临床决策支持正朝着在共享工作空间中进行审议的语言模型代理委员会的方向发展。我们研究这样的委员会是否会被捷径所欺骗，即基准测试奖励但临床医生会忽略的线索。在跨越六个公共数据集的七个队列中，涵盖文本（MedQA-USMLE、MedMCQA、MIMIC-CXR报告）、影像（NIH ChestX-ray14、MIMIC-CXR-JPG、CheXpert）和表格型ICU记录（SUPPORT2），Gemini委员会在孤立情况下抵抗这些线索（翻转5-16%），然而一种社会性合理的捷径会传播：当两个同伴断言同一个错误答案时，受测的持异议者会在38%的案例中采纳它，一个虚假的“预筛查”系统标志也是如此，在两个能力层级上均如此。在三个监督代理中，一个门控无法区分采纳与诚实同意（假阳性率100%）；一个同源裁判仅阅读文本记录即可在文本上标记采纳（精确率100%，召回率93%），但在影像上却与门控表现趋同；一个私下重新查询持异议者的裁判可迁移到影像（精确率77-88%，假阳性率13-21%）。将线索的视觉显著性提高三倍并不能改变传染性，而第二个同伴的声音使其提高一半。对隐藏评分标准的博弈几乎无声：只有1/10的文本漂移者和1/134的影像漂移者提到了他们趋向的评分标准。委员会被博弈的是社会合理性，只有独立于自我报告的裁判才能捕捉到它。代码：https://github.com/criticaldata/benchmaxxing

Original abstract: Clinical decision support is moving toward committees of language-model agents deliberating on a shared workspace. We ask whether such committees can be gamed by shortcuts, cues a benchmark rewards but a clinician would ignore. Across seven cohorts on six public datasets spanning text (MedQA-USMLE, MedMCQA, MIMIC-CXR reports), imaging (NIH ChestX-ray14, MIMIC-CXR-JPG, CheXpert) and tabular ICU records (SUPPORT2), Gemini committees resist these cues in isolation (flip 5-16%), yet a socially plausible shortcut spreads: when two peers assert the same wrong answer, the holdout under test adopts it in 38% of cases, as does a false "pre-screen" system flag, on both capability tiers. Of three oversight agents, a gate cannot separate adoption from honest agreement (false-positive rate 100%); a same-lineage judge reading only the transcript flags adoption on text (precision 100%, recall 93%) but collapses onto the gate in imaging; a referee that privately re-queries the holdout transfers to imaging (77-88% precision, 13-21% false-positive rate). Tripling a cue's visual salience does not move contagion, whereas a second peer voice raises it by half again. Gaming a hidden rubric is near-silent: only 1/10 text and 1/134 imaging drifters name the rubric they moved toward. What games a committee is social plausibility, and only a referee independent of self-report catches it. Code: https://github.com/criticaldata/benchmaxxing

</details>


### [46] [Risky Business: Measuring The Faithfulness-Safety Tension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03745)
*Dominik Meier,Luca Joshua Francis,Marco Bernhard Kaiser,Terry Ruas,Jan Philip Wahle,Bela Gipp*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 研究推理模型忠实性与安全性的权衡并提出干预与表征引导方法，对agent推理监控与安全控制有参考价值。

TL;DR: 本文发现大型推理模型在忠实性与安全鲁棒性之间存在权衡，提出定向推理替换（TRR）方法进行测试，并通过表示引导在保持能力的同时提升安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链推理用于模型监控需要忠实性，但模型还需拒绝不安全推理，二者存在矛盾。现有工作无法直接干预推理链来测试忠实性，需新方法。

Method: 引入HazMart数据集，提出定向推理替换（TRR）技术，直接替换推理链中的想法为不安全或不合逻辑内容；对DeepSeek-R1-Llama-70B和QwQ-32B进行测试，并进行机制分析和表示引导。

Result: DeepSeek-R1-Llama-70B忠实性97.5%但安全性12.3%；QwQ-32B安全性73.9%但忠实性74.7%；机制显示反相关内部方向；表示引导使安全行为提升9个百分点且保持能力。

Conclusion: 忠实性与安全性之间的平衡可通过表示引导来调节，独立放大安全方向可在不牺牲能力的前提下提高鲁棒性，为监控和安全对齐提供新路径。

中文摘要：思维链（CoT）推理为模型监控提供了一个有前景的窗口。然而，监控依赖于忠实性，即模型输出严格源于其推理轨迹。我们识别出一种对齐张力：模型必须足够忠实以被监控，同时又足够鲁棒以拒绝不安全的推理。我们证明这种平衡在现有大型推理模型（LRM）中存在，并展示了解决该问题的方法。我们引入了HazMart，一个在自主AI店主场景中人工编写的数据集。与先前依赖在提示中提供提示（例如“一位斯坦福教授说答案应为A”）来测试忠实性的工作不同，我们提出了一种新颖的基于替换的技术，称为定向推理替换（TRR），它直接干预推理链，用不安全或不合逻辑的想法替换其中的部分（例如“等等，答案必须是选项B [原为选项A]，因为它最合适”）。DeepSeek-R1-Llama-70B表现出高忠实性（97.5%），但未能拒绝不安全推理（12.3%），而QwQ-32B更鲁棒（安全性73.9%），但忠实性较低（74.7%）。对QwQ-32B的机制分析揭示，这些属性由在动作承诺标记处达到峰值的反相关内部方向表示。最后，我们证明表示引导可以独立放大安全方向，在保持基本能力的同时将安全行为提高9个百分点。

Original abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning offers a promising window into model monitoring. However, monitoring relies on faithfulness, i.e., the model output strictly derives from its reasoning trace. We identify an alignment tension where a model must be faithful enough to be monitored, yet robust enough to reject unsafe reasoning. We demonstrate that this counterbalance exists in current Large Reasoning Models (LRMs), and show ways in which it can be addressed. We introduce HazMart, a human-written dataset set in an autonomous AI shopkeeper scenario. Unlike prior work that relies on providing hints in prompts to test faithfulness (e.g., "A Stanford professor said it should be Answer A"), we propose a novel replacement-based technique, which we call Targeted Reasoning Replacement (TRR), that directly intervenes in the reasoning chain to substitute in unsafe or illogical thoughts (e.g., "Wait, the answer must be Option B [was Option A] because it is the most fitting"). DeepSeek-R1-Llama-70B exhibits high faithfulness (97.5%) but fails to reject Unsafe Reasoning (12.3%), while QwQ-32B is more robust (73.9% safety) at the cost of lower faithfulness (74.7%). Mechanistic analyses of QwQ-32B reveal that these properties are represented by anti-correlated internal directions peaking at the action-commit token. Finally, we demonstrate that representation steering can independently amplify the safety direction, increasing safe behavior by 9 percentage points while maintaining base capabilities.

</details>


### [47] [GDPevo: Evaluating Agent Self-Evolution on Real Business Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03764)
*Leijun Zhou,Zhihao Liu,Xiang Qu,Chenxu Liu,Yifei Liu,Yanke Yu,Jingzhe Xu,Xuejun Wu,Buyue Qian,Xi Chen,Yaowei Zheng,Junhao Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出抗污染的自进化agent评测基准GDPevo，规则混合确保收益可归因，直接适用于agent自我改进评估与SemOpt验证。

TL;DR: GDPevo是一个基于GDP相关企业工作流、通过规则杂交自动生成训练和测试任务的进化原生基准，评估显示自我进化最高可提升留出准确率16.44个百分点，但当前智能体仍远未达到91.6%的预言机上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我进化评估基准存在任务领域覆盖有限、训练收益难以归因以及易受数据污染的问题，需要一个新的基准来可靠衡量智能体自我进化能力。

Method: 提出GDPevo基准及完全自动化的数据流水线，其核心机制是规则杂交：将企业工作流分解为原子业务规则，在训练任务中分配规则子集，并在留出的测试任务中重新组合，以明确归因测试时的性能提升。

Result: 在四种监督类型下评估四个智能体，自我进化一致提升留出准确率，最高提升16.44个百分点；最佳进化智能体准确率为远低于91.6%的完全信息预言机上限。

Conclusion: 当前智能体的自我进化能力尚未完全实现，GDPevo为评估和推动该能力提供了实用的基准与自动化数据管道，并已公开代码、数据和评估结果。

中文摘要：智能体自我进化利用先前经验更新智能体的持久状态，并复用它以更有效地解决相关任务。评估自我进化是困难的：现有基准对具有经济价值的任务领域覆盖有限，并不总是设计训练和测试任务使得测试时的收益可归因于训练经验，并且仍然容易受到数据污染的影响。我们提出了GDPevo，一个基于GDP相关企业工作流的、原生面向进化的基准，以及生成该基准的完全自动化数据流水线。其核心机制——规则杂交——将每个企业工作流分解为原子业务规则，将这些规则的子集分布到训练任务中，并在留出的测试任务中重新组合它们，从而使测试时的收益可归因。GDPevo涵盖CRM、ERP、金融、医疗、法律和数据中心工作流。其V1版本包含12组共120个任务，每组有五个训练任务和五个留出的测试任务。完全自动化使得流水线能够在两天内将套件扩展到24组240个任务（V2），为污染问题提供了实用的应对方案。使用GDPevo，我们在四种监督类型下评估了四个智能体，每个智能体由一个框架和一个模型组成。自我进化持续将留出准确率提升了最多16.44个百分点。但最好的进化智能体仍远低于完全信息预言机上限91.6%，表明当前智能体的自我进化能力远未完全实现。我们公开发布了流水线、基准和完整评估结果，网址为 https://github.com/Prism-Shadow/GDPevo。

Original abstract: Agent self-evolution updates an agent's persistent state from prior experience and reuses it to solve related tasks more effectively. Evaluating self-evolution is difficult: existing benchmarks provide limited coverage of economically valuable task domains, do not always design training and test tasks such that test-time gains can be attributed to training experience, and remain vulnerable to data contamination. We present GDPevo, an evolution-native benchmark grounded in GDP-related enterprise workflows, together with the fully automated data pipeline that generates it. Its core mechanism, rule hybridization, decomposes each enterprise workflow into atomic business rules, distributes subsets of these rules across training tasks, and recombines them in held-out test tasks so that test-time gains are attributable. GDPevo spans CRM, ERP, finance, healthcare, legal, and data-centric workflows. Its V1 release contains 120 tasks in 12 groups, with five training and five held-out test tasks per group. Full automation enables the pipeline to expand the suite to 240 tasks in 24 groups (V2) within two days, providing a practical response to contamination. Using GDPevo, we evaluate four agents, each comprising a harness and a model, under four supervision types. Self-evolution consistently improves held-out accuracy by up to 16.44 percentage points. But the best evolved agents remain far below the fully informed oracle ceiling of 91.6%, indicating that the self-evolution ability of current agents remains far from fully realized. We publicly release the pipeline, benchmark, and full evaluation results at https://github.com/Prism-Shadow/GDPevo.

</details>


### [48] [LatentGuard: Efficient and Inspectable Latent Reasoning for LLM Safeguards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03838)
*Zhinan Liu,Jie Li,Mingyu Kang,Jiayi Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 用连续潜在推理做可检查的LLM安全护栏，减少推理成本并保留审计能力，对agent安全控制与效率设计有潜在启发。

TL;DR: LatentGuard将潜在推理引入防护模型，在保持可检查性的同时显著降低推理成本并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于推理的防护模型虽有效但显式推理成本高；潜在推理方法未被用于安全审核且缺乏可检查性。

Method: 提出LatentGuard框架，通过分阶段课程将文本推理压缩为紧凑潜在状态，直接预测安全判定，并用辅助解码器按需生成审计工件。

Result: LatentGuard-8B相比GuardReasoner-8B将F1从83.95提升至84.91，推理令牌从268.56降至1.60，审计效用分数85.75。

Conclusion: LatentGuard为LLM安全防护提供了一种高效且可检查的路径，兼顾性能与部署可行性。

中文摘要：基于推理的防护模型改进了LLM的安全保障，但为每次交互解码显式推理过程使其部署成本高昂。尽管潜在推理方法通过将推理移入连续状态来减少令牌生成，但它们在安全审核方面仍未得到充分探索，且缺乏用于部署的检查接口。在本文中，我们提出LatentGuard，一种高效且可检查的安全防护框架，将连续潜在推理引入防护模型。LatentGuard使用分阶段课程逐步将任务对齐的文本推理压缩为紧凑的潜在状态，从而可以直接从连续表示预测安全判定。为保持可检查性，一个独立的辅助解码器按需生成紧凑的审计工件，使推理生成脱离标准推理路径。实验表明，LatentGuard-8B在加权平均F1上从83.95提升到84.91（相较于GuardReasoner-8B），同时将关键路径推理成本从268.56个生成的推理令牌减少到1.60个潜在推理令牌。其审计解码器达到85.75的审计效用分数，展示了迈向可部署LLM安全防护的高效且可检查的路径。

Original abstract: Reasoning-based guard models improve LLM safeguards, but decoding explicit rationales for every interaction makes them costly to deploy. Although latent-reasoning methods reduce token generation by moving reasoning into continuous states, they remain underexplored for safety moderation and lack an inspection interface for deployment. In this paper, we propose LatentGuard, an efficient and inspectable safeguard framework that brings continuous latent reasoning to guard models. LatentGuard uses a staged curriculum to progressively compress task-aligned textual rationales into compact latent states, enabling safety verdicts to be predicted directly from continuous representations. To preserve inspectability, an isolated auxiliary decoder generates compact audit artifacts on demand, keeping rationale generation off the standard inference path. Experiments show that LatentGuard-8B improves mean weighted F1 from 83.95 to 84.91 over GuardReasoner-8B, while reducing critical-path reasoning cost from 268.56 generated rationale tokens to 1.60 latent reasoning tokens. Its audit decoder achieves an audit utility score of 85.75, demonstrating an efficient and inspectable path toward deployable LLM safeguards.

</details>


### [49] [MAFIA: Query-Only Memory Attacks via Probing and Factual Injection against Audited LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03844)
*Jiaming Chen,Yisen Gao,Yanping Li,Zifan Liu,Yumeng Zhang,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 针对记忆增强LLM agent的投毒攻击与审计，对agent安全控制有直接启发，可迁移至coding agent记忆模块防护。

TL;DR: 提出MAFIA，一种针对内存增强LLM代理的仅查询内存投毒攻击框架，通过内存探测和事实注入绕过审计，在大型良性内存池和主动审计下仍能达到90.7%攻击成功率，并将审计检测率从83.3%降至7.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内存投毒攻击在大型良性内存池和主动输入审计下失效，无法同时应对高检索竞争性和严格语义检查的挑战，因此需要更强大的攻击框架来揭示漏洞。

Method: MAFIA框架包含两个关键组件：放置策略（通过内存探测、预算分配和调度确保注入记录被检索到）和载荷设计（使用紧凑事实伪装绕过审计，同时保持高语义相似性和恶意效果）。

Result: 实验表明MAFIA实现高达90.7%的攻击成功率，并将审计检测率从峰值83.3%抑制到最多7.4%，显著暴露了智能体内存系统的脆弱性。

Conclusion: MAFIA证明现有内存防御措施不足，亟需更鲁棒的安全机制；该工作揭示了内存投毒攻击在现实条件下依然可行且危险，为后续防御研究提供了方向。

中文摘要：内存增强的LLM代理依赖丰富的上下文进行长时推理和行动，然而其内存模块暴露了恶意记录的持续攻击面，使得内存投毒威胁的研究变得至关重要。然而，现有的仅查询攻击在两种现实且普遍的场景中往往难以保持有效性：大规模良性内存池和主动输入审计。因此，当前方法在面对高检索竞争性和严格语义检查的双重挑战时表现不足。为克服这些限制，我们提出了MAFIA，一种通过探测和事实注入针对审计的仅查询内存攻击框架，适用于这一扩展的威胁模型。具体而言，MAFIA引入了：（1）一种通过内存探测、预算分配和调度确保检索竞争性注入的放置策略；（2）一种利用紧凑事实伪装绕过审计的载荷设计，在保持高语义相似性的同时保留恶意效果。大量评估表明，MAFIA实现了高达90.7%的攻击成功率，同时将审计检测从峰值83.3%抑制至最多7.4%，暴露了智能体内存系统中的关键漏洞。代码将在https://github.com/JiamingChen1234/MAFIA公开。

Original abstract: Memory-augmented LLM agents rely on rich context for long-horizon reasoning and acting, yet their memory modules expose a persistent attack surface for malicious records, making the study of memory poisoning threats imperative. However, existing query-only attacks often fail to remain effective in two realistic and prevalent settings: large-scale benign memory pools and active input auditing. Consequently, current approaches fall short when facing the dual challenges of high retrieval competitiveness and rigorous semantic checks. To overcome these limitations, we propose MAFIA, a query-only Memory Attack framework via probing and Factual Injection against Audit, tailored to this extended threat model. Specifically, MAFIA introduces: (1) a placement strategy that ensures retrieval-competitive injection via memory probing, budget allocation, and scheduling; and (2) a payload design that bypasses audits using compact factual cloaks, preserving malicious effects while maintaining high semantic similarity. Extensive evaluations reveal that MAFIA achieves up to a 90.7% attack success rate while suppressing audit detection from a peak of 83.3% to at most 7.4%, exposing critical vulnerabilities across agentic memory systems. Code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/JiamingChen1234/MAFIA.

</details>


### [50] [ContinualSkillBench: Can LLM Agents Truly Evolve Their Capabilities?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03874)
*Tianyi Guan,Yiding Wang,Haotong Yang,Siyuan Cao,Shirui Liu,Yi Hu,Jiaqi Li,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出持续技能学习基准，评估LLM agent技能累积与跨任务复用，对SemOpt经验沉淀和技能演化有直接参考价值。

TL;DR: 提出了ContinualSkillBench评估框架，发现上下文持续技能学习能提升性能但提升有限，显式技能仅在特定任务上有优势，且弱模型产生更碎片化的技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体框架虽配备技能库，但缺乏对技能进化和实际效果的系统评估，尤其是上下文持续技能学习机制的有效性未知。

Method: 设计动态评估框架ContinualSkillBench，覆盖5个领域、各100个按难度递增的互联子任务，比较顺序执行、显式技能维护等学习范式。

Result: 顺序执行普遍提升性能但差异大；上下文学习与显式技能维护平均相当；显式技能在可复用程序或精确输出任务上更有益；较弱模型产生更多碎片化技能。

Conclusion: 当前上下文技能进化能支持持续适应，但难以稳健地整合经验为可迁移技能，未来需改进技能巩固机制。

中文摘要：现代智能体框架为大型语言模型配备外部技能库以解决复杂任务。然而，目前尚不清楚这些系统能否有效地进化其技能，以及所产生的技能是否真正提升任务解决能力。为弥补这一空白，我们引入了ContinualSkillBench，一个用于上下文持续技能学习的动态评估框架。它涵盖五个代表性领域，每个领域包含100个相互关联的子任务，按难度递增排序，并提供跨任务技能复用的机会。实验表明，顺序执行通常能提升性能，但提升幅度在不同模型和领域间差异显著。此外，上下文学习在平均表现上与显式技能维护相当，这表明大部分提升源于对先前上下文和反馈的适应，而非单纯的可复用技能抽象。尽管如此，显式技能在需要可复用程序或精确输出的任务上仍具有选择性优势。我们进一步发现，能力较弱的模型倾向于积累更大、更碎片化的任务特定技能集合。这些发现表明，当前的上下文技能进化机制能够支持持续适应，但仍难以稳定地将经验整合为稳健且可迁移的技能。

Original abstract: Modern agent frameworks equip large language models with external skill libraries to solve complex tasks. However, it remains unclear whether these systems can effectively evolve their skills and whether the resulting skills improve task-solving capabilities. To bridge this gap, we introduce ContinualSkillBench, a dynamic evaluation framework for in-context continual skill learning. It covers five representative domains, each containing 100 interconnected subtasks ordered by increasing difficulty and opportunities for cross-task skill reuse. Our experiments show that sequential execution generally improves performance, but the gains vary substantially across models and domains. Moreover, in-context learning performs comparably to explicit skill maintenance on average, suggesting that much of the improvement arises from adaptation to prior context and feedback rather than reusable skill abstraction alone. Explicit skills nevertheless provide selective benefits for tasks requiring reusable procedures or precise outputs. We further find that less capable models tend to accumulate larger, more fragmented collections of task-specific skills. These findings show that current in-context skill evolution mechanisms can support continual adaptation, but still struggle to consistently consolidate experience into robust and transferable skills.

</details>


### [51] [Socially Grounded Agentic AI: Coordinating Plural Perspectives through Social Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03910)
*Matt Ratto,Abhishek Moturu,Daniel Silver*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 从社会理论探讨多agent系统的多元对齐与协调，为agent协作设计和评估提供概念框架。

TL;DR: 本文提出利用社会理论将多元对齐重新定义为社会基础协调问题，而非简单的输出多样化，并给出具体设计启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐方法通常假设单一价值集，但现实社会存在多种合法视角。当前多元对齐方法缺乏对价值如何在社会中组织、争论和协调的理论解释。

Method: 借鉴社会学传统，将视角理解为由角色结构化、通过互动塑造、并在权力和专业知识场域中分布；将这些概念转化为AI系统设计启示，包括角色表示、结构化协调、情境敏感评价，以及对代理系统的角色激活、审议轨迹、聚合规则和反馈循环进行对齐。

Result: 重新定位多元对齐为社会基础协调问题，并勾勒出支持多视角结构化、可问责参与的设计空间。

Conclusion: 社会理论为多元对齐提供关键基础，未来需在真实环境中实施和实证评估这些方法。

中文摘要：随着人工智能系统被部署在日益多样化的社会背景中，对齐不能再被框定为对单一、统一价值集的优化。相反，系统必须能够识别、代表并回应多种合法的视角。这导致了对多元对齐（pluralistic alignment）的兴趣日益增长，其旨在超越一刀切的适当行为模型。然而，当前的方法通常缺乏对价值在社会中如何被组织、争论和协调的清晰说明。在本文中，我们主张社会理论为解决这些挑战提供了必要的概念和设计资源。借鉴社会学中的既有传统，我们展示了视角如何被理解为由角色结构化、通过互动塑造，并在权力和专业知识的场域中分布。我们将这些见解转化为对AI系统设计的具体启示，包括基于角色的表示、视角之间的结构化协调，以及情境敏感的评价。对于代理系统，这不仅需要对最终输出进行对齐，还需要对产生这些输出的角色激活、审议轨迹、聚合规则和反馈循环进行对齐。我们的贡献是将多元对齐重新定位为一种社会基础协调问题，而非输出多样化问题。我们勾勒了一个设计空间，使系统能够以结构化和可问责的方式参与多种视角，并确定了未来在现实环境中实施和实证评估这些方法的工作方向。

Original abstract: As AI systems are deployed across increasingly diverse social contexts, alignment can no longer be framed as the optimization of a single, unified set of values. Instead, systems must be able to recognize, represent, and respond to multiple legitimate perspectives. This has led to growing interest in pluralistic alignment, which seeks to move beyond one-size-fits-all models of appropriate behaviour. However, current approaches often lack a clear account of how values are socially organized, contested, and coordinated in practice. In this paper, we argue that social theory provides essential conceptual and design resources for addressing these challenges. Drawing on established traditions in sociology, we show how perspectives can be understood as structured by roles, shaped through interaction, and distributed across fields of power and expertise. We translate these insights into concrete implications for AI system design, including role-based representations, structured coordination among perspectives, and context-sensitive evaluation. For agentic systems, this requires aligning not only final outputs, but also the role activations, deliberative traces, aggregation rules, and feedback loops through which those outputs are produced. Our contribution is to reposition pluralistic alignment as a problem of socially grounded coordination rather than output diversification. We outline a design space for systems that engage multiple perspectives in structured and accountable ways, and we identify directions for future work to implement and empirically evaluate these approaches in real-world settings.

</details>


### [52] [A game theory for foundation models shows new paths to rational cooperation through similarity inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03958)
*Alexander Meulemans,Maciej Wołczyk,Marissa A. Weis,Rajai Nasser,Roberta Rocca,Seijin Kobayashi,Guillaume Lajoie,Angelika Steger,Blake Richards,Marcus Hutter,James Manyika,Rif A. Saurous,João Sacramento,Blaise Agüera y Arcas*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出嵌入贝叶斯agent理论解释LLM多agent协作，对多agent协作机制与博弈论建模有可迁移价值。

TL;DR: 基础模型智能体在经典社会困境中通过最优规划会稳定收敛于合作，违背经典博弈论预测。我们提出嵌入式贝叶斯智能体和嵌入式均衡，解释其通过相似性推断将自身决策作为证据的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典博弈论假设解耦能动性，但现代AI智能体在规划中联合预测自身行动与外部环境，导致其集体行为与经典预测相悖。理解这种新行为模式对于确保AI安全与合作至关重要。

Method: 引入嵌入式贝叶斯智能体理论模型，将智能体视为嵌入宇宙中并对其自身决策保持不确定性；通过推断其他智能体行为相似性，将自身规划中的决策视为证据；并形式化嵌入式均衡作为新的解概念。

Result: 在风格化社会困境中，基础模型智能体进行最优规划时持续收敛于稳定合作，直接矛盾于经典博弈论的相互背叛预测；嵌入式均衡可解释该现象。

Conclusion: 从解耦能动性转向嵌入式能动性，为现代AI智能体的社会行为提供了新的基础博弈论框架，有助于预测和引导其集体行为，促进安全与合作。

中文摘要：随着由基础模型驱动的自主智能体日益融入社会和经济系统，理解其集体行为背后的原理对于确保安全与合作至关重要。经典博弈论作为模拟理性互动的主流框架，建立在“解耦能动性”假设之上，即智能体将自己的决策视为独立于环境和其他行动者。然而，现代人工智能智能体会联合预测自身未来行动与外部观察。在此，我们报告了一个引人注目的发现：当在风格化的社会困境中互动时，进行最优规划的基础模型智能体始终收敛于稳定合作，这与经典博弈论中相互背叛的预测直接矛盾。为理解这一现象，我们引入了“嵌入式贝叶斯智能体”，一个针对基础模型智能体的理论模型。通过从解耦能动性转向嵌入式能动性，这些智能体将自身建模为其所处宇宙的一部分，并对其自身决策算法保持认知不确定性。我们表明，通过推断其他智能体是否在行为上相似，嵌入式智能体将规划过程中的自身深思熟虑视为证据：合作的决定预测了相似伙伴的类似决定。我们通过“嵌入式均衡”这一新颖解概念形式化了这种相似性推断机制，该概念取代了纳什均衡，为现代人工智能智能体的社会行为提供了一个基础性博弈论框架。

Original abstract: As autonomous agents powered by foundation models are increasingly integrated into social and economic systems, understanding the principles governing their collective behavior is essential for ensuring safety and cooperation. Classical game theory, the dominant framework for modeling rational interaction, is built upon the assumption of `decoupled agency,' where agents treat their own decision-making as independent of the environment and other actors. Modern AI agents, however, jointly predict their own future actions alongside external observations. Here, we report a striking finding: when interacting in stylized social dilemmas, foundation model agents engaging in optimal planning consistently converge to stable cooperation, directly contradicting classical game-theoretic predictions of mutual defection. To understand this phenomenon, we introduce the `embedded Bayesian agent,' a theoretical model for foundation model agents. By shifting from decoupled to embedded agency, these agents model themselves as part of the universe they inhabit, maintaining epistemic uncertainty about their own decision-making algorithms. We show that by inferring whether others are behaviorally similar, an embedded agent treats its own deliberation during planning as evidence: a decision to cooperate predicts a similar decision by a similar partner. We formalize this mechanism of similarity inference through the `embedded equilibrium,' a novel solution concept replacing the Nash equilibrium to provide a foundational game theory for the social behavior of modern AI agents.

</details>


### [53] [ReflectRL: Learning from Golden Negative Trajectories via Reflective-to-Direct Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03972)
*Jinhe Bi,Chennan Zhou,Zengjie Jin,Aniri,Shuo Lu,Wenke Huang,Hu Cao,Xun Xiao,Zhihong Zhu,Volker Tresp,Fei Shen,Yunpu Ma,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 从失败轨迹中反思并迁移到直接推理的训练框架，可迁移至coding agent的训练与自我改进。

TL;DR: ReflectRL利用专家模型失败的轨迹作为反思信号，通过两阶段方法提升大型语言模型的推理能力，在多个基准上一致改进且开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当专家模型在难题上失败时，现有轨迹引导策略训练方法会丢弃失败轨迹，失去监督信号；但这些失败轨迹仍包含推理线索，反思它们可能比直接解题更易获得优势。

Method: 提出ReflectRL框架，包含两个阶段：首先利用金色负轨迹引发模型的反思性推理，然后通过反思到直接策略转换，将反思行为迁移到直接推理中，实现无需额外推理开销的性能提升。

Result: 在9个基准、4种大语言模型骨干和4种基于策略的训练方法上，ReflectRL一致提升推理性能，且计算开销最小。

Conclusion: 金色负轨迹并非无用，将其作为反思对象而非丢弃，可以有效增强策略训练；ReflectRL提供了一种轻量、通用的方式来利用这些信号，为后训练中处理失败数据提供了新思路。

中文摘要：基于策略的训练（on-policy training）已成为提升大型语言模型推理能力的强大后训练范式，通常通过来自更强专家模型的金色轨迹（golden trajectories）来增强。然而，当专家在更难的问题上失败时，现有的轨迹引导方法会失去其主要监督来源，这些失败轨迹通常作为负样本被丢弃。我们认为，这些失败——我们称之为“金色负轨迹”（Golden Negative Trajectories）——在被视为需要反思的有缺陷轨迹而非模仿示范时，仍能提供有价值的推理信号。我们发现了一种“反思优势”（Reflection Advantage）：对于难题，反思一条有缺陷的轨迹可能比直接从零开始解决问题更容易且更有效。基于此，我们提出了ReflectRL，一个轻量级即插即用框架，在基于策略的训练期间从金色负轨迹中学习。ReflectRL首先利用这些轨迹引发反思性推理（Reflective Reasoning），然后应用反思到直接策略转换（Reflective-to-Direct Policy Transition），将所学推理行为迁移回直接推理（Direct Reasoning）。在9个基准、4个LLM骨干和4种基于策略的训练方法上的实验表明，ReflectRL以最小开销持续提升了推理性能。

Original abstract: On-policy training has emerged as a powerful post-training paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models, and is often enhanced by golden trajectories from stronger expert models. However, when the expert fails on harder problems, existing trajectory-guided methods lose their main source of supervision, and these failed trajectories are typically discarded as negative samples. We argue that such failures, which we call Golden Negative Trajectories, can still provide valuable reasoning signals when treated not as demonstrations to imitate, but as flawed trajectories to reflect upon. We identify a Reflection Advantage: for hard problems, reflecting on a flawed trajectory can be easier and more effective than solving the problem directly from scratch. Motivated by this, we propose ReflectRL, a lightweight plug-and-play framework that learns from Golden Negative Trajectories during on-policy training. ReflectRL first uses these trajectories to elicit Reflective Reasoning, then applies Reflective-to-Direct Policy Transition to transfer the acquired reasoning behavior back to Direct Reasoning. Experiments across 9 benchmarks, 4 LLM backbones, and 4 on-policy training methods show that ReflectRL consistently improves reasoning performance with minimal overhead.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [54] [An Empirical Analysis of the Glob Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02610)
*Phyllis Lim,Connor Adkins,Mark Marron*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 首次大规模实证研究 glob 生态系统，发现实现不一致、安全漏洞普遍，并提出标准化规范 GlobSpec。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Glob 模式被广泛使用但缺乏标准化，导致行为不一致、安全漏洞和可用性问题，亟需系统性量化和解决方案。

Method: 分析 1,966 个开源项目、1,355 个 GitHub issue、444 份 CVE 报告和 361 个 StackOverflow 帖子，映射六个生态系统的功能支持和实际使用情况。

Result: 发现各实现与开发者采用存在显著差距；一致性差影响可移植性和可靠性；安全漏洞是主导问题，占开发者讨论近四分之一。

Conclusion: 提出通过标准化改进 glob，引入正式规范 GlobSpec 以解决语义歧义并弥合表达力差距。

中文摘要：Glob 模式，一种用于结构化字符串匹配的领域特定语言，是现代软件开发中基础但研究不足的组成部分，嵌入从构建脚本、配置文件到 Web 服务器路由的一切中。然而，这种广泛使用建立在脆弱的基础之上。尽管它们在工具和编程语言中无处不在，但缺乏标准化导致了碎片化的生态系统，充斥着不一致的行为、安全漏洞和可用性陷阱。本文首次对 glob 生态系统进行实证研究，以量化这些挑战并绘制通往稳健解决方案的路径。通过对 1,966 个开源项目、1,355 个 GitHub issue、444 份 CVE 报告和 361 个 StackOverflow 帖子的分析，我们系统地绘制了六个常见生态系统中 glob 的功能支持和实际使用情况。我们的研究结果揭示了各种 glob 实现与开发者采用之间的鲜明分歧。此外，我们记录了一致性问题，这些问题阻碍了可移植性、可靠性，并产生安全缺陷。我们的分析表明，安全漏洞并非特例，而是一个主导问题，占所有开发者讨论的近四分之一。我们提出了一条通过标准化前进的道路，并为 glob 引入了一个正式规范 GlobSpec，旨在解决语义歧义并弥合当前实现与开发者需求之间的表达力差距。

Original abstract: Glob patterns, a domain-specific language for structured string matching, are a foundational yet understudied component of modern software development embedded in everything from build scripts and configuration files to web server routes. However, this widespread use rests on a fragile foundation. Despite their ubiquity across tools and programming languages, a lack of standardization has led to a fragmented ecosystem rife with inconsistent behaviors, security vulnerabilities, and usability pitfalls. This paper presents the first empirical study of the glob ecosystem to quantify these challenges and chart a path toward robust solutions.
  Through an analysis of 1,966 open source projects, 1,355 Github issues, 444 CVE reports, and 361 StackOverflow posts, we systematically map the feature support and real-world usage of globs across six common ecosystems that utilize globs. Our findings reveal a stark divide between various globbing implementations and developer adoption. In addition, we document inconsistencies that hinder portability, reliability, and create security flaws. Our analysis reveals that security vulnerabilities are not corner cases, but a dominant concern, comprising almost a quarter of all developer discussions. We propose a path forward through standardization and introduce a formal specification for globs, GlobSpec, designed to resolve semantic ambiguities and bridge the expressiveness gap between current implementations and developer requirements.

</details>


### [55] [Rethinking Self-Evolving Agent Skills: Feedback Dynamics over Multiple Rounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02636)
*Yuxuan Liu,Zhaochen Su,Yuhao Zhang,Jiahe Guo,Zhongwei Xie,Huihao Jing,Lingyun Xie,Qing Zong,Yauwai Yim,Zhixiong Zhang,Haoran Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 技能自进化在多个基准上表现为稀疏的、依赖反馈的验证过滤搜索，成功轨迹并非必需，失败轨迹更为关键，测试时计算难以完全替代持久进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化技能系统虽有前景，但不清楚进一步进化何时有帮助、成功与失败轨迹如何影响修订，以及额外测试时计算能否带来同等收益。

Method: 提出受控评估框架，覆盖五个基准和三个模型，固定执行器/优化器配置、修订过程、验证规则和轮次预算，仅改变反馈类型（正常/仅失败/仅成功），进行42次反馈运行；另包含八模型的SearchQA扩展和GPT-5.5测试时扩展对照。

Result: 进化稀疏（388个候选仅55个验证最优），14个设置中11个选择进化技能，其中9个提升测试性能；所有选择均包含失败轨迹；不同评估视角偏好不同反馈；测试时计算部分接近但无法完全替代持久进化。

Conclusion: 持久性技能自进化应被理解为稀疏、经过验证过滤的搜索，其收益依赖模型和基准，而非额外轮次带来的稳定改进。

中文摘要：自进化技能系统有望通过将执行反馈转化为持久的技能更新（而不改变底层模型）来改进智能体。然而，目前仍不清楚进一步的进化何时有帮助、成功与失败的轨迹如何影响修订，以及额外的测试时计算是否能带来同样的收益。为解决这些问题，我们提出了一个覆盖五个基准和三个模型的受控评估框架。我们的主要研究包含14个受支持的模型-基准设置中的42次反馈运行。在每个设置中，我们固定执行器和优化器配置、修订过程、验证规则和轮次预算，仅改变向优化器显示的反馈：成功与失败（正常）、仅失败或仅成功。进化是稀疏的：388个候选者中只有55个创造了字节级不同的验证最优值。基于验证的选择在14个设置中的11个中选择了进化技能，其中9个提升了发布测试性能。所有11个选择都来自包含失败轨迹的反馈条件，尽管正常与仅失败条件的相对排名在不同设置中有所变化。验证以及测试、鲁棒性和迁移方面的下游评估有时会偏好不同的反馈视角。覆盖八个模型的更广泛SearchQA分析显示了类似的稀疏、依赖反馈的动态。在GPT-5.5的测试时扩展对照中，oracle并行采样与进化的SearchQA技能相差0.43分，但在SpreadsheetBench上仍落后30.96分；顺序精炼无法恢复任何一种收益。总体而言，持久性技能自进化应被更好地理解为一种稀疏的、经过验证过滤的搜索，其收益依赖于模型和基准，而非通过额外轮次获得的稳定改进。实现可在https://github.com/HKUST-KnowComp/rethinkskill获取。

Original abstract: Self-evolving skill systems promise to improve agents by turning execution feedback into persistent skill updates without changing the underlying model. Yet it remains unclear when further evolution helps, how successful and failed trajectories shape revision, and whether extra test-time computation can recover the same gains. To address these questions, we present a controlled evaluation framework across five benchmarks and three models. Our primary study contains 42 feedback runs across 14 supported model-benchmark settings. Within each setting, we hold the executor and optimizer configuration, revision procedure, validation rule, and round budget fixed, while varying only the feedback shown to the optimizer: successes and failures (Normal), failures only, or successes only. Evolution is sparse: only 55 of 388 candidates establish byte-distinct validation bests. Validation-based selection chooses an evolved skill in 11 of 14 settings, nine of which improve released-test performance. All 11 selections come from feedback conditions that include failed trajectories, although the relative ranking of Normal and Fail-only varies across settings. Validation and downstream evaluations on test, robustness, and transfer sometimes favor different feedback views. A broader SearchQA analysis covering eight models shows similarly sparse, feedback-dependent dynamics. In the GPT-5.5 test-time-scaling controls, oracle Parallel Sampling comes within 0.43 points of the evolved SearchQA skill but remains 30.96 points behind on SpreadsheetBench; Sequential Refinement recovers neither gain. Overall, persistent skill self-evolution is better understood as sparse, validation-filtered search with model- and benchmark-dependent returns, rather than steady improvement from additional rounds. The implementation is available at https://github.com/HKUST-KnowComp/rethinkskill.

</details>


### [56] [Studying, Identifying, and Fixing Hidden Technical Debt in AI-Intensive Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02638)
*Beena*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本论文针对AI-CPS中的技术债务，提出表征、识别与修复方法，并计划开发自动化工具支持智能体AI管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI-CPS硬件、AI组件与传统模块混合，技术债务独特且更具挑战性，现有研究不足。

Method: 通过分析AI生态系统和AI-CPS仓库、采访开发人员表征TD，据此定义识别与缓解方法，并开发自动化工具。

Result: 尚未给出最终结果，计划验证自动化工具。

Conclusion: 旨在实现AI-CPS技术债务的系统化识别与修复，支持智能体AI监控治理。

中文摘要：人工智能（AI）组件日益渗透到多个软件系统中，包括网络物理系统（CPS）。AI-CPS被用于多个领域，包括自动驾驶汽车、工业、家庭自动化、机器人和医疗保健。由于由硬件、AI组件和传统模块组成，AI-CPS可能表现出技术债务（TD），这种债务是特殊的，并且可能比传统系统的债务更具挑战性。本论文旨在刻画AI-CPS的TD，并提出识别和修复的方法。在第一阶段，我们通过分析AI生态系统和AI-CPS仓库，以及采访开发人员来刻画AI-CPS的TD。基于所获得的知识，我们定义了识别和缓解此类TD的方法。最后，我们计划开发并验证一个自动化工具，该工具支持智能体AI解决方案来监控、治理和偿还AI-CPS的TD。

Original abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) components are increasingly pervasive in several software systems, including Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs). AI-CPS are used in several domains, including autonomous vehicles, industry, home automation, robotics, and healthcare. Being composed of hardware, AI components, and conventional modules, AI-CPS can exhibit technical debt (TD) that is peculiar and potentially more challenging than that of conventional systems. This thesis aims to characterize AI-CPS TD and propose approaches for its identification and repair. In a first phase, we characterize AI-CPS TD by analyzing AI ecosystems and AI-CPS repositories, as well as interviewing developers. Based on the acquired knowledge, we define approaches to identify and mitigate such TD. Finally, we plan to develop and validate an automated tool that supports agentic AI solutions to monitor, govern, and repay AI-CPS TD.

</details>


### [57] [Instruction Stacking Collapse: A Benchmark and the Capability-Dependent Value of Prompt Compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02639)
*Atul Anand,Sourav Chattaraj*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 研究多指令堆叠下指令遵循的非线性退化，并提出一种无需训练的指令编译器重写提示词，可显著提升较弱模型的遵循率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产提示词常包含多条指令，但现有研究很少关注约束累积对指令遵循的影响。

Method: 构建包含24条可验证指令的基准，一次堆叠1至20条，评估三个生产级LLM；并提出指令编译器在单次LLM调用中重写堆叠提示词且跨查询复用。

Result: 指令遵循率从约96%非线性降至20%，由成对冲突驱动；指令编译器为较弱模型恢复最高+11个百分点的遵循率，对较强模型基本无影响。

Conclusion: 重写本身而非额外令牌或重排序带来收益；已发布基准、验证器和缓存运行以便复现。

中文摘要：生产环境中的提示词很少只包含单一指令。一条系统消息可能同时要求有效的JSON、字数限制、三个引用和固定的语气。我们研究随着此类约束的累积，指令遵循如何退化。我们引入了一个基准测试，该基准测试一次堆叠24条经过验证器检查的指令，从一条到二十条，并评估了三个生产级LLM（Claude Sonnet 4.6、GPT-5-mini、Gemini 2.5 Flash）。指令遵循呈非线性退化：遵循率从约96%下降到低至20%，这由一组结构化且可复现的成对冲突驱动。例如，单条“输出JSON”约束与另外九条约束联合不可满足。然后，我们评估了一种无需训练的补救措施：一个指令编译器，它在单次LLM调用中重写堆叠的提示词，并在查询间复用。其收益是分能力等级的。对于较弱的模型（也是最常大规模部署的模型），它恢复了高达+11个百分点的遵循率，而对于已经内化了相同结构的较强模型，则基本保持不变。聚类稳健检验、相同基线对照以及族内缩放阶梯将该收益归因于重写本身，而非额外的令牌、重排序或测量余量。我们发布了基准测试、验证器和缓存运行结果，以供完全复现。

Original abstract: Production prompts rarely carry a single instruction. One system message may require valid JSON, a word limit, three citations, and a fixed tone at the same time. We study how instruction-following degrades as such constraints accumulate. We introduce a benchmark that stacks 24 verifier-checked instructions, one to twenty at a time, and evaluate three production-tier LLMs (Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-5-mini, Gemini 2.5 Flash). Instruction-following degrades non-linearly: the follow rate falls from ~96% to as low as 20%, driven by a structured and reproducible set of pairwise conflicts. A single "output JSON" constraint, for example, is jointly unsatisfiable with nine others. We then evaluate a training-free remedy: an instruction compiler that rewrites the stacked prompt in a single LLM call and is reused across queries. Its benefit is capability-graded. It recovers up to +11 points of follow rate for weaker models, which are also the models most often deployed at scale, while leaving stronger models, which already internalise the same structure, essentially unchanged. Cluster-robust tests, same-baseline controls, and a within-family scaling ladder attribute the gain to the rewrite itself rather than to additional tokens, reordering, or measurement headroom. We release the benchmark, verifiers, and cached runs for full reproduction.

</details>


### [58] [IR2Solve: Structured Intermediate Representations for Cost-Efficient Optimization Autoformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02641)
*Penglin Zhu,Linhai Zhang,Jungang Xu,Xinchi Wei,Xiuqi Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: IR2Solve是一种以中间表示优先的LLM自动公式化流水线，通过一次语义调用生成结构化ModelIR，再结合确定性验证与编译，在降低推理成本的同时保持目标正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接使用LLM生成求解器代码存在脆弱性，容易出现编译失败、模型不可行或目标错误，而现有方法依赖迭代修复或多智能体工作流，导致推理成本过高。

Method: IR2Solve使用单次语义LLM调用生成受模式约束的ModelIR（显式表示集合、参数、变量、目标和约束），并采用标量约束约定将逐索引约束族表示为独立条目，随后通过确定性的验证和IR到求解器编译两个阶段处理。

Result: 在六个基准上达到强目标正确性；消融实验显示三个设计选择均有增益；成本面板中IR2Solve每实例仅需1次语义调用，token量仅为Chain-of-Experts的1/3.3和SAC-Opt的1/22.9。

Conclusion: 结构化中间表示结合确定性后处理，为基于LLM的优化自动公式化提供了实用的准确性-成本权衡，是一种有效且经济的方案。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）可以将自然语言优化问题转化为求解器可用的公式，但直接生成代码是不稳定的：模式、索引和语义错误可能导致编译失败、模型不可行或目标不正确，而迭代修复、搜索和多智能体工作流会增加推理成本。我们提出了IR2Solve，一种以中间表示优先的自动公式化流水线，它使用一次语义LLM调用来生成受模式约束的ModelIR，随后是两个确定性阶段：验证和IR到求解器的编译。ModelIR使用受限的类Python表达式字符串显式表示集合、参数、变量、目标和约束。一种具体的标量约束约定将有限的逐索引约束族表示为单独的条目，减少了自由索引和隐式量化错误，同时简化了下游验证和编译。在六个清理后的优化基准上，IR2Solve实现了较强的目标正确性，并与最新的优化建模系统保持竞争力。对153个IndustryOR和ComplexLP实例的受控消融实验表明，结构化IR接口、标量约束指令和确定性验证带来了顺序性改进。在一个匹配的十实例成本面板上，IR2Solve每个实例使用一次语义调用，而Chain-of-Experts和SAC-Opt每个实例分别使用8次和39次调用，并分别消耗IR2Solve 3.3倍和22.9倍的token量。这些结果表明，结构化中间表示结合确定性的生成后处理，为基于LLM的优化自动公式化提供了一种实用的准确性-成本权衡。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can translate natural-language optimization problems into solver-ready formulations, but direct code generation is brittle: schema, indexing, and semantic errors can cause compilation failures, infeasible models, or incorrect objectives, while iterative repair, search, and multi-agent workflows increase inference cost. We present IR2Solve, an intermediate-representation-first autoformulation pipeline that uses a single semantic LLM call to produce a schema-constrained ModelIR, followed by two deterministic stages: verification and IR-to-solver compilation. ModelIR explicitly represents sets, parameters, variables, objectives, and constraints using restricted Python-like expression strings.
