A concrete scalar-constraint convention represents finite per-index constraint families as individual entries, reducing free-index and implicit-quantification errors while simplifying downstream verification and compilation. Across six cleaned optimization benchmarks, IR2Solve achieves strong objective correctness and remains competitive with recent optimization-modeling systems. A controlled ablation on 153 IndustryOR and ComplexLP instances shows sequential gains from the structured IR interface, the scalar-constraint instruction, and deterministic verification. On a matched ten-instance cost panel, IR2Solve uses one semantic call per instance, whereas Chain-of-Experts and SAC-Opt use 8 and 39 calls per instance and consume 3.3 and 22.9 times the token volume of IR2Solve, respectively. These results show that structured intermediate representations, combined with deterministic post-generation processing, provide a practical accuracy-cost trade-off for LLM-based optimization autoformulation.

</details>


### [59] [CUADebug: Diagnosing and Repairing Computer-Use Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02643)
*Weijia Zhang,Kunlun Zhu,Zeyi Liu,Yinting Chen,Tianyi Ma,Jiateng Liu,Jiaxun Zhang,Bingxuan Li,Xiangru Tang,Heng Ji,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出CUADebug框架，包含错误分类法、基准和调试器，通过根因诊断显著提升CUA故障修复效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CUA故障源于视觉感知、空间定位、交互、推理和环境动态的耦合，现有调试方法难以定位根因，需要专门的多模态因果定位框架。

Method: 构建CUA错误分类法CUAErrorBench（204条人工标注故障），开发CUADebugger，通过前后截图和动作痕迹主动检查可疑步骤，输出结构化诊断（根因步骤、错误类型、证据、纠正策略）用于重新执行。

Result: 任务推理和控制是最大故障族（110/204）；联合诊断准确率从11.2%提升至19.6%；单次重执行成功率从13.89%提升至29.90%，持续重执行从12.2%提升至25.86%。

Conclusion: CUA根因诊断能提供可操作的修复信号，而非仅事后解释，验证了结构化诊断在CUA调试中的有效性。

中文摘要：计算机使用智能体（CUA）通过截图、鼠标和键盘操作以及有状态的UI反馈来操作真实的桌面和网页界面，但它们的故障仍然难以诊断和修复。与纯文本智能体不同，CUA故障源于视觉感知、空间定位、低级交互、任务推理和环境动态的耦合，使得调试成为一个独特的多模态因果定位问题。我们提出了CUADebug，一个用于诊断和修复CUA故障的框架。CUADebug包含一个CUA特定的错误分类法、CUAErrorBench（一个人工标注的OSWorld故障基准）以及CUADebugger（一个工具增强的调试器）。CUADebugger不是一次性提示整个轨迹，而是主动检查可疑步骤，配对待修复前后的截图和动作痕迹，然后提交包含根因步骤、错误类型、基于证据的定位和纠正策略的结构化诊断，用于重新执行。对204条失败轨迹的人工标注显示，任务推理和控制是最大的故障族（110/204），其次是感知（36）、定位/交互（25）、外部/系统（13），以及20个OSWorld不可行任务案例的其他类别。在主要的Claude智能体划分上，CUADebugger将联合子类型和步骤诊断从11.2%提高到19.6%（使用Gemini 2.5 Pro），并在不同调试器骨干上保持一致改进。在单次重新执行包评估中，基于RCA的条件比仅历史延续获得更高的任务完成率（机器RCA为28.47%，我们的方法为29.90%，而历史延续为13.89%）；在持续重新执行中，我们的方法将成功率从12.2%提高到25.86%，而人类预言指导达到29.21%。这些结果表明，CUA根因诊断可以提供可操作的修复信号，而不仅仅是事后解释。

Original abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) operate real desktop and web interfaces through screenshots, mouse and keyboard actions, and stateful UI feedback, yet their failures remain difficult to diagnose and repair. Unlike text-only agents, CUA failures arise from coupled visual perception, spatial grounding, low-level interaction, task reasoning, and environment dynamics, making debugging a distinctive multimodal causal localization problem. We introduce CUADebug, a framework for diagnosing and repairing CUA failures. CUADebug includes a CUA-specific error taxonomy, CUAErrorBench, a human-annotated OSWorld failure benchmark, and CUADebugger, a tool-augmented debugger. Instead of prompting over the full trajectory once, CUADebugger actively inspects suspicious steps with paired before/after screenshots and action traces, then submits a structured diagnosis containing the root-cause step, error type, grounded evidence, and corrective strategy for re-execution. Human annotations over 204 failed trajectories show that task reasoning and control is the largest failure family (110/204), followed by perception (36), grounding/interaction (25), external/system (13), and an others category of 20 OSWorld infeasible-task cases. On the main Claude-agent split, CUADebugger improves joint subtype-and-step diagnosis from 11.2% to 19.6% with Gemini 2.5 Pro and improves consistently across debugger backbones. In single re-execution package evaluation, RCA-based conditions achieve higher task completion than history-only continuation (28.47% with machine RCA and 29.90% with our method, versus 13.89%); in continual re-execution, our method improves success from 12.2% to 25.86%, while human-oracle guidance reaches 29.21%. These results show that CUA root-cause diagnosis can provide actionable repair signals rather than merely post-hoc explanations.

</details>


### [60] [A Unified Feature Model for Microservice Identification and Refactoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02644)
*Ana Almeida,António Rito Silva*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于微服务识别工具的特征模型，通过系统映射和工具实例化评估，发现现有工具互补覆盖了设计空间，但缺乏统一集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微服务识别方法多样，但缺乏共同概念框架，导致系统比较受限；且不存在单一最优方法，需要整合多种方法以探索设计权衡。

Method: 基于对现有技术的广泛分析提出特征模型，并通过系统映射代表性文献和分析现有工具架构中的实例化来评估该模型。

Result: 特征模型成功捕获了现有方法论的主要变化点；没有单个工具覆盖模型的大部分，但工具联合几乎覆盖所有变化点，呈现互补性。

Conclusion: 设计空间已被碎片化地填充于独立工具中，需要统一框架来比较和整合替代方案，以支持更全面的微服务识别。

中文摘要：已有多种方法被提出用于在单体系统中自动识别微服务。这些方法论在数据收集与分析技术、所应用的分解算法，以及用于可视化和细化候选微服务的机制上各不相同。尽管存在这种多样性，但由于缺乏共同的概念框架，跨方法的系统实验与比较仍然受限。此外，当前研究表明不存在单一最优方法；相反，有必要整合多种方法，以充分探索任何设计解决方案中固有的权衡。为弥补这一空白，本文在对现有技术进行广泛分析的基础上，提出了一个面向变体丰富的微服务识别工具的特征模型。我们通过对代表性文献的系统映射，并分析该模型在现有微服务识别工具架构中的实例化，来评估这一特征模型。我们的发现有两方面。第一，所提出的特征模型成功捕获了现有方法论的主要变化点，为分析、比较和设计微服务识别工具提供了统一基础。第二，虽然没有任何单个工具覆盖该模型的超过一小部分，但所分析的工具共同几乎涵盖了其所有变化点。这种互补性是核心结果：设计空间已被填充，但分散在独立的工具中，其中没有任何工具能够比较或整合其他工具所实现的替代方案。

Original abstract: Several approaches have been proposed for the automatic identification of microservices within monolithic systems. These methodologies differ in their data collection and analysis techniques, the decomposition algorithms applied, and the mechanisms used to visualize and refine candidate microservices. Despite this diversity, systematic experimentation and comparison across approaches remain limited by the absence of a common conceptual framework. Furthermore, current research indicates that no single optimal method exists; rather, integrating multiple approaches is necessary to fully explore the trade-offs inherent in any design solution.
  To address this gap, this paper proposes a feature model for variant-rich microservice identification tools, grounded in an extensive analysis of the state of the art. We evaluate this feature model through a systematic mapping of representative literature and by analyzing its instantiation within the architecture of an existing microservice identification tool. Our findings are two-fold. First, the proposed feature model successfully captures the primary variation points of existing methodologies, providing a unifying foundation for analyzing, comparing, and designing microservice identification tools. Second, while no individual tool covers more than a fraction of the model, the analyzed tools jointly span almost all of its variation points. This complementarity is the central result: the design space is already populated, but it is fragmented across standalone tools, none of which can compare or integrate the alternatives that the others implement.

</details>


### [61] [Verified Tool Calls Improve LLM Agent Reliability Under Non-Atomic Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02645)
*Isham Kalappurackal Mansoor,Abhishek Phadke,Pratip Rana*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出了一种验证感知的工具包装器，通过后置条件验证、验证后重试逻辑和幂等键来增强LLM代理的工具调用，从而减少重复操作并保持任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理框架假设工具调用是原子且返回二元成功/失败信号，但现实系统存在超时、延迟可见性、部分状态更新等非原子行为，导致重复操作、任务成功问题及多余工具执行等可靠性问题。

Method: 引入轻量级验证感知工具包装器，为工具调用添加后置条件验证、验证后重试逻辑和幂等键，以增强工具交互语义。

Result: 在受控模拟环境中注入非原子故障进行多任务模板评估，结果显示该方法显著减少了重复操作，同时保持了相当的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 强化工具交互语义是提高LLM代理可靠性的一种有前景的方向，无需修改底层语言模型即可实现改进。

中文摘要：大语言模型（LLM）代理依赖外部工具来执行多阶段任务。现有的代理框架通常假设工具调用是原子性的，并返回二进制的成功或失败信号。然而，现实世界的系统表现出非原子性行为，例如调度后的超时、延迟可见性和部分状态更新。这些不匹配会导致可靠性问题，包括重复操作、任务成功和多余的工具执行。本文介绍了一种轻量级的、具有验证意识的工具包装器，它通过后置条件验证、验证后重试逻辑和幂等键来增强工具调用。该方法在受控模拟环境中进行了评估，该环境在多个任务模板中注入了非原子性故障。结果表明，所提出的方法显著减少了重复操作，同时保持了相当的任务成功率。总的来说，研究结果表明，强化工具交互语义是提高LLM代理可靠性的一条有前途的途径，而无需对底层语言模型进行修改。

Original abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents rely on external tools to perform multistage tasks. Existing agent frameworks typically assume that tool calls are atomic and return binary success or failure signals. However, real-world systems exhibit non-atomic behaviors such as timeouts after dispatch, delayed visibility, and partial state updates. These mismatches lead to reliability issues including duplicate actions, task success, and unnecessary tool executions. A lightweight, verification-aware tool wrapper is introduced that augments tool calls with postcondition verification, verify-before-retry logic, and idempotency keys. The approach is evaluated in a controlled simulated environment with injected non-atomic failures across multiple task templates. The results demonstrate that the proposed method significantly reduces duplicate actions, while maintaining comparable task success rates. Overall, the findings suggest that strengthening tool interaction semantics is a promising direction for improving LLM agent reliability without requiring modifications to the underlying language model.

</details>


### [62] [Intent-Level Quantum Programming with Assertion-Guided Execution and Inspectable Intermediate Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02648)
*Ilesh Vora,Srikanth Thudumu,John Carlson,Harshil Kamdar,Rajesh Vasa*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出一种量子领域特定语言，通过意图-执行分离、IR内省和断言引导模式推断，简化量子程序验证，并在基准测试中实现高故障检测率和跨框架一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子程序验证困难，因为电路缺乏可检查性、执行模式需手动选择、输出概率性，且跨后端框架移植时约定不同，需要更系统化的验证机制。

Method: 设计QDSL，包含三个机制：将算法构造作为可检查对象并在IR验证后编译；提供IR内省暴露电路属性；利用验证断言自动选择执行模式。实现原型并评估。

Result: 在故障注入中，TPR为1.0，仅128射击时FPR为2.7-3.8%；差分测试显示PennyLane和Qiskit编译目标数值一致；IR生成时间低于毫秒。

Conclusion: 该QDSL通过可检查的表示和自动化模式选择，提升了量子程序验证的效率和可移植性，且性能开销极低，适合实际应用。

中文摘要：量子程序难以验证：电路通常表示为可检查性有限的命令式门序列，执行模式必须手动选择，并且输出本质上是概率性的。当程序必须跨内部约定不同的后端框架移植时，这些挑战会更加复杂。我们提出了一种量子领域特定语言（QDSL），通过三种机制解决这些问题：（i）意图-执行分离，其中制备、叠加、纠缠和测量等算法结构表示为可检查的对象，仅在预执行中间表示（IR）验证后编译为后端特定电路；（ii）一流IR内省，在执行前向开发者公开电路宽度、线映射、操作顺序和测量意图以进行检查；（iii）断言引导的模式推断，引擎检查声明的验证属性以自动选择采样、态向量或双重执行，无需用户干预。结果以结构化形式记录，以支持可复现的回归测试。我们在涵盖纠缠、基于Oracle、结构化变换和变分示例的基准电路上评估了我们的原型。在对Bell和3量子比特GHZ电路的故障注入实验中，全变差距离检测器在所有评估的射击预算下识别出注入的故障（真阳性率1.0），观察到的假阳性（假阳性率2.7-3.8%）仅在128次射击时出现。差分测试表明，在字节序规范化后，PennyLane和Qiskit编译目标在评估的构造上数值一致。IR生成成本低于毫秒。

Original abstract: Quantum programs are difficult to validate: circuits are typically expressed as imperative gate sequences with limited inspectability, execution modalities must be selected manually, and outputs are inherently probabilistic. These challenges are compounded when programs must be portable across backend frameworks with differing internal conventions. We present a quantum domain-specific language (QDSL) that addresses these problems through three mechanisms: (i) intent-execution separation, where algorithmic constructs such as preparation, superposition, entanglement, and measurement are represented as inspectable objects compiled into backend-specific circuits only after pre-execution Intermediate Representation (IR) validation; (ii) first-class IR introspection, exposing circuit width, wire mapping, operation order, and measurement intent for developer inspection prior to execution; and (iii) assertion-guided modality inference, where the engine examines declared verification properties to automatically select sampling, statevector, or dual execution without user intervention. Results are logged in structured form to support reproducible regression testing. We evaluate our prototype on benchmark circuits spanning entanglement, oracle-based, structured-transform, and variational examples. In fault-injection experiments on Bell and 3-qubit GHZ circuits, a total-variation-distance detector identifies the injected faults across all evaluated shot budgets (True Positive Rate 1.0), with observed false positives (False Positive Rate 2.7-3.8%) only at 128 shots. Differential testing demonstrates numerical agreement across PennyLane and Qiskit compilation targets for the evaluated constructs after endianness canonicalization. IR generation costs are sub-millisecond.

</details>


### [63] [When Policies Change Probabilities: Modular Decision-Making for LLM Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02677)
*Rasvik Kudum,Max Corbett,Hitansh Paliwal,Romaisa Fatima,Thomas Jiralerspong,Sneheel Sarangi*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 研究发现，LLM 代码审查者在同一提示中混合概率估计和决策策略，导致成本策略扭曲了报告的风险概率；模块化流水线分离风险、证据和行动，能提高准确性并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 代码审查工具往往在单个提示中同时估计补丁风险和做出批准决策，混淆了概率评估和基于成本的行动选择，可能导致概率被下游策略影响，从而产生次优决策。

Method: 使用 15,792 条响应，对 720 个候选补丁（360 个仓库问题，每个含一个通过和一个失败补丁）测试四个已部署审查者界面，比较等成本与 10:1 假接受策略下的概率和行动；并评估一个模块化流水线，该流水线在无策略信息下引出风险，结合独立监控器分数，在代码中应用成本。

Result: 改变成本策略使报告失败概率平均变化 13.6-16.9 个百分点，所有审查者的高成本行动均差于全部拒绝；将高成本规则应用于等成本下引出的概率可降低损失。模块化流水线在等成本下平均概率准确性提高，每个问题损失降低 0.073，接受 58-68% 补丁；在 10:1 时不接受任何补丁并匹配全部拒绝。

Conclusion: 下游策略会改变其本应使用的概率，因此必须将风险估计、外部证据和行动决策分开评估，模块化方法能有效提高概率准确性和决策质量。

中文摘要：LLM 代码审查者通常在一个提示中估计补丁风险并做出批准决定。概率应取决于证据；成本应决定根据概率采取的行动。我们使用 15,792 条针对 720 个候选补丁的响应，测试了四个已部署的审查者界面是否保持了这种分离，其中每个补丁对应 360 个仓库问题中的一个，每个问题都有一个通过和一个未通过存档测试套件的补丁。在匹配的调用中，补丁和监控证据固定，将等成本策略替换为 10:1 的假接受策略，会使报告失败概率平均变化 13.6 至 16.9 个百分点。对于每个审查者，在高成本提示下返回的行动都比拒绝所有补丁更差。将相同的高成本规则应用于等成本下获得的概率，对四个系统都降低了损失，表明概率引出本身导致了超额损失。我们还评估了一个模块化流水线，它在没有策略信息的情况下引出风险，结合独立的监控器分数，并在代码中应用成本。相对于校准后的仅审查者分数，该流水线提高了平均概率准确性，并在等成本下，将每个问题的平均损失降低了 0.073，同时接受了 58% 至 68% 的补丁。在 10:1 的情况下，它不接受任何补丁，并与全部拒绝相匹配。因此，下游策略可以改变它本应使用的概率，这促使对风险、外部证据和行动进行独立评估。

Original abstract: LLM code reviewers often estimate patch risk and make approval decisions in one prompt. A probability should depend on evidence; costs should determine the action taken from it. We test whether four deployed reviewer interfaces preserve this separation using 15,792 responses on 720 candidate patches, with one that passed and one that failed an archived test harness for each of 360 repository issues. In matched calls with the patch and monitor evidence fixed, replacing an equal-cost policy with a 10:1 false-accept policy changes reported failure probabilities by 13.6 to 16.9 percentage points on average. For every reviewer, the actions returned under the high-cost prompt are worse than rejecting all patches. Applying the same high-cost rule to probabilities elicited under equal costs reduces loss for all four systems, showing that probability elicitation itself contributes to the excess loss. We also evaluate a modular pipeline that elicits risk without policy information, combines an independent monitor score, and applies costs in code. Relative to calibrated reviewer-only scores, the pipeline improves average probability accuracy and, at equal costs, reduces mean loss by .073 per issue while accepting 58 to 68% of patches. At 10:1, it accepts none and matches reject-all. Downstream policy can therefore change the probability it is meant to use, motivating separate evaluation of risk, outside evidence, and action.

</details>


### [64] [AI Sandbox: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02679)
*Muhammad Waseem,Md Aidul Islam,Md Nasir Uddin Shuvo,Md Mahade Hasan,Kai-Kristian Kemell,Jussi Rasku,Mika Saari,Vilma Saari,Roope Pajasmaa,Markku Oivo,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个治理感知的多租户AI沙箱的设计与实现，该沙箱支持结构化实验、可重用评估证据，并通过分离UI与控制平面、专用执行和数据管理层来实现合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行业与学术界需要既支持快速原型制作又满足受控访问、租户隔离和透明工作流的AI实验平台，但缺乏关于集成实验与治理需求的实用设计指导。

Method: 基于与工业伙伴迭代细化的需求，设计了一种参考架构，将多租户UI与后端控制平面分离，并将执行和数据管理放在专用层，同时实现治理功能如用户入职、审批、审计和可追踪实验。

Result: 平台支持受治理的协作、受管理的AI服务访问、审批工作流和审计日志，并将实验配置、上下文和治理决策持久化，以支持跨项目的证据比较与重用。开发过程提供了实用经验。

Conclusion: 这项工作为在协作研究和工业环境中部署和扩展治理感知的AI沙箱平台提供了设计实现和实用经验，填补了相关实践指导的空白。

中文摘要：跨行业和学术界的协作式AI实验需要能够实现快速原型制作，同时保持受控访问、租户隔离和透明工作流的平台。尽管对AI沙箱的兴趣日益增长，但关于如何设计和实现将实验功能与治理要求相结合的平台的实用指导仍然有限。这项工作提出了一个治理感知的多租户AI沙箱的设计与实现，用于跨项目和利益相关者群体的结构化实验以及可重用评估证据的生成。该沙箱是在行业-学术界合作中开发的，其需求是与工业合作伙伴迭代细化的。其参考架构将多租户用户界面与后端控制平面分离，并将执行和数据管理功能置于专用层中。该平台支持受治理的用户入职、以项目为中心的合作、对AI服务的受管理访问、审批工作流、审计日志记录和可追踪实验。实验配置、上下文信息和治理决策存储为持久记录，允许跨项目比较和重用证据和结果。开发过程为在协作研究和工业环境中部署和扩展治理感知的AI沙箱平台提供了实用经验。

Original abstract: Collaborative AI experimentation across industry and academia requires platforms that enable rapid prototyping while preserving controlled access, tenant separation, and transparent workflows. Despite growing interest in AI sandboxes, there is still limited practical guidance on how to design and implement platforms that integrate experimentation capabilities with governance requirements. This work presents the design and implementation of a governance-aware, multi-tenant AI sandbox for structured experimentation and the generation of reusable evaluation evidence across projects and stakeholder groups.
  The sandbox was developed within an industry-academia collaboration based on requirements that were iteratively refined with industrial partners. Its reference architecture separates the multi-tenant user interface from the backend control plane and places execution and data-management functions in dedicated layers. The platform supports governed user onboarding, project-centered collaboration, managed access to AI services, approval workflows, audit logging, and traceable experimentation. Experiment configurations, contextual information, and governance decisions are stored as persistent records, allowing evidence and outcomes to be compared and reused across projects.
  The development process provides practical lessons for deploying and extending governance-aware AI sandbox platforms in collaborative research and industrial environments.

</details>


### [65] [TraceCompiler: Skill-Guided Mining and Compilation of LLM Agent Traces into Mostly Deterministic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02680)
*Salma El Yadouni,Guanyi Li*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出TraceCompiler，从嘈杂的智能体轨迹中挖掘并编译出确定性工作流，通过可审计的依赖边提升依赖恢复精度，并减少重复API调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体在重复执行任务时会生成混合了可复用结构与噪声的轨迹，现有方法难以从中提取稳定、可审计的工作流。

Method: TraceCompiler基于技能引导，从轨迹簇中挖掘生产者-消费者依赖，仅接受可唯一归因的硬依赖并附加证据元组，将绑定分类为常量、用户输入、复制输出、变换或LLM决策，从而编译为确定性工作流。

Result: 在T1上依赖恢复精确率0.928/召回0.943，优于基线F1约0.71；编译器盲跑可达0.992。在AppWorld上精确率0.993。Venmo意图调用从34次降至11次，并在21项状态测试中通过15项；对副作用不确定的意图正确拒绝编译。

Conclusion: TraceCompiler能有效从噪声轨迹中编译可复用工作流，提高可审计性并减少调用，但未评估离线编译成本，因此不主张净效率收益。

中文摘要：使用工具的语言模型智能体会反复重新发现它们已经执行过的程序，产生的轨迹混合了可复用结构、重试、探索、偶然排序和重复查找。我们提出TraceCompiler，一个技能引导的系统，它挖掘嘈杂的智能体轨迹簇，并将其编译为可执行的、大多数情况下确定性的工作流。它仅当消费者参数包含唯一可归因于先前生产者的值时才承认工具间依赖关系；每条硬边携带一个可审计的证据元组，模糊关系被标记为疑似且不施加排序约束。绑定被分类为常量、用户输入、复制输出、变换或残余的LLM决策。在T1上，该规则的机械化形式在其训练划分的15,775条def-use边上恢复了生产者-消费者依赖关系，精确率0.928，召回率0.943，而在相同数据上，邻接方法的F1为0.711，频率阈值直接跟随方法的F1为0.712；编译器技能盲跑在其中的250条边上达到0.992。在AppWorld上，我们在确定性模拟器中重放发布的轨迹以恢复被掩蔽的返回值，并在563个token边上以0.993的精确率衡量该规则——这是一个自一致性检查，因为重放通过相关启发式注入token。我们编译两个重复出现的意图：一个Venmo请求金钱意图将34次观察到的API调用减少到11次运行时调用，并且在针对基准自身的状态测试进行留一法执行时，通过21次中的15次，失败的那一折选择升级而不是行动，因为它所需的分支从未被观察到；以及一个Spotify/Todoist意图，编译器正确拒绝编译，因为一个不可逆的副作用未被充分确定。我们衡量了调用减少量，但没有衡量离线编译成本，因此我们不声称净效率结果。

Original abstract: Tool-using language-model agents repeatedly rediscover procedures they have already executed, producing traces that mix reusable structure with retries, exploration, accidental ordering, and repeated lookups. We present TraceCompiler, a skill-guided system that mines clusters of noisy agent traces and compiles them into executable, mostly deterministic workflows. It admits an inter-tool dependency only when a consumer argument contains a value attributable uniquely to an earlier producer; every hard edge carries an auditable evidence tuple, and ambiguous relations are marked suspected and impose no ordering constraint. Bindings are classified as constants, user inputs, copied outputs, transforms, or residual LLM decisions. On T1, a mechanized form of the rule recovers producer-consumer dependencies at 0.928 precision and 0.943 recall over 15,775 def-use edges of its training split, against 0.711 F1 for adjacency and 0.712 for a frequency-thresholded directly-follows measure on identical data; the compiler skill run blind reaches 0.992 on 250 of those edges. On AppWorld we replay released trajectories in the deterministic simulator to recover masked return values and measure the rule against 563 token edges at 0.993 precision - a self-consistency check, since replay injects tokens by a related heuristic. We compile two recurring intents: a Venmo money-request intent reduces 34 observed API calls to 11 runtime calls and, under leave-one-out execution against the benchmark's own state tests, passes 15 of 21, the failing fold escalating rather than acting because its required branch was never observed; and a Spotify/Todoist intent the compiler correctly refuses to compile, because an irreversible side effect is under-determined. We measure call reduction but not offline compilation cost, so we claim no net efficiency result.

</details>


### [66] [Studying Developer Perceptions on the Potential of CI Recommendation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02682)
*Osamah H. Alaini,Taher A. Ghaleb*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 一项针对约5000名GitHub开发者的探索性调查，旨在了解CI采用和服务的驱动因素、CI的必要性认知与采用障碍，以及对自动化CI推荐系统的看法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者难以选择最合适的CI服务，先前研究仅识别了采用障碍，但缺乏关于CI服务选择决策的实证证据，尤其是需求驱动与社会影响的区分。

Method: 探索性调查研究，联系约5000名活跃GitHub开发者（包括CI用户和非用户），通过问卷或访谈收集数据，分析采用动机、必要性认知、障碍及对推荐系统的感知。

Result: 研究尚处于计划阶段，预期结果将揭示CI采用和服务选择中的需求驱动与社会影响动机、开发者对CI普遍性或情境依赖性的看法、以及阻碍采用的具体障碍，并提供对自动化CI推荐系统感知的洞察。

Conclusion: 该研究将填补CI采用实证证据的空白，为开发CI推荐系统的研究人员和简化开源项目CI采用的从业者提供有价值的指导。

中文摘要：持续集成（CI）是现代软件开发的核心，但开发者往往难以选择最合适的CI服务。先前的研究已经识别了采用CI的障碍，但关于开发者如何选择CI服务，或者采用决策是由真实项目需求驱动还是受社会影响驱动，几乎没有实证证据。本文提出一项探索性调查研究以填补这一空白。我们旨在联系约5000名活跃的GitHub开发者，包括CI用户和非用户。该研究调查：（1）什么推动了CI的采用和服务选择，区分需求驱动与社会影响的动机；（2）开发者是否认为CI是普遍必要的还是依赖具体情境，以及什么障碍阻碍了采用；（3）开发者对自动化CI推荐系统的看法。我们的发现将为开发CI推荐系统的研究人员以及旨在简化开源项目中CI采用实践的从业者提供信息。

Original abstract: Continuous Integration (CI) is central to modern software development, yet developers often struggle to choose the most suitable CI service. Prior work has identified barriers to CI adoption but offers little empirical evidence on how developers select CI services or whether adoption decisions are driven by genuine project needs versus social influence. This paper presents an exploratory survey study addressing that gap. We aim to contact about 5,000 active GitHub developers, including both CI users and non-users. The study investigates: (1) what drives CI adoption and service selection, distinguishing need-driven from socially influenced motivations; (2) whether developers consider CI universally necessary or context-dependent and what barriers hinder adoption; and (3) developers' perceptions of automated CI recommendation systems. Our findings will inform researchers developing CI recommendation systems and practitioners aiming to streamline CI adoption in open-source projects.

</details>


### [67] [BulkPR-Bench: Benchmarking Queue-Level Governance of Interacting Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02685)
*Zetong Xiong,Qiao Zhao,Jun Zhang,Xueying Lyu,Zhi Li,Yixiang Tu,Xiaowen Yang,Yunjie Zhang,Yufeng Wang,Zhe Zhang,Kaize Yu,Hanwen Du,Zhongkai Sun,Zhuoxin Liu,Zekun Lin,Jianwen Yang,Ruining Chen,Ying Zhang,Tingxuan Pan,Ke Chen,Shubin Han,Chuanhao Sun,Yehua Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出了BulkPR-Bench基准，用于评估编码智能体在滚动发布协议下处理相互交互的PR队列的能力，实验表明现有模型在关系组上的表现提升尚未转化为可靠的整个队列治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体基准通常只处理单一结果或固定变更序列，忽略了PR队列中候选之间的交互。当PR相互影响时，顺序策略无法最优地决定合并哪些变更以及顺序，从而难以最大化安全交付。

Method: 构建BulkPR-Bench，包含18个真实仓库冻结快照上的581个候选PR，通过注册的逐状态仓库执行（含隐藏安全检查）验证黄金关系图，并使用精确预言机计算最大安全子集；提出关系交付得分（RDS）和全局安全门控产出（Global-SGY）作为评价指标。

Result: 在批量大小K=32的协议下，六模型中最高三个RDS分别为66.6%、62.0%和57.9%，强于最强顺序基线的53.1%；但324次运行中仅8次精确完成队列，关键关系召回率在35.2%至57.7%之间，提供黄金关系的诊断运行显示仍有大量改进空间。

Conclusion: 尽管模型在关系组上的表现有所提升，但尚未转化为可靠的整个队列治理，距离精确完成复杂PR队列仍有显著差距，需要进一步改进关系发现和全局规划能力。

中文摘要：编码智能体基准测试越来越涵盖长周期、端到端和交互式开发，但通常只保留一个请求的结果或固定的变更序列。顺序策略可以一次处理一个拉取请求（PR）队列中的候选，但当队列中的PR相互交互时，最大化安全交付可能需要联合决定合并哪些变更以及以何种顺序合并。我们引入了BulkPR-Bench，一个可执行的基准测试，其中智能体必须恢复重要的PR关系，并在滚动发布协议下按可执行顺序返回一个大的安全子集。该套件包含581个新编写的候选PR，位于18个真实仓库的冻结快照上。注册的逐状态仓库执行（包括隐藏的安全检查）验证了黄金关系图；然后一个精确的预言机计算最大的安全子集。我们的主要指标——关系交付得分（RDS）——根据实际合并轨迹对关系组的安全交付和正确拒绝进行评分；全局安全门控产出（Global-SGY）则单独衡量实际整个队列计划的严格交付。在批量大小K=32的缓冲主协议下，六个模型中三个最高的RDS估计值分别为66.6%、62.0%和57.9%，而最强的顺序基线为53.1%。324次模型运行中只有8次精确完成一个队列。关键关系召回率在35.2%到57.7%之间，并且提供黄金关系的诊断运行显示出相当大的剩余提升空间。因此，关系组上的收益尚未转化为可靠的整个队列治理。

Original abstract: Coding-agent benchmarks increasingly cover long-horizon, end-to-end, and interactive development, but typically retain one requested outcome or a fixed change sequence. Sequential policies can process a pull-request (PR) queue one candidate at a time, but when queued PRs interact, maximizing safe delivery can require jointly deciding which changes to merge and in what order. We introduce BulkPR-Bench, an executable benchmark in which an agent must recover consequential PR relations and return a large safe subset in executable order under a rolling-release protocol. The suite contains 581 newly authored candidate PRs on frozen snapshots of 18 real repositories. Registered state-by-state repository execution, including hidden safety checks, validates the gold relation graph; an exact oracle then computes the largest safe subset. Our primary metric, Relational Delivery Score (RDS), scores safe delivery and correct rejection over relation groups from the realized merge trace; Global Safety-Gated Yield (Global-SGY) separately measures strict delivery of the realized whole-queue plan. Under the buffered primary protocol with batch size $K=32$, the three highest RDS estimates among the six models are 66.6%, 62.0%, and 57.9%, compared with 53.1% for the strongest sequential baseline. Only 8 of 324 model runs complete a queue exactly. Critical-relation recall ranges from 35.2% to 57.7%, and diagnostic runs supplied with the gold relations show substantial remaining headroom. Gains on relation groups therefore do not yet translate into dependable whole-queue governance.

</details>


### [68] [PRWeaver: Evaluating LLM-Based Code Auditors against Long-Horizon Malicious Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02693)
*Yuekun Wang,Mingfei Cheng,Xiaofei Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: PRWeaver基准测试表明，在拉取请求审查中，将攻击与良性修改交错或融合到连贯载体中，比单纯跨提交分解更能有效规避基于LLM的代码审计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对LLM代码审计器在面对跨仓库演化的对抗性修改时可靠性的系统评估，特别是在PR工作流中，攻击者可能利用审查上下文和叙事来隐藏恶意代码。

Method: 构建PRWeaver基准，包含208个执行验证的攻击，每个攻击在四种匹配的审查渲染下实例化，共832个渲染。评估三个PR审计代理在六个审计器-模型系统上的检测性能，并对比不同攻击分解和融合策略。

Result: 攻击分解对检测率影响微弱（≤5个百分点），而每PR交错（N=16）和连贯载体融合分别降低检测率5-13和10-18个百分点。在整窗口审查（N=24）下，检测率仅为16-22%，远低于每PR审查的50-60%。

Conclusion: 仅提供仓库历史不足以防止规避；当恶意修改与良性修改混合在审查上下文中，或攻击被包装成看似合理的目的时，隐蔽攻击更有效。这提示需要改进审查上下文管理和目的验证机制。

中文摘要：基于LLM的代码审计器正越来越多地集成到拉取请求（PR）工作流中，但它们在跨仓库演化的对抗性修改下的可靠性仍然知之甚少。我们引入了PRWeaver，一个包含来自十个真实仓库的208个经过执行验证的攻击的基准，每个攻击在四种匹配的审查渲染下实例化（共832个渲染）。我们评估了三个PR审计代理在六个审计器-模型系统上的表现。在所有系统中，将攻击分解对检测率的影响最多为五个百分点，表明提交边界本身并不能解释规避。相比之下，在$N=16$时，每PR交错和连贯载体融合分别将检测率降低5-13和10-18个百分点。在$N=24$的整窗口审查下，检测率降至16-22%，而每PR审查为50-60%。这些结果表明，访问仓库历史是不够的：当良性修改和恶意修改共同占据审计者的活动审查上下文，或者当所述目的合理地解释了携带攻击的差异时，隐蔽变得最有效。

Original abstract: LLM-based code auditors are increasingly integrated into pull-request (PR) workflows, yet their reliability against adversarial changes distributed across repository evolution remains poorly understood. We introduce PRWeaver, a benchmark of 208 execution-validated attacks from ten real-world repositories, each instantiated under four matched review renderings (832 renderings in total). We evaluate three PR-auditing agents across six auditor-model systems. Across all systems, decomposing an attack changes detection by at most five percentage points, showing that commit boundaries alone do not explain evasion. In contrast, per-PR interleaving at $N=16$ and coherent carrier fusion reduce detection by 5-13 and 10-18 points, respectively. Under whole-window review at $N=24$, detection falls to 16-22%, compared with 50-60% under per-PR review. These results show that access to repository history is insufficient: concealment becomes most effective when benign and malicious changes jointly occupy the auditor's active review context or when the stated purpose plausibly accounts for the attack-bearing diff.

</details>


### [69] [Don't Regenerate, Debug: A Domain-Specific Agent for Repairing Near-Miss Hardware Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02712)
*Yansong Sun,Shenxiu Wu,Siyuan Chen,Runlin Hou,Junhao Qiu,Junming Cao,Shudi Shao,Zhichao Lu,Qingfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出领域专用调试代理，通过调试而非重新生成来修复接近成功的硬件内核，显著提升首次通过率并降低token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM内核生成流水线丢弃大量接近正确但因数值验证失败而被淘汰的候选内核，浪费其中蕴含的领域知识和计算投入；重新生成不如调试，因为调试搜索空间小、反馈密集。

Method: 设计领域专用调试代理，利用检索模式和诊断插桩缓解知识稀缺，采用反作弊检测和全覆盖评估保证完整性，并通过收敛保护和有界迭代控制成本。

Result: Debug Pass@1达66.7%，优于重新生成的Avg Pass@1（25.9%）和Pass@3（40.7%），每次成功token消耗降低92.8%；消融实验表明知识库驱动恢复，完整性门控拒绝12.5%-33.3%的工作流误接受结果。

Conclusion: 调试范式能有效恢复重新生成无法产生的算子，扩展能力边界并降低成本，为硬件内核生成提供了一种更高效的纠错路径。

中文摘要：面向GPU和NPU等硬件加速器的内核生成已成为大语言模型（LLM）的试验场，最先进的系统通过将LLM与智能体强化学习和进化搜索相结合的流水线来提高正确性。此类流水线生成、编译并执行大量候选内核，丢弃其中大部分，从而放弃了将失败提炼为可复用知识的机会。许多被丢弃的候选者是接近成功的算子：它们能编译并运行，但未通过数值验证；每个算子都蕴含真正的领域知识，以及LLM推理、交叉编译和硬件执行方面的不菲投入。我们主张一种范式转变：与其重新生成，不如调试。调试比从头生成所受约束大得多：搜索空间小，反馈密集。我们提出一个领域专用调试代理，以应对自主修复中的三个核心挑战：通过检索模式和诊断插桩缓解知识稀缺，通过反作弊检测和全覆盖评估确保完整性，通过收敛保护和有界迭代控制成本。调试发挥着两个互补作用：它通过恢复重复重新生成无法产生的算子来扩展能力边界，并降低每个可交付算子的成本。Debug Pass@1达到66.7%，而Regenerate Avg Pass@1为25.9%、Regenerate Pass@3为40.7%，且每次成功消耗的token比三次重新生成少92.8%。组件消融实验表明，知识库驱动恢复，而完整性门控拒绝了工作流自身接受的12.5%-33.3%的成功结果。

Original abstract: Kernel generation for hardware accelerators such as GPUs and NPUs has become a proving ground for large language models (LLMs), and state-of-the-art systems raise correctness through pipelines that couple LLMs with agentic reinforcement learning and evolutionary search. Such pipelines generate, compile, and execute large numbers of candidate kernels, discarding most of them and forgoing the opportunity to distill failures into reusable knowledge. Many discarded candidates are near-miss operators that compile and run but fail numerical validation; each embodies genuine domain knowledge and a nontrivial investment in LLM inference, cross-compilation, and hardware execution. We argue for a paradigm shift: rather than regenerate, debug. Debugging is far more constrained than generating from scratch: the search space is small and feedback is dense. We present a domain-specific debug agent that addresses three core challenges in autonomous repair: mitigating knowledge scarcity through retrieved patterns and diagnostic instrumentation, ensuring integrity through anti-cheat detection and full-coverage evaluation, and controlling cost via convergence guards and bounded iteration. Debugging serves two complementary roles: it extends the capability frontier by recovering operators that repeated regeneration fails to produce, and it lowers cost per deliverable operator. Debug Pass@1 achieves 66.7% versus Regenerate Avg Pass@1's 25.9% and Regenerate Pass@3's 40.7%, while consuming 92.8% fewer tokens per success than three-trial regeneration. Component ablations show that the knowledge base drives recovery, while integrity gates reject 12.5-33.3% of the successes the workflow itself accepted.

</details>


### [70] [HyperFL: Query-Adaptive Representation Learning for Software Fault Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02967)
*Shuai Shao,Yiming Zeng,Yu Zhao,Tingting Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出HyperFL，一种查询自适应的表示学习框架，通过超网络为查询编码器生成LoRA参数，在故障定位中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于检索的故障定位方法对所有问题报告使用固定的查询表示，未能适应真实世界问题报告在长度、结构和调试信息上的多样性，限制了定位性能。

Method: HyperFL采用轻量级超网络，根据输入查询动态生成查询编码器的LoRA参数，实现查询自适应编码，同时保持代码编码器固定并复用。

Result: 在真实基准上，HyperFL在多种嵌入骨干上均提升检索性能，函数级MRR@10相对提升最高13.3%，Hit@1相对提升最高16.7%，优于SweRank。

Conclusion: HyperFL通过查询自适应表示有效提升了软件故障定位的准确性，并针对不同问题特征学习到不同的适应模式，验证了查询自适应方法的有效性。

中文摘要：软件故障定位识别导致所报告问题的代码位置，是迈向自动化调试和程序修复的基本步骤。最近的基于检索的方法通过在学习问题报告和源代码之间的共享嵌入空间，将故障定位表述为密集检索任务。然而，这些方法使用固定的查询表示对所有问题报告进行编码，尽管真实世界的问题报告在长度、结构和调试信息方面存在显著差异。为了解决这一限制，我们提出了HyperFL，一种用于软件故障定位的查询自适应表示学习框架。HyperFL采用轻量级超网络为查询编码器生成查询特定的LoRA参数，实现动态查询自适应，同时保持代码编码器固定且可重用。在真实世界问题定位基准上的实验表明，HyperFL在多个嵌入骨干上持续提高检索性能，在函数级MRR@10上相对改进高达13.3%，在Hit@1上相对改进高达16.7%，超过了最先进的方法SweRank。进一步分析表明，HyperFL针对不同的问题特征学习了不同的自适应模式，突显了查询自适应表示对软件问题定位的有效性。

Original abstract: Software fault localization identifies the code locations responsible for reported issues and is a fundamental step toward automated debugging and program repair. Recent retrieval-based approaches formulate fault localization as a dense retrieval task by learning a shared embedding space between issue reports and source code. However, these methods encode all issue reports using a fixed query representation, despite the substantial diversity of real-world issue reports in length, structure, and debugging information. To address this limitation, we propose HyperFL, a query-adaptive representation learning framework for software fault localization. HyperFL employs a lightweight hypernetwork to generate query-specific LoRA parameters for the query encoder, enabling dynamic query adaptation while keeping the code encoder fixed and reusable. Experiments on a real-world issue localization benchmark demonstrate that HyperFL consistently improves retrieval performance across multiple embedding backbones, achieving up to 13.3% relative improvement in function-level MRR@10 and 16.7% relative improvement in Hit@1 over the state-of-the-art method SweRank. Further analysis shows that HyperFL learns distinct adaptation patterns for different issue characteristics, highlighting the effectiveness of query-adaptive representations for software issue localization.

</details>


### [71] [ConFL: Explainable Concurrent Fault Localization via Hierarchy-Guided LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02974)
*Shuai Shao,Dingbang Wang,Yiming Zeng,Tingting Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出ConFL，一种利用结构化并发知识增强LLM推理的可解释并发故障定位框架，通过构建并发知识库和交互级DSL，显著提升定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM方法在并发错误定位中因信息不完整、误导性提及和复杂跨线程交互，导致推理不稳定且可解释性差。

Method: 构建并发知识库（CKB），采用LLM引导的层次化检索，从组件到交互级并发上下文逐步缩小搜索空间，并使用交互级DSL显式编码跨线程交互。

Result: 在八个大型Java项目的真实并发错误上，MRR达0.503，MAP达0.486，优于SOTA基线，且对噪声、未见错误和不同LLM骨干具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ConFL通过结构化并发知识增强LLM，实现了高效、可解释且鲁棒的并发故障定位。

中文摘要：仅从错误报告定位并发错误具有挑战性，原因在于信息不完整、误导性的程序实体提及以及复杂的跨线程交互，导致现有的基于LLM的方法存在推理不稳定和可解释性有限的问题。我们提出了ConFL，一个可解释的并发故障定位框架，通过结构化并发知识增强LLM推理。ConFL从源代码构建并发知识库（CKB），并执行LLM引导的层次化检索，逐步将搜索空间从组件缩小到交互级并发上下文。一种交互级DSL显式编码了共享资源上的跨线程交互，使得无需遍历深层调用链即可进行聚焦推理。在来自八个大规模Java项目的真实并发错误上的实验表明，ConFL显著优于最先进的基于IR和基于LLM的基线，实现了0.503的MRR和0.486的MAP，同时对噪声错误报告、未见错误和不同LLM骨干保持鲁棒性。

Original abstract: Localizing concurrent bugs from bug reports alone is challenging due to incomplete information, misleading program-entity mentions, and complex cross-thread interactions, causing existing LLM-based approaches to suffer from unstable reasoning and limited explainability. We propose ConFL, an explainable concurrent fault localization framework that augments LLM reasoning with structured concurrency knowledge. ConFL constructs a Concurrent Knowledge Base (CKB) from source code and performs LLM-guided hierarchical retrieval to progressively narrow the search space from components to interaction-level concurrency contexts. An interaction-level DSL explicitly encodes cross-thread interactions over shared resources, enabling focused reasoning without traversing deep call chains. Experiments on real-world concurrent bugs from eight large-scale Java projects show that ConFL significantly outperforms state-of-the-art IR-based and LLM-based baselines, achieving an MRR of 0.503 and a MAP of 0.486, while remaining robust to noisy bug reports, unseen bugs, and different LLM backbones.

</details>


### [72] [The Ground Is Shifting: A Reflection on the Foundations of Software Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03007)
*Thomas Bock,Audris Mockus,Bogdan Vasilescu*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文指出AI代理使用版本控制等工具破坏了软件度量数据基于人类行为的假设，并提出AI辅助复现计划以保持度量研究的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理生成的数据痕迹不符合传统假设，导致以往研究结论可能失效，需立即应对。

Method: 提出系统性AI辅助复现计划，利用现代技术重新验证关键发现，并开发适应新情况的方法。

Result: 该计划旨在识别假设被违反的情况，并在当前数据上获得一致的研究结果。

Conclusion: 软件度量研究需要立即适应AI代理带来的数据变化，否则其相关性将受损；通过AI辅助复现可延续其价值。

中文摘要：在过去六十年的大部分时间里，软件度量依赖于劳动密集型的手工专有数据收集，这阻碍了进展。转而利用版本控制及相关工具中的痕迹极大扩展了数据可用性——尤其是在开源软件兴起的背景下——但这一转向依赖于一个往往未明确说明的假设：这些工具是由专业开发人员用来构建真正的软件系统的。然而，随着产生痕迹的工具、数据类型和规模以及实证方法都发生了演变，越来越清楚的是，数据生成和分析方法的变化影响了许多先前关于软件开发、维护和演化的发现。现在，AI代理也在积极使用这些相同的工具，由此产生的痕迹经常违反最初的人类来源假设。为了保持软件度量研究的相关性，需要立即采取行动：我们必须检测当代数据中基础假设何时被违反，并开发在变化环境下仍然有效的新方法论。为此，我们提出了一个系统的AI辅助复现计划，利用现代技术重新审视关键发现，旨在使方法在当前数据上产生一致的结果，以保持软件度量的意义。

Original abstract: For most of the past six decades, software measurement relied on labor-intensive manual collection of proprietary data, which hampered progress. The shift to repurposing traces from version control and related tools dramatically expanded data availability$\unicode{x2014}$especially with the rise of open-source software$\unicode{x2014}$but hinged on an often unstated assumption: that these tools are used by professional developers to build genuine software systems. However, as trace-generating tools, data types and scale, and empirical methods have all evolved, it has become clear that changes in data generation and analytical approaches affect many prior findings about software development, maintenance, and evolution. With AI agents now actively using these same tools, the resulting traces frequently violate the original assumption of human origin. To preserve the relevance of software measurement research, immediate action is needed: We must detect when foundational assumptions are violated in contemporary data and develop new methodologies that remain valid under changed circumstances. To this end, we propose a systematic AI-assisted replication program that revisits key findings using modern techniques, aiming for methods that yield consistent results on current data to keep software measurement meaningful.

</details>


### [73] [LLM Serving in the Wild: An Empirical Study of Frameworks, Methods, and System Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03036)
*Forough Majidi,Mohammad Mehdi Morovati,Foutse Khomh,Heng Li*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 对开源系统中LLM服务框架的采用进行了大规模实证研究，发现vLLM最流行，并行计算和内存管理等方法最常见，多框架使用较少，但组合框架可互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务效率是软件工程的关键问题，但现有研究多为框架和技术本身，缺乏对实际采用情况的了解。

Method: 分析五个LLM专用框架（vLLM、SGLang、TensorRT-LLM、LMDeploy、FlashInfer）在开源仓库中的使用，研究单独/组合采用、按LLM类别差异及仓库级特征。

Result: vLLM在流行度和采用上居首；并行计算、内存管理、网络剪枝是最常用方法；多框架使用有限，但组合可互补；采用因模型家族、模态、大小等而异；支持RL推理、多模态、微服务和云架构。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了LLM服务框架实践的实证表征，为研究者、维护者和从业者提供了洞察，表明当前采用以单一框架为主，组合使用有潜力。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）被集成到软件系统和AI服务中，使得高效的LLM服务成为软件工程关注的问题。服务LLM具有挑战性，因为推理需要计算、内存、GPU资源和执行，同时还要保持延迟和吞吐量。尽管先前的研究已经提出了LLM推理、优化和服务技术与框架，但关于它们在实际中如何被采用却知之甚少。在本研究中，我们调查了开源软件系统中LLM服务框架和服务方法的使用情况。我们识别并分析了五个LLM专用框架：vLLM、SGLang、TensorRT-LLM、LMDeploy和FlashInfer。我们研究了这些框架和技术如何单独或组合使用，采用如何随LLM类别变化，以及仓库在意图、焦点、用例和架构设计上如何不同。我们的结果表明，vLLM在流行度和采用率上是最显眼的框架，而并行计算、内存管理和网络剪枝是最常用的服务方法类别。多框架使用有限，表明开发者依赖单一服务框架；然而，组合框架连接了服务栈中的互补能力。框架采用随模型家族、模态、模型大小、领域专业化和部署设置而变化。仓库级分析表明，LLM服务框架支持各种应用和架构，包括基于强化学习（RL）的推理、多模态生成与理解、微服务和云基础设施。总体而言，本研究对LLM服务框架在实践中采用进行了大规模经验性描述，并为研究人员、框架维护者和从事LLM系统的从业者提供了见解。

Original abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are integrated into software systems and AI services, making efficient LLM serving a concern for software engineering. Serving LLMs is challenging because inference requires computation, memory, GPU resources, and execution while maintaining latency and throughput. Although prior research has proposed LLM inference, optimization, and serving techniques and frameworks, little is known about how they are adopted in practice. In this study, we investigate the use of LLM serving frameworks and serving methods in open-source software systems. We identify and analyze five LLM-specific frameworks: vLLM, SGLang, TensorRT-LLM, LMDeploy, and FlashInfer. We examine how these frameworks and techniques are adopted individually and in combination, how adoption varies across categories of LLMs, and how repositories differ in intent, focus, use case, and architectural design. Our results show that vLLM is the most visible framework in popularity and adoption, while parallel computation, memory management, and network pruning are the most frequently used serving-method categories. Multi-framework usage is limited, suggesting that developers rely on a single serving framework; however, combined frameworks connect complementary capabilities across the serving stack. Framework adoption varies across model families, modalities, model sizes, domain specializations, and deployment settings. Repository-level analysis shows that LLM serving frameworks support applications and architectures, including Reinforcement Learning (RL)-based reasoning, multimodal generation and understanding, microservices, and cloud infrastructure. Overall, this study provides a large-scale empirical characterization of LLM serving framework adoption in practice and offers insights for researchers, framework maintainers, and practitioners working on LLM systems.

</details>


### [74] [Integration Barriers in Open-Source SSI Frameworks: An Exploratory Developer Experience Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03039)
*Breno Cerqueira Reis Nakamura,Arlindo Flavio da Conceição*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文通过探索性探针研究发现SSI开源工具存在抽象鸿沟：被动操作成熟但主动构建（如模式定制）有架构摩擦，并提出三种架构转变以改善开发者体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSI承诺去中心化数字身份，但集成复杂性和工具不成熟阻碍广泛采用，需要系统研究开发者遇到的集成障碍。

Method: 采用探索性探针研究，让9名熟悉去中心化身份概念的开发者使用Walt.id、Traction和MetaMask完成核心凭证生命周期任务，通过定性主题分析和任务难度评级识别障碍。

Result: 发现关键抽象鸿沟：被动操作成熟，主动构建任务（特别是模式定制）存在重大架构摩擦，根源在于API抽象不足、环境配置脆弱和文档滞后。

Conclusion: 提出三个架构转变：基于Web的沙箱、AI辅助模式生成器和可执行文档策略，以解决SSI生态系统的集成障碍。

中文摘要：自主身份（SSI）承诺去中心化数字身份，但广泛采用仍受到集成复杂性和工具成熟度不足的阻碍。本文通过一项探索性探针研究，调查了开源SSI工具的开发体验（DX）。九名具有去中心化身份概念先验知识的开发者，代表早期集成者，使用Walt.id、Traction和MetaMask尝试完成核心凭证生命周期任务。我们的目标是通过对开放式开发者报告的定性主题分析，辅以任务级难度评级，来揭示重复出现的集成障碍。我们的研究结果揭示了一个关键的抽象鸿沟：虽然被动操作（如凭证接收）相对成熟，但主动构建任务，特别是模式定制，暴露了显著的架构摩擦。我们发现这些障碍源于API抽象不足、脆弱的环境配置，以及未能跟上生态系统快速演变的文档。这些问题反映了当前框架中的结构性设计决策。本研究描述了当前SSI开源生态系统中结构性集成障碍的特征。为了解决已识别的差距，我们为开发者工具提出了三个架构转变：基于Web的沙箱、AI辅助模式生成器和可执行文档策略。

Original abstract: Self-Sovereign Identity (SSI) promises to decentralize digital identity, but widespread adoption remains hindered by integration complexity and tooling immaturity. This paper investigates the Developer Experience (DX) of open-source SSI tooling through an exploratory probe study. Nine developers with prior knowledge of decentralized identity concepts, representing early integrators building SSI applications, attempted core credential lifecycle tasks using Walt.id, Traction, and MetaMask. Our goal was to surface recurring integration barriers through qualitative thematic analysis of open-ended developer reports, complemented by task-level difficulty ratings. Our findings reveal a critical abstraction gap: while passive operations like credential receipt are relatively mature, active construction tasks, particularly schema customization, expose significant architectural friction. We identify that these barriers stem from inadequate API abstractions, brittle environment configurations, and documentation that fails to track the ecosystem's rapid evolution. These issues reflect structural design decisions in current frameworks. This study characterizes the structural integration barriers in the current SSI open-source ecosystem. To address the identified gaps, we propose three architectural shifts for developer tooling: web-based sandboxes, AI-assisted schema generators, and executable documentation strategies.

</details>


### [75] [Efficient Grammar-Constrained Decoding via Parser Stack Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03065)
*Yongmin Li,Yihong Dong,Jia Li,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出PSC，一种语法约束解码方法，通过预计算将掩码计算复杂度与词汇表大小解耦，显著加速LLM的结构化输出生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GCD方法在线计算开销大，延迟随词汇表大小线性增长，制约了大词汇表LLM的吞吐量。

Method: PSC在预处理阶段将所有token的接受条件合并为解析器栈的分类器，解码时每次仅检查一次栈，即可得到完整掩码，时间与词汇表大小无关。

Result: 在复杂文法上掩码计算快达700倍，JSON上快达30倍；端到端吞吐量接近无约束解码，并提供预处理开销平衡点分析。

Conclusion: PSC有效消除词汇表大小对GCD的性能瓶颈，使约束解码接近无约束速度，并辅助用户权衡预处理成本。

中文摘要：LLM被广泛用于生成结构化输出，如源代码或JSON。语法约束解码（GCD）通过屏蔽违反上下文无关文法规则的token，可以保证生成输出的语法有效性。然而，现有GCD方法的在线计算开销（其延迟通常随词汇表大小线性扩展）限制了LLM的吞吐量，尤其是对于具有大词汇表的模型。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了PSC，一种新颖的语法约束解码方法。通过在预处理阶段将所有词汇token的接受条件合并为解析器栈（parser stack）的单一分类器，PSC可以在每个解码步骤仅检查一次解析器栈，从而计算出完整的词汇表掩码，时间复杂度与词汇表大小无关。实验表明，在复杂的编程语言文法上，PSC计算掩码的速度比基线快高达700倍，对于符合模式的JSON则快高达30倍；使用PSC的端到端LLM吞吐量接近无约束解码。我们分析了预处理提供者和解码用户的预处理开销，并提供了盈亏平衡点分析，以帮助用户决定是否自行进行预处理。

Original abstract: LLMs are widely used to generate structured output like source code or JSON. Grammar-constrained decoding (GCD) can guarantee the syntactic validity of the generated output, by masking out tokens that violate rules specified by a context-free grammar. However, the online computational overhead of existing GCD methods, with latency typically scaling linearly with vocabulary size, limits the throughput of LLMs, especially for models with large vocabularies. To address this issue, we propose PSC, a novel grammar-constrained decoding method. By combining acceptance conditions of all vocabulary tokens into a single classifier of the parser stack during preprocessing, PSC can compute the complete vocabulary mask by checking the parser stack exactly once per decoding step, with time complexity independent of the vocabulary size. Experiments show that PSC computes masks up to 700$\times$ faster than baselines on complex programming language grammars, and up to 30$\times$ faster for schema-conformant JSON; end-to-end LLM throughput with PSC approaches that of unconstrained decoding. We analyze the preprocessing overhead for preprocessing providers and decoding users, and provide a break-even point analysis to help users decide whether to do preprocessing by themselves.

</details>


### [76] [Cross-Ecosystem Bug Classification in Quantum Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03173)
*Mir Mohammad Yousuf,Shabir Ahmad Sofi,Bisma Majid*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 首个跨生态系统的量子软件缺陷比较研究，基于规则框架分析 12,910 个 Qiskit 和 4,613 个其他存储库问题，发现经典缺陷占 67%，量子特定缺陷占 27-30%，且规则方法优于机器学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件中经典与量子组件的交互导致复杂且难理解的缺陷模式，系统表征这些缺陷是提升测试、调试与质量保证的关键前提，但缺乏跨生态系统的比较研究。

Method: 收集 Qiskit（12,910 个问题）和 11 个其他存储库（4,613 个问题，包括 Cirq、PyQuil），采用基于规则的分类框架，按缺陷类型、类别、严重性、质量属性和量子特定子类型进行分析，并用统计验证及四种监督式机器学习基线进行对比评估。

Result: 经典缺陷占主导（67%），量子特定缺陷占 27-30%；Qiskit 兼容性缺陷多，其他生态系统语法和量子特定缺陷率高；门和电路问题是最常见量子特定类型，非 Qiskit 项目多样性更强；规则框架在细粒度量子子类型上优于机器学习；纵向分析显示量子特定缺陷随时间稳定（2017-2025）。

Conclusion: 该研究提供首个量子软件缺陷分布跨生态系比较，证明可解释、自动化就绪的规则框架能有效指导测试、调试和质量保证，且优于数据驱动模型。

中文摘要：量子软件工程面临着由于经典组件与量子组件交互而产生的独特挑战，这些交互导致了复杂且往往难以理解的缺陷模式。对这些缺陷进行表征对于推进量子生态系统中的测试、调试和质量保证至关重要。本研究对来自 Qiskit 的 12,910 个问题以及来自包括 Cirq 和 PyQuil 在内的 11 个额外存储库的 4,613 个问题进行了比较研究。我们使用基于规则的分类框架，按类型、类别、严重性、质量属性和量子特定子类型对缺陷进行分析。结果表明，经典缺陷在各生态系统中始终占主导地位（67%），而量子特定缺陷占比为 27-30%。生态系统特定的趋势浮现：Qiskit 存储库表现出更多的兼容性相关缺陷，而其他生态系统则表现出更高的语法和量子特定缺陷率。在两个生态系统中，门和电路问题主导了量子特定缺陷，但非 Qiskit 项目揭示了更广泛的多样性，包括算法、资源和混合接口问题。统计验证确认该框架在缺陷类型层面具有泛化性，同时在更细粒度层面检测到显著差异。与四种监督式机器学习基线的基准测试进一步表明，基于规则的框架始终优于数据驱动模型，尤其是在细粒度的量子特定子类型上，而纵向分析（2017-2025）表明量子特定缺陷随时间保持相对稳定，而非呈现稳定增长。本研究提供了量子软件中缺陷分布的首次跨生态系统比较，展示了可解释、自动化就绪且基于规则的框架在指导测试、调试和质量保证方面的实用性。

Original abstract: Quantum software engineering faces unique challenges due to the interaction of classical and quantum components, which produce complex and often poorly understood bug patterns. Characterizing these bugs is essential for advancing testing, debugging, and quality assurance in quantum ecosystems. This paper presents a comparative study of 12,910 issues from Qiskit and 4,613 issues from 11 additional repositories, including Cirq and PyQuil. Using a rule-based classification framework, we analyze bugs by type, category, severity, quality attributes, and quantum-specific subtypes. Results show that classical bugs consistently dominate (67%) across ecosystems, while quantum-specific bugs account for 27-30%. Ecosystem-specific trends emerge: Qiskit repositories exhibit more compatibility related bugs, whereas other ecosystems show higher syntax and quantum-specific bug rates. Across both ecosystems, gate and circuit issues dominate quantum-specific bugs, though non-Qiskit projects reveal broader diversity, including algorithmic, resource, and hybrid-interface issues. Statistical validation confirms that the framework generalizes at the bug-type level while detecting significant variations at finer levels. Benchmarking against four supervised machine-learning baselines further shows that the rule-based framework consistently outperforms data-driven models, particularly for fine-grained quantum-specific subtypes, while longitudinal analysis (2017-2025) indicates that quantum- specific bugs remain relatively stable over time rather than exhibiting a steady increase. This study provides the first cross- ecosystem comparison of bug distributions in quantum software, demonstrating the utility of an interpretable, automation-ready, rule-based framework for guiding testing, debugging, and quality assurance.

</details>


### [77] [Fail-Fast, Restart-Smart: Early Failure Prediction and Restart for SWE Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03222)
*Chenyu Wang,Yunbo Lyu,Junda He,Zhou Yang,Chenxing Zhong,Yaniv Harel,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出FailFast-RestartSmart，一个两阶段控制器：轻量监控器提前预测失败并触发同策略重启，在SWE-bench Verified上节省14.6%-20.4%执行令牌，并将解决率从66.6%提升至71.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程智能体执行轨迹长且成本高，失败轨迹常包含冗余探索，但提前终止可能中断成功轨迹，且失败轨迹中可能存在有用的编辑。因此需要一种方法在保留有用信息的同时，尽早识别并恢复失败轨迹。

Method: 两阶段控制器。FailFast：0.6B监控器，用终端和密集失败到通过监督训练，仅从前缀预测失败；RestartSmart：报警后启动无历史的新同策略轨迹，并将被打断的差异作为可选的叠加层供智能体使用。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，监控器跨策略迁移有效，在目标5%假阳性率下节省14.6%-20.4%执行令牌；在Qwen3.6-27B上节省20.4%，优于AgentStop的12.5%。在25%假阳性率下，RestartSmart将解决率从66.6%提升至71.8%，而冷重启仅66.8%。

Conclusion: 实验支持提前终止与顺序同策略恢复相结合的有效性，表明该方案能在节省计算资源的同时提升任务解决率。

中文摘要：软件工程（SWE）智能体通过长时间的执行轨迹来解决仓库级问题，随着上下文积累，这些轨迹变得越来越昂贵。失败的运行往往更长，并表现出冗余探索或循环，这表明某些失败可能在完成之前就可以被检测到。然而，提前终止可能会中断原本会成功的轨迹；相反，不成功的轨迹仍可能包含有用的仓库编辑。我们提出了FailFast-RestartSmart，一个用于单条活动轨迹的两阶段控制器。FailFast是一个轻量级的0.6B监控器，使用终端和密集的失败到通过监督进行训练，以从可观察的前缀预测失败，而无需策略对数概率或隐藏状态。一旦发出警报，RestartSmart会启动一条没有先前提示历史的新的同策略轨迹，并将被打断的仓库差异作为可选叠加层提供，智能体可以检查、应用或丢弃该叠加层。在SWE-bench Verified上，仅使用Qwen3.6-27B轨迹训练的监控器可以迁移到其他三个策略（包括一个闭源API模型），并在目标5%假阳性率下节省14.6%-20.4%的执行令牌；在Qwen3.6-27B上，其20.4%的节省超过了我们逐步AgentStop适应达到的12.5%。在目标25%假阳性率下，RestartSmart将Qwen3.6-27B的解决率从66.6%提升至71.8%，而冷重启仅达到66.8%。这些结果共同支持了提前终止与顺序同策略恢复。

Original abstract: Software engineering (SWE) agents resolve repository-level issues through long trajectories that grow increasingly expensive as context accumulates. Failed runs tend to be longer and exhibit redundant exploration or looping, suggesting that some failures may be detectable before completion. Early termination, however, risks interrupting trajectories that would otherwise succeed; conversely, an unsuccessful trajectory may still contain useful repository edits. We present FailFast-RestartSmart, a two-stage controller for a single active trajectory. FailFast is a lightweight 0.6B monitor trained with terminal and dense fail-to-pass supervision to predict failure from observable prefixes without policy logits or hidden states. Upon an alarm, RestartSmart launches a fresh same-policy rollout without prior prompt history and offers the interrupted repository diff as an optional overlay that the agent may inspect, apply, or discard. On SWE-bench Verified, a monitor trained solely on Qwen3.6-27B trajectories transfers to three other policies, including a closed-API model, and saves 14.6%-20.4% of execution tokens at a target 5% false-positive rate; on Qwen3.6-27B, its 20.4% saving exceeds the 12.5% achieved by our per-step AgentStop adaptation. At a target 25% false-positive rate, RestartSmart raises Qwen3.6-27B resolution from 66.6% to 71.8%, whereas cold restart reaches only 66.8%. Together, these results support early termination with sequential same-policy recovery.

</details>


### [78] [NotDec: WebAssembly Decompilation With Inter-Procedural Type Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03286)
*Jikai Wang,Ningyu He,Tianming Liu,Junhai Wang,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: NotDec是一个先进的WebAssembly反编译框架，通过改进类型恢复和结构化控制流分析，实现了高重编译成功率和类型恢复准确性，显著优于Ghidra等现有工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebAssembly作为通用字节码标准被广泛采用，但其二进制行为难以理解，现有反编译工具存在结果冗长、可读性差和类型恢复局限的问题，亟需更好的反编译方案来辅助安全审计。

Method: NotDec扩展WebAssembly类型检查算法，将字节码提升为SSA-IR；应用过程间类型恢复算法Retypd，结合指针和数值区分方法恢复复杂数据结构；利用内存SSA和保持语义的结构化控制流分析生成可读且语义一致的C代码。

Result: NotDec在5,241个Juliet样本和所有Howard数据集上达到100%重新编译成功率（Ghidra为45.95%）；真实程序中恢复85.33%结构体成员访问（Ghidra为9.24%）；过程内版本NotDec_F内存消耗低于Ghidra一半，未优化二进制执行时间最多减少97%。

Conclusion: NotDec在WebAssembly反编译的重编译成功率和类型恢复准确性上大幅领先现有工具，且其过程内变体在效率上具有显著优势，为WebAssembly安全分析提供了有力支持；但完整过程间版本仍需应对大型二进制的可扩展性挑战。

中文摘要：随着WebAssembly在浏览器、容器、物联网设备和服务端无服务器平台中得到广泛支持，并日益被采纳为通用的底层字节码标准，审计其隐藏漏洞和恶意意图变得至关重要。反编译现有的WebAssembly模块可以帮助安全研究人员和最终用户理解二进制行为，但当前工具存在结果冗长、可读性差和类型恢复有限的问题。我们提出了NotDec，一个先进的WebAssembly反编译框架。NotDec扩展了WebAssembly类型检查算法，将字节码提升为基于SSA的中间表示（IR），应用过程间类型恢复算法Retypd，并结合指针和数值区分方法来恢复复杂数据结构，同时利用内存SSA和保持语义的结构化控制流分析来生成可读且语义一致的C代码。NotDec在所有5,241个Juliet样本和所有Howard数据集程序上实现了100%的重新编译成功率，显著优于包括Ghidra（45.95%成功率）在内的基线。在类型恢复准确性方面，NotDec在真实世界程序中恢复了85.33%的结构体成员访问，远超Ghidra的9.24%。虽然完整的过程间版本在大型二进制文件上面临可扩展性挑战，但过程内变体NotDec_F展现出卓越的效率，其内存消耗不到Ghidra的一半，在未优化二进制文件上执行时间最多减少97%。

Original abstract: With WebAssembly widely supported in browsers, containers, IoT devices, and serverless platforms and increasingly adopted as a universal low-level bytecode standard, auditing its hidden vulnerabilities and malicious intentions has become critical. Decompiling existing WebAssembly modules can help security researchers and end users understand binary behavior, but current tools suffer from verbose result, poor readability, and limited type recovery.
  We present NotDec, an advanced WebAssembly decompilation framework. NotDec extends the WebAssembly type checking algorithm to lift bytecode into an SSA-based IR, applies the inter-procedural type recovery algorithm Retypd with pointer and numeric value differentiation methods to recover complex data structures, and leverages Memory SSA alongside semantics-preserving structured control-flow analysis to emit readable, semantically consistent C code.
  NotDec achieves 100% recompilation success rate on all 5,241 Juliet samples and all Howard dataset programs, significantly outperforming baselines including Ghidra (45.95% success rate). On type recovery accuracy, NotDec recovers 85.33% of struct member accesses in real-world programs, vastly exceeding Ghidra's 9.24%. While the full inter-procedural version faces scalability challenges on large binaries, the intra-procedural variant NotDec_F demonstrates superior efficiency, consuming less than half of Ghidra's memory and up to 97% less execution time on unoptimized binaries.

</details>


### [79] [Evaluating LLM Trade-offs for Enterprise Automation: Lessons from Workflow Generation in a Production Enterprise Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03311)
*Xavier Wrenn,Radoslav Raykov,Aleksandar Angelov,Hirokuni Kitahara,Yuji Watanabe,Anca Sailer*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 通过逐段分解生成工作流，将结构成功率从最高82.8%提升至最高97.8%，并让较小模型在生产中可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业合规管理需要快速适应不断变化的监管要求，而传统静态编排器无法在混合云环境中处理事件驱动的动态工作流，因此需要AI驱动的自动化生成方案。

Method: 评估六个大语言模型，对比单体生成与逐段分解（变量脚手架、基础块组装、嵌套块生成）两种流水线，在29个真实场景中进行了2784次运行。

Result: 逐段流水线将结构成功率从31.5-82.8%提升至74.1-97.8%；mistral-small以95.7%成功率和0.01美元成本达到生产级，而最高性能模型成本高19倍。

Conclusion: 结构有效性与语义正确性需分离，逐段分解能降低对昂贵模型的依赖，实现模型无关且可扩展的云工程自动化。

中文摘要：企业合规管理需要快速适应不断演变的监管框架（例如DORA、AI RMF、FedRAMP）以及严格的修复SLA。传统的静态编排器在混合云环境中往往失败，因为事件驱动的评估要求自动化代码在数秒内适应运行时上下文。本文介绍了在生产级企业平台中评估六个大型语言模型用于AI驱动工作流生成的经验教训，基于29个真实场景、两种生成流水线架构、每个提示-模型-流水线配置八次独立运行（共2784次运行）进行了基准测试。我们最初的流水线使用单体工作流生成，实现了31.5-82.8%的结构成功率（JSON模式有效性和正确的UI渲染），大多数模型在复杂JSON生成上存在困难。我们开发了一种重新设计的逐段流水线，将工作流构建分解为变量脚手架、基础块组装和嵌套块生成，将所有模型的结构成功率提高到74.1-97.8%。我们分析了生产权衡，包括成本（每个工作流0.008-0.20美元）、延迟（交互使用低于50秒）和模型选择。逐段分解使较小的模型（例如mistral-small，结构成功率95.7%，每个工作流0.01美元）能够达到生产可行性，摆脱了对昂贵前沿模型的依赖。虽然mistral-medium-2505和gpt-oss-120b达到了最高的结构成功率（96.1%和97.8%），但mistral-medium-2505相比mistral-small有19倍的成本溢价。我们的部署经验强调了将结构有效性与语义正确性（用户意图的逻辑实现）区分开来的必要性，并为云工程中模型无关、可扩展的自动化提供了解决方案。

Original abstract: Enterprise compliance management requires rapid adaptation to evolving regulatory frameworks (e.g., DORA, AI RMF, FedRAMP) and tight remediation SLAs. Traditional static orchestrators often fail in hybrid cloud environments where event-driven assessments demand that automation code adapt to runtime context in seconds. This paper presents lessons learned from evaluating six large language models for AI-driven workflow generation in a production enterprise platform, benchmarked across 29 real-world IT automation scenarios, two generation pipeline architectures, and eight independent runs per prompt-model-pipeline configuration (2,784 runs total).
  Our initial pipeline used monolithic workflow generation, achieving 31.5-82.8% structural success rates (JSON schema validity and correct UI rendering), with most models struggling on complex JSON generation. We developed a redesigned piecewise pipeline that decomposes workflow construction into variable scaffolding, base block assembly, and nested block generation, raising structural success to 74.1-97.8% across all models.
  We analyze production tradeoffs including cost (USD 0.008-0.20 per workflow), latency (under 50s for interactive use), and model selection. Piecewise decomposition enables smaller models (e.g., mistral-small at 95.7% structural success and USD 0.01 per workflow) to reach production viability, removing dependency on expensive frontier models. While mistral-medium-2505 and gpt-oss-120b achieved the highest structural success (96.1% and 97.8%), mistral-medium-2505 carries a 19x cost premium versus mistral-small. Our deployment lessons highlight the need to separate structural validity from semantic correctness (logical fulfillment of user intent) and provide a solution for model-agnostic, scalable automation in cloud engineering.

</details>


### [80] [Making AI Visible, Not Vanished: How AI Policies Reshape Developer Experience on GitHub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03329)
*Yunqi Chen,Thomas Zimmermann,Bianca Trinkenreich*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 首个大规模实证研究基于29,624个GitHub仓库和385个采用AI政策的项目，提出TRACE框架，发现AI治理以规范为主，强调透明度与责任的政策效果更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI正在改变开源开发，但缺乏对AI治理政策的系统理解及其对开发者体验的影响。

Method: 分析29,624个GitHub仓库，识别385个采用AI政策的项目，提出TRACE框架，使用倾向性评分匹配和双重差分分析评估政策效果。

Result: AI治理主要规范而非禁止AI辅助开发；政策采纳提升维护者参与度、AI披露、审查互动和代码质量；强调透明度与责任的政策优于限制性政策。

Conclusion: 不同AI治理策略会塑造开发者体验，研究为OSS社区设计有效AI政策提供了实证依据。

中文摘要：生成式人工智能正在迅速重塑开源软件（OSS）开发，促使项目引入管理AI辅助贡献的政策。然而，关于这些政策如何不同或是否影响开发者体验，我们知之甚少。我们首次对OSS中的AI治理政策进行了大规模实证研究。通过分析29,624个GitHub仓库，我们识别出385个采用AI政策的项目，并推导出TRACE框架，该框架涵盖五个治理维度：透明度、责任、归属、约束和执行。我们进一步将政策分为五个治理家族，并使用倾向性评分匹配和纵向双重差分分析来估计其效果。结果表明，AI治理主要是规范而非禁止AI辅助开发。政策采纳带来了维护者参与度的提高、AI披露的增加、更丰富的审查互动以及代码质量的提升，同时AI辅助贡献持续增长。治理设计很重要：强调透明度和责任的政策比单纯限制性方法产生了更强的社区和质量成果。我们的研究结果展示了不同的AI治理策略如何塑造开发者体验，并为帮助OSS社区设计有效的AI政策提供了证据。

Original abstract: Generative AI is rapidly reshaping Open Source Software (OSS) software development,prompting projects to introduce policies governing AI-assisted contributions. However, little is known about how these policies differ or whether they influence developer experience. We present the first large-scale empirical study of AI governance policies in OSS. Analyzing 29,624 GitHub repositories, we identify 385 projects that adopted AI policies and derive TRACE, a framework capturing five governance dimensions: Transparency, Responsibility, Attribution, Constraints, and Enforcement. We further classify policies into five governance families and estimate their effects using propensity-score matching and longitudinal difference-in-differences analysis. Our results show that AI governance primarily regulates rather than prohibits AI-assisted development. Policy adoption brings maintainer engagement, increased AI disclosure, richer review interactions, and improved code quality while AI-assisted contributions continue to grow. Governance design matters: policies emphasizing transparency and responsibility produced stronger community and quality outcomes than restrictive approaches alone. Our findings show how different AI governance strategies shape developer experience and provide evidence to help OSS communities design effective AI policies.

</details>


### [81] [Assessing Behavioral Validation in UI Component Test Suites Using Inferred Metamorphic Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03337)
*Yu Pei,Cunming Zhang,Jeongju Sohn,Mike Papadakis*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出基于蜕变关系的MR覆盖率指标，用于评估UI组件测试套件的行为验证程度，补充传统执行覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统执行指标（语句/分支覆盖率）无法反映测试是否验证了组件API和文档隐含的行为关系，导致测试质量评估不完整。

Method: 利用UI特定分类法从源码、文档和测试中推断蜕变关系，通过混合确定性分析和语义分析将测试与关系对齐，计算关系级MR覆盖率指标。

Result: MR Cover在42.5%-47.6%之间，低于MR Touch；未覆盖关系多为弱预言机案例；MR覆盖率能揭示语句/分支覆盖率无法体现的行为空白；问题描述可映射到MR类型，MR标签有助故障检测。

Conclusion: MR覆盖率提供了互补的关系级视角，能更全面评估UI组件测试的行为验证质量，具有实际应用价值。

中文摘要：UI组件库通常使用基于执行的指标（如语句覆盖率和分支覆盖率）进行评估，然而这些指标对于测试是否验证了组件API和文档所隐含的行为关系的洞察有限。本文提出了一个基于MR的框架，使用推断的蜕变关系（MRs）作为经验行为参考（而非完整规范）来评估UI组件测试套件。给定组件的源码、文档和测试，该框架使用UI特定分类法推断组件特定的MRs，通过混合确定性分析和语义分析将测试与推断的关系对齐，并计算关系级MR覆盖率指标。我们手动验证了推断的MR空间和测试-MR对齐。我们的评估表明，现有测试套件执行的行为关系数量远超其显式验证的数量：在三种LLM配置下，MR Cover保持在42.5%至47.6%之间，且始终低于MR Touch。大多数未覆盖的关系属于弱预言机情况，即行为被执行但缺乏显式行为验证。MR覆盖率还通过揭示语句或分支覆盖率无法反映的行为空白，补充了基于执行的覆盖率。我们进一步通过问题描述映射、预言机增强和MR相关注入故障评估实际相关性。大多数报告的问题描述可以映射到推断的MR关系类型；弱预言机关系往往暴露缺失的验证证据；MR标签在MR相关故障检测中显示出趋势。总体而言，MR覆盖率为现代UI组件测试中的行为验证评估提供了互补的关系级视角。

Original abstract: UI component libraries are commonly assessed using execution-based metrics such as statement and branch coverage, yet these metrics provide limited insight into whether tests verify the behavioral relations implied by component APIs and documentation. This paper presents an MR-based framework that uses inferred metamorphic relations (MRs) as an empirical behavioral reference, rather than a complete specification, for assessing UI component test suites. Given a component's source, documentation, and tests, the framework infers component-specific MRs using a UI-specific taxonomy, aligns tests with the inferred relations through hybrid deterministic and semantic analysis, and computes relation-level MR coverage metrics. We manually validate both the inferred MR space and the test--MR alignment. Our evaluation shows that existing test suites exercise substantially more behavioral relations than they explicitly validate: MR Cover remains between 42.5% and 47.6% across three LLM configurations and consistently below MR Touch. Most uncovered relations are weak-oracle cases, where behaviors are exercised but lack explicit behavioral validation. MR coverage also complements execution-based coverage by revealing behavioral gaps not reflected by statement or branch coverage alone. We further assess practical relevance through issue-description mapping, oracle strengthening, and MR-relevant injected faults. Most reported issue descriptions can be mapped to inferred MR relation types; weak-oracle relations often expose missing validation evidence; and MR labels show a trend in MR-relevant fault detection. Overall, MR coverage provides a complementary relation-level perspective for assessing behavioral validation in modern UI component testing.

</details>


### [82] [Route-Align-Verify for Functional Correctness in Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03341)
*Erxue Zhou,Jingxiang Meng,Aofan Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出RAV框架，通过路由、对齐、验证三阶段协同，在不改变骨干模型的情况下，显著提升代码生成的功能正确性，在MBPP上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码生成在面对异构任务时，单一提示策略和直接生成输出难以保证功能正确性；且微调与推理时的提示分布不一致，限制了模型性能。

Method: RAV框架包含三个阶段：1) Route：任务感知提示路由，为每个任务选择最佳提示；2) Align：使用对齐的LoRA适配来缩小微调与推理提示的分布差距；3) Verify：执行多个候选输出并通过公共测试筛选最终结果。

Result: 在MBPP Sanitized上达到0.8911，MBPP Full上达到0.8520，相比基础模型分别提升6.35和9.92个百分点；消融实验显示各组件协同有效，鲁棒性和污染分析验证了改进的可靠性。

Conclusion: 通过联合优化提示路由、模型适配和输出验证，可以在不修改骨干架构的前提下显著提升代码生成的功能正确性，为改进LLM代码生成提供了轻量且模块化的新思路。

中文摘要：大语言模型（LLMs）已显著改善了代码生成，但实现强功能正确性仍然困难，尤其是对于异构编程任务，单一提示策略和单一直接生成的输出往往不足。在本文中，我们提出了RAV，一个轻量级且模块化的框架，通过三个协调阶段改进固定骨干模型的代码生成：路由（Route），在生成前应用任务感知的提示路由；对齐（Align），通过对齐的LoRA适配减少微调提示与推理时提示之间的不匹配；验证（Verify），通过针对可见公共测试执行多个候选来选择最终输出。我们在MBPP基准的清洗（Sanitized）和完整（Full）设置下评估了RAV。完整的RAV流水线在所有评估配置中取得最佳性能，在MBPP Sanitized上达到0.8911，在MBPP Full上达到0.8520。与基础模型相比，这些结果分别提高了6.35和9.92个百分点。组件消融实验进一步表明，当与基于执行的验证结合时，任务感知路由和对齐适配变得更加有效。额外的鲁棒性和污染分析支持了观察到的改进的可靠性。总体而言，结果表明，在不修改骨干架构的情况下，通过联合优化任务提示方式、模型适配方式和最终输出选择方式，可以有意义地改进代码生成的功能正确性。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have substantially improved code generation, yet achieving strong functional correctness remains difficult, especially for heterogeneous programming tasks where a single prompting strategy and a single directly generated output are often insufficient. In this paper, we present RAV, a lightweight and modular framework that improves code generation with a fixed backbone model through three coordinated stages: Route, which applies task-aware prompt routing before generation; Align, which reduces the mismatch between fine-tuning prompts and inference-time prompts through aligned LoRA adaptation; and Verify, which selects the final output by executing multiple candidates against visible public tests.
  We evaluate RAV on the MBPP benchmark under both the sanitized and full settings. The complete RAV pipeline achieves the best performance among all evaluated configurations, reaching 0.8911 on MBPP Sanitized and 0.8520 on MBPP Full. Compared with the base model, these results represent improvements of 6.35 and 9.92 percentage points, respectively. Component-wise ablation experiments further show that task-aware routing and aligned adaptation become substantially more effective when combined with execution-based verification. Additional robustness and contamination analyses support the reliability of the observed improvements. Overall, the results indicate that functional correctness in code generation can be meaningfully improved without modifying the backbone architecture, by jointly optimizing how tasks are prompted, how the model is adapted, and how final outputs are selected.

</details>


### [83] [Self-Evolving Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03392)
*Hao Zhou,Haichuan Hu,Ye Shang,Quanjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文对自演变编码代理进行了系统性综述，定义了概念、提出了以对象为中心的 taxonomy，并总结了该领域的独特优势与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码代理在部署后保持静态，而软件开发是动态、反馈丰富的过程，这种矛盾催生了自演变编码代理的研究需求。

Method: 通过定义自演变编码代理、开发以对象为中心的 taxonomy，并补充演变时间和软件特定证据两个正交视角，系统综合已有工作。

Result: 发现可执行反馈、代码仓库级上下文和编码轨迹使软件工程成为自演变的自然领域，同时面临反馈可靠性、基准过拟合、安全性、可维护性、成本和泛化等挑战。

Conclusion: 该综述澄清了自演变编码代理的概念边界，并为设计更自适应、可靠且软件感知的代理系统提供了基础。

中文摘要：大型语言模型日益嵌入软件工程工作流程中，作为能够检查代码仓库、调用工具、执行测试、调试失败并生成补丁的编码代理。然而，大多数现有代理在部署后仍基本保持静态，尽管软件开发是一个动态的、反馈丰富的过程，其中代码仓库会演变、依赖关系会变化、测试会失败，而修复尝试会留下可复用的经验。这种张力促使了越来越多关于自演变编码代理的研究工作，在这种代理中，代理通过从先前的编码交互中更新其框架、记忆、技能、工具、模型或协作结构来改进其未来行为。在这篇综述中，我们对该新兴领域进行了系统的综合。我们首先定义自演变编码代理，并将其与常规编码代理和一般自演变代理区分开来。然后，我们开发了一个以对象为中心的 taxonomy（分类法），用于刻画这些系统中演变的内容，并用两个正交视角加以补充：演变发生的时间以及驱动演变的特定于软件的证据。在文献中，我们发现可执行反馈、代码仓库级上下文和编码轨迹使软件工程成为代理自演变的自然领域，具有独特性，但也带来了反馈可靠性、基准过拟合、安全性、可维护性、成本和泛化方面的新挑战。通过围绕这些维度组织现有工作，本综述旨在阐明自演变编码代理的概念边界，并为设计更具适应性、可靠性和软件感知能力的代理系统提供基础。我们收集的论文可在 https://github.com/zhouhao1024/Awesome-Self-Evolving-Coding-Agents 找到。

Original abstract: Large language models are increasingly embedded in software engineering workflows as coding agents that can inspect repositories, invoke tools, execute tests, debug failures, and generate patches. Yet most existing agents remain largely static after deployment, even though software development is a dynamic, feedback-rich process in which repositories evolve, dependencies change, tests fail, and repair attempts leave reusable experience. This tension has motivated a growing body of work on self-evolving coding agents, where the agent improves its future behavior by updating its framework, memory, skills, tools, models, or collaboration structures from prior coding interactions. In this survey, we provide a systematic synthesis of this emerging area. We first define self-evolving coding agents and distinguish them from conventional coding agents and general self-evolving agents. We then develop an object-centered taxonomy that characterizes what evolves in these systems, and complement it with two orthogonal perspectives: when evolution occurs and what software-specific evidence drives it. Across the literature, we find that executable feedback, repository-level context, and coding trajectories give software engineering a distinctive role as a natural domain for agent self-evolution, but also introduce new challenges in feedback reliability, benchmark overfitting, safety, maintainability, cost, and generalization. By organizing existing work around these dimensions, this survey aims to clarify the conceptual boundaries of self-evolving coding agents and provide a foundation for designing more adaptive, reliable, and software-aware agentic systems. The papers we collect can be found at https://github.com/zhouhao1024/Awesome-Self-Evolving-Coding-Agents.

</details>


### [84] [When AI Joins the Team! A Model of How AI Adoption Relates To Social Patterns in Software Engineering Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03462)
*Giusy Annunziata,Rudrajit Choudhuri,Anita Sarma,Gemma Catolino,Filomena Ferrucci*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 基于TMS理论，通过152名软件专业人士的调查数据，发现AI采用与社区异味的关系取决于工作类型：专业化工作中通过提升知识共享减少异味，协调工作中直接提升沟通质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助开发工具日益普及，但其对团队社交动态（如协调与沟通）的影响尚不明确，存在研究空白。

Method: 基于TMS理论，验证HumanAI和HumanHuman互动测量工具，构建五个PLS-SEM模型，使用152名软件专业人士的调查数据，并通过专家调查和因子分析验证社区异味构念。

Result: AI采用与社区异味的关系并非单一，而是通过机制依赖工作类型：专业化工作中，AI与更高知识共享互动相关，进而减少异味；协调工作中，AI直接提升沟通质量，补充而非替代人类互动。

Conclusion: 提供了实证验证的TMS模型，表明AI与社区异味的关系取决于协作类型，并贡献了可复用测量工具及研究实践启示。

中文摘要：背景：AI辅助开发工具的日益普及正在改变软件团队协作、知识共享和协调的方式，但其对团队社交动态的影响在很大程度上仍未得到探索。空白：目前尚不清楚AI的采用是否与社区异味（反映协调和沟通中断的社会技术反模式）的增加或减少相关，以及通过哪些机制。方法：基于Transactive Memory Systems（TMS）理论，我们验证了HumanAI和HumanHuman互动在两个TMS维度（专业化与协调）上的测量工具，并基于152名使用AI工具的软件专业人士的调查数据测试了五个PLS-SEM模型。社区异味构念源自文献，并通过专家调查和因子分析进行验证。结果：AI采用与社区异味并非以单一方式相关，而是通过取决于工作类型的机制相关。在专业化工作中，AI与更高的知识共享同伴互动相关，而后者又与更少的异味相关。在协调工作中，AI直接与更高的沟通质量相关，补充而非替代人类互动。贡献：我们提供了一个经过实证验证的、基于TMS的模型，表明AI与社区异味之间的关系取决于协作类型，并提供了可复用的测量工具以及基于证据的研究和实践启示。

Original abstract: Context: The growing adoption of AI-assisted development tools is changing how software teams collaborate, share knowledge, and coordinate, yet its consequences for team social dynamics remain largely unexplored. Gap: It is unclear whether AI adoption is associated with an increase or reduction in community smells,socio-technical anti-patterns reflecting coordination and communication breakdowns,and through which mechanisms. Method: Grounded in Transactive Memory Systems (TMS) theory, we validate instruments for HumanAI and HumanHuman interaction along two TMS dimensions, Specialization and Coordination, and test five PLS-SEM models on survey data from 152 software professionals using AI tools. Community smell constructs were derived from the literature and validated through expert surveys and factor analysis. Results: AI adoption relates to community smells not in a single way, but through mechanisms depending on the work. In specialization work, AI is associated with higher knowledge-sharing peer interaction, which is in turn associated with fewer smells. In coordination work, AI is directly associated with higher communication quality, complementing rather than replacing human interaction. Contributions: We provide an empirically validated, TMS-grounded model showing that the AIcommunity-smell relationship is contingent on the type of collaboration, with a reusable instrument and evidence-based implications for research and practice.

</details>


### [85] [Leveraging System-Level Observations to Inform Bayesian Learning of Model Parameters for Quantitative Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03489)
*Simos Gerasimou,Xingyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: EPIK是一种通过引出并嵌入先验知识来改进贝叶斯定量验证的方法，利用系统级可观察属性推导转移参数分布，从而提升验证准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯定量验证依赖先验知识，但不准确或信息不足的PK会导致错误的验证结果；且现有方法需要模型转移参数的PK，难以获取。

Method: EPIK利用系统级可直接观察且具有现实语义的属性，构建双重优化问题来推导未知转移参数的分布，并将这些分布嵌入到验证流程中。

Result: 在多种真实世界案例研究变体和多样EPIK实例化上的实验表明，该方法有效、灵活且具有通用性。

Conclusion: EPIK通过引入系统级可观察属性来替代难以获得的转移参数PK，显著提升了定量验证的准确性和鲁棒性，为贝叶斯验证提供了一种实用且可扩展的解决方案。

中文摘要：将贝叶斯学习与定量验证相结合，是分析软件系统关键定量属性（如可靠性和响应时间）的强大工具集。然而，验证结果的准确性和鲁棒性在很大程度上取决于贝叶斯推断所依据的先验知识（PK）。这种知识反映了对事件概率的原始信念，通常依赖于领域专业知识。使用不准确或信息量不足的PK会对定量分析产生负面影响，导致错误的验证结果。我们的EPIK方法通过引出并嵌入带有贝叶斯估计器的定量验证中的PK来应对这一重要挑战。与现有方法要求对形式化模型转移参数的PK不同，EPIK利用系统级属性，这些属性可直接观察并与现实世界语义相关联。EPIK制定了一个双重优化问题来推导未知转移参数的分布，然后将这些分布嵌入以验证新的或难以测量的（ elusive）属性。使用多种真实世界案例研究变体和不同EPIK实例化的详细实验评估显示了其有效性、灵活性和通用性。

Original abstract: Combining Bayesian learning and quantitative verification is a powerful toolset for analysing key quantitative properties of software systems, like reliability and response time. However, the accuracy and robustness of verification results strongly depend on the prior knowledge (PK) underlying Bayesian inference. This knowledge reflects original beliefs about the probability of events and typically depends on domain expertise. Using inaccurate or uninformative PK can negatively affect quantitative analysis, yielding incorrect verification results. Our EPIK approach tackles this important challenge by eliciting and embedding PK in quantitative verification equipped with Bayesian estimators. Unlike existing approaches that require PK on formal model transition parameters, EPIK leverages system-level properties that are directly observable and are linked to real-world semantics. EPIK formulates a twofold optimisation problem to derive the distributions of unknown transition parameters and then embeds these distributions to verify new or difficult-to-measure (elusive) properties. The detailed experimental evaluation using multiple variants of real-world case studies and diverse EPIK instantiations shows its effectiveness, flexibility and generality.

</details>


### [86] [CodeAssay: A Multi-Metric Benchmark with Audited Ground Truth for LLM Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03535)
*Shahbaz Siddeeq,Muhammad Waseem,Umar Subhan Malhi,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出了CodeAssay基准，通过审计真值、隐藏测试和代码属性度量，发现现有评估低估了模型间差异，且安全提示未显著改善安全属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试基准依赖的参考和测试可能不可靠，且只度量正确性，无法全面评估生成代码质量，需要更严谨的多维度评估方法。

Method: 构建CodeAssay，包含185个Python任务，结合审计真值、公共测试、隐藏测试、突变验证及代码属性度量，并评估多个专有LLM在不同提示下的表现。

Result: 重新评分改变了9%的标签并使模型差距从11.9增至23.7个百分点；隐藏测试突变分数74.8%；标准提示正确性77.3%-98.9%；安全提示未显著改善正确性或安全分析。

Conclusion: 可靠评估需要验证真值、受保护测试和多种明确度量，CodeAssay为LLM代码生成评估提供了可复现的基础。

中文摘要：大型语言模型越来越多地使用基于测试的基准来评估代码生成。此类评估的有效性取决于其参考和测试的可靠性，而基于测试的正确性仅捕获了生成代码可观察属性的一部分。我们提出了CodeAssay，一个以分类法为先导的基准，包含十个软件工程类别中的185个Python任务。它结合了经审计的真值、用于生成和修复的公共测试、用于评分的隐藏测试、基于突变的测试套件验证以及选定的代码属性度量。审计后重新评分固定模型输出，改变了1,890个正确性标签中的170个（9.0%），并将测得的模型最佳与最差之间的差距从11.9个百分点增加到23.7个百分点，尽管总体正确性几乎没有变化。完整测试套件和隐藏测试套件的突变得分分别为82.6%和74.8%。在七个专有LLM中，标准提示正确性范围为77.3%到98.9%，在21个模型对中有12对存在显著差异。在所有14个模型-提示配置都解决的120个任务上，没有模型在所有选定的代码属性上表现最佳。一个以安全为重点的提示在正确性上没有产生显著变化，也没有在选定的静态分析结果上带来一致的减少，但所有模型的程序长度和圈复杂度都有所增加。这些发现表明，可靠评估LLM生成的代码需要经过验证的真值、受保护的测试以及多个明确解释的度量。CodeAssay为AI增强软件开发中的循证模型评估提供了可复现的基础。

Original abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly evaluated for code generation using test-based benchmarks. The validity of such evaluations depends on the reliability of their references and tests, while test-based correctness captures only part of the observable properties of generated code. We present CodeAssay, a taxonomy-first benchmark of 185 Python tasks across ten software-engineering categories. It combines audited ground truth, public tests for generation and repair, hidden tests for grading, mutation-based test-suite validation, and selected code-property measures. Regrading fixed model outputs after the audit changed 170 of 1,890 correctness labels (9.0%) and increased the measured best-to-worst model spread from 11.9 to 23.7 percentage points, although aggregate correctness remained nearly unchanged. The complete and hidden test suites achieved mutation scores of 82.6% and 74.8%, respectively. Across seven proprietary LLMs, standard-prompt correctness ranged from 77.3% to 98.9%, with significant differences in 12 of 21 model pairs. On the 120 tasks solved by all 14 model-prompt configurations, no model performed best across all selected code properties. A security-focused prompt produced no significant change in correctness or consistent reduction in the selected static-analysis findings, while increasing program length and cyclomatic complexity across all models. These findings show that reliable evaluation of LLM-generated code requires validated ground truth, protected tests, and multiple explicitly interpreted measures. CodeAssay provides a reproducible basis for evidence-based model evaluation in AI-augmented software development.

</details>


### [87] [EffiHolmes: Differential Profiling-Guided Repository Level Time Inefficiency Fix Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03558)
*Haowen Yang,Yun Peng,Zishuo Ding*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: EffiHolmes是一个基于LLM的仓库级时间低效修复定位框架，通过差异剖析、紧凑执行路径提取和领域引导推理，有效定位性能热点；引入RepoEffi-Bench基准，在140个问题上显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型软件系统的时间低效问题难以定位，因为不像功能缺陷那样产生测试失败或堆栈线索，传统及基于LLM的故障定位方法不适用；运行时剖析虽有潜力，但在仓库级场景下面临单次运行噪声、执行路径提取困难以及热点与修复位置语义鸿沟三大挑战。

Method: EffiHolmes采用默认与缩放工作负载下的差异剖析识别低效热点，提取连接热点与所报告低效函数的紧凑执行路径，再利用领域引导的LLM推理定位底层低效逻辑；同时构建了包含140个高质量Python仓库问题的RepoEffi-Bench基准。

Result: 实验显示EffiHolmes一致优于最先进的检索、代理和剖析基线：使用GPT-5.1使文件级Acc@3提升4.29个百分点，使用qwen3-4b使函数级Acc@5提升15.00个百分点，且在不同模型容量下保持稳健。

Conclusion: EffiHolmes有效解决了仓库级时间低效修复定位难题，验证了差异剖析与LLM推理结合的优势，并提供首个专用基准，为后续研究与工具实践奠定基础。

中文摘要：大型软件系统常常遭受时间低效问题，导致尽管功能正确但执行时间过长。定位其修复位置很困难，因为与功能缺陷不同，它们既不产生测试失败，也不产生堆栈跟踪线索，使得传统和近期基于LLM的故障定位方法不适用。运行时剖析提供了替代证据，但在仓库级设置中面临三个挑战：单次运行剖析无法可靠地区分低效热点与执行噪声；现有剖析器难以从大量后台执行中提取相关执行路径；观察到的热点与实际修复位置之间仍存在语义鸿沟。我们提出EffiHolmes，一个基于LLM的仓库级时间低效修复定位框架。EffiHolmes在默认和缩放工作负载下使用差异剖析来识别低效热点，提取连接这些热点到所报告低效函数的紧凑执行路径，并采用领域引导的LLM推理来定位底层低效逻辑。我们还引入了RepoEffi-Bench，这是第一个仓库级低效定位基准，包含从流行Python仓库收集的140个高质量问题。实验表明，EffiHolmes始终优于最先进的基于检索、基于代理和基于剖析的基线，使用GPT-5.1将文件级Acc@3提高了4.29个百分点，使用qwen3-4b将函数级Acc@5提高了15.00个百分点。它在不同模型容量下也保持稳健。

Original abstract: Large software systems often suffer from time inefficiencies that cause excessive execution time despite functional correctness. Localizing their fix locations is difficult because, unlike functional bugs, they produce neither test failures nor stack-trace clues, making traditional and recent LLM-based fault localization methods unsuitable.
