Runtime profiling provides alternative evidence but faces three challenges in repository-level settings: single-run profiling cannot reliably distinguish inefficiency hotspots from execution noise; existing profilers struggle to extract relevant execution paths from extensive background execution; and a semantic gap remains between observed hotspots and actual fix locations. We propose EffiHolmes, an LLM-based framework for repository-level time inefficiency fix localization. EffiHolmes uses differential profiling under default and scaled workloads to identify inefficiency hotspots, extracts compact execution paths connecting these hotspots to the reported inefficient function, and employs domain-guided LLM reasoning to locate the underlying inefficiency logic. We also introduce RepoEffi-Bench, the first benchmark for repository-level inefficiency localization, containing 140 high-quality issues collected from popular Python repositories. Experiments show that EffiHolmes consistently outperforms state-of-the-art retrieval-, agent-, and profiling-based baselines, improving file-level Acc@3 by 4.29 percentage points with GPT-5.1 and function-level Acc@5 by 15.00 percentage points with qwen3-4b. It also remains robust across model capacities.

</details>


### [88] [From Bug Reports to Browser-Executable Procedures: An LLM-Driven Agent for Web GUI Bug Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03598)
*Cunming Zhang,Yu Pei,Michail Papadakis*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: ReBug是一个上下文感知的代理系统，通过重构缺失先决条件并在真实浏览器中执行状态感知的交互，从自然语言报告中自动复现Web GUI缺陷，在667个真实报告上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言缺陷报告常缺少依赖和输入文件等先决条件，现有复现技术无法端到端地验证Web GUI，导致复现困难。

Method: ReBug分为两个阶段：准备阶段从报告和工件中重构缺失先决条件并生成高层计划；执行阶段通过工具交互驱动浏览器，维护页面状态和动作历史，并基于报告预期验证最终状态。

Result: 在四个开源应用的667个真实缺陷报告上，平均RSR为49.96%，任务完成率74.96%，动作执行成功率86.54%，均优于基线；历史回放显示成功流程能暴露原始缺陷行为。

Conclusion: 显式上下文重构与状态感知浏览器执行可有效支持从报告派生的Web GUI复现，为自动化缺陷复现提供了可行方案。

中文摘要：从自然语言缺陷报告中复现Web GUI缺陷对于软件维护至关重要，但由于报告通常缺少依赖项和输入文件等先决条件，这一工作仍然困难重重。现有的缺陷复现技术主要针对代码单元或移动应用，缺乏对Web GUI的端到端可视化执行和验证。我们提出了ReBug，一个上下文感知的代理系统，通过驱动真实浏览器，从Web GUI缺陷报告中重构、执行并验证浏览器级别的复现流程。ReBug将复现分为两个阶段。在准备阶段，ReBug从报告和可用工件中重构缺失的先决条件，并生成高层级的复现计划。在执行阶段，它在浏览器中执行工具中介的交互，维护页面状态和动作历史的结构化摘要，并根据从报告中得出的预期来验证最终状态。我们在来自四个开源Web应用的667个真实缺陷报告上评估了ReBug。在受控的当前部署中，ReBug优于两个基线，平均RSR达到49.96%，平均任务完成率为74.96%，平均动作执行成功率为86.54%。我们的结果表明，显式的上下文重构和状态感知的浏览器执行有效支持了从报告派生的浏览器复现，而历史回放则表明，成功的复现流程在恢复的有缺陷版本上往往能暴露原始的存在缺陷行为。

Original abstract: Reproducing web GUI bugs from natural-language bug reports is critical for software maintenance, but remains difficult because reports often lack prerequisites such as dependencies and input files. Existing bug reproduction techniques mainly target code units or mobile applications and lack end-to-end visual execution and validation for web GUIs. We present ReBug, a context-aware agent system that reconstructs, executes, and validates browser-level reproduction procedures from web GUI bug reports by driving a real browser. ReBug separates reproduction into two stages. In the preparation stage, ReBug reconstructs missing prerequisites from the report and available artifacts, and it produces a high-level reproduction plan. In the execution stage, it performs tool-mediated interactions in the browser, maintains structured summaries of page state and action history, and validates the final state against expectations derived from the report. We evaluate ReBug on 667 real-world bug reports from four open-source web applications. On controlled current deployments, ReBug outperforms both baselines, achieving an average RSR of 49.96%, a mean task completion rate of 74.96%, and a mean action execution success rate of 86.54%. Our results show that explicit context reconstruction and state-aware browser execution effectively support report-derived browser reproduction, while historical replay shows that successful procedures often expose the original bug-present behavior on restored buggy versions.

</details>


### [89] [Pattern over Pixels: Measuring Pattern Completion Bias in Multimodal Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03691)
*Khai-Nguyen Nguyen,Oscar Chaparro,Antonio Mastropaolo*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态大语言模型在网页截图转代码任务中会受到重复UI模式的强烈影响，倾向于忽略局部扰动而输出模式一致但错误的答案，并通过新基准量化了这一偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在将网页截图转换为前端代码时，重复的UI模式可能导致模型忽视局部细节，产生视觉上符合模式但实际错误的输出，需要系统性地测试和量化这种偏差。

Method: 构建了第一个视觉模式完成偏见基准，从Design2Code数据集中选取30个网页，生成1,440个截图，包括结构卡片和文本样式模式，并设置标准与噪声叠加条件，评估五个前沿MLLM在填空任务中的表现。

Result: 所有模型均表现出对重复基线的高偏差：卡片宽度扰动平均偏差率69.78%，文本字体大小扰动为80.22%；平均准确率分别仅为21.17%和7.89%。噪声、细微扰动和边界位置会进一步提高偏差率。

Conclusion: 重复UI模式导致的多模态代码生成失败模式具有普遍性，其严重性与视觉显著性相关；增大推理努力可降低偏差，但模型即使识别异常也可能覆盖为模式一致答案，需在模型设计和评测中重视此问题。

中文摘要：多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）越来越多地被用于将网页截图转换为前端代码，但重复的UI模式可能使它们倾向于输出视觉上不正确但符合模式的结果。在这项工作中，我们测试了重复网页模式如何在客观的截图到代码填空任务中损害MLLM的准确性。我们引入了第一个用于视觉模式完成偏见的基准，其中重复UI模式中的一个局部元素被扰动，模型必须从截图和HTML上下文中恢复被遮挡的宽度或字体大小值。从Design2Code数据集中精选的30个网页开始，我们构建了1,440个评估截图，涵盖标准条件和噪声叠加条件下的结构卡片和文本样式模式。我们评估了五个前沿MLLM，发现它们都强烈偏向于重复基线。平均偏差率在卡片宽度扰动上达到69.78%，在文本字体大小扰动上达到80.22%，而平均准确率分别仅为21.17%和7.89%。Codex-5.3表现最佳，但在卡片上的准确率从68.61%下降到文本上的13.89%，而Flash-3.0在文本上的偏差率达到96.11%。噪声、更微妙的扰动和边界位置进一步增加了偏差率。推理分析进一步表明，更大的推理努力与更低的偏差相关，但定性证据显示，模型能够识别异常元素，但仍然用符合模式的答案覆盖它。我们的结果确定了多模态代码生成中的一个具体失败模式，并表明其严重性与视觉显著性密切相关。

Original abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to translate webpage screenshots into front-end code, but repeated UI patterns may sway them toward visually incorrect yet pattern-consistent outputs. In this work, we test how repeated webpage patterns hurt MLLM accuracy on an objective screenshot-to-code fill-in-the-blank task. We introduce the first benchmark for visual pattern-completion bias, where one localized element in a repeated UI pattern is perturbed and the model must recover the masked width or font-size value from the screenshot and HTML context. Starting from 30 webpages curated from the Design2Code dataset, we build 1,440 evaluated screenshots spanning structural card and text-style patterns under standard and noise-overlaid conditions. We evaluate five frontier MLLMs and find that all are strongly biased toward the repeated baseline. Mean bias rate reaches 69.78% on card-width perturbations and 80.22% on text font-size perturbations, while mean accuracy is only 21.17% and 7.89%, respectively. Codex-5.3 performs best but still drops from 68.61% accuracy on cards to 13.89% on text, while Flash-3.0 reaches 96.11% bias on text. Noise, subtler perturbations, and boundary positions further increase bias rate. Reasoning analysis further shows that greater reasoning effort correlates with lower bias, yet qualitative evidence reveals that models can identify the anomalous element and still override it with the pattern-consistent answer. Our results identify a concrete failure mode in multimodal code generation and show that its severity is strongly associated with visual saliency

</details>


### [90] [We Must Have Missed This Comment: Detecting and Repairing Stale Function References in Linux Kernel Comments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03734)
*Kexin Sun,Yunbo Lyu,Xutong Ma,Hongyu Kuang,Ratnadira Widyasari,He Zhang,Xiaoxing Ma,Julia Lawall,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出ReCite方法，利用Git历史和LLM检测并修复Linux内核注释中过时的函数引用，在v6.18-rc1上检测到869个问题，修复建议有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Linux内核演化导致注释中的函数引用过时，现有方法不适用于这种外部引起的陈旧性，需要自动检测和修复。

Method: 提出ReCite三阶段方法：检测未解析函数形式符号、通过Git历史追踪演化、基于LLM生成修复建议。

Result: 在Linux内核v6.18-rc1上检测到869个过时引用；200个抽样中89.0%有指导意义，42.5%可直接应用；提交75个补丁中50个被接受。

Conclusion: ReCite能有效识别和修复Linux内核注释中的过时函数引用，提升代码可维护性，并可扩展至其他场景。

中文摘要：随着Linux内核的发展，代码注释可能会变得过时，因为注释引用的函数可能被独立地重构或删除，而注释没有相应更新。这种过时的函数引用可能会误导维护者，从而阻碍代码理解。先前关于检测代码-注释不一致的工作主要侧重于解决Javadoc注释与其直接注释函数之间的语义错位，因此不适用于Linux内核中这种外部引起的过时性。因此，我们提出了ReCite，一个三阶段的方法来识别和修复这种过时的引用：（1）检测未解析的函数形式符号——注释中看起来引用函数但在当前代码库中找不到匹配函数的符号；（2）通过Git历史追踪每个未解析符号的演化历史；（3）生成基于LLM的修复建议，这些建议基于演化历史和当前代码上下文。在Linux内核v6.18-rc1上，ReCite检测到869个过时引用并生成了修复建议。对200个抽样修复的手动评估显示，178个（89.0%）提供了有用的修复指导，其中85个（42.5%）可直接应用。在我们提交的75个补丁中，50个已被接受。我们还对所有未解析的函数形式符号进行了实证研究。

Original abstract: As the Linux kernel evolves, code comments may become outdated, as the functions they reference can be refactored or removed independently without corresponding updates to the comments. Such stale function references can mislead maintainers and thus hinder code comprehension. Prior work on detecting code-comment inconsistency mainly focused on addressing semantic misalignment between Javadoc comments and their directly annotated functions, making them inapplicable to this type of externally induced staleness in the Linux kernel. Therefore, we propose ReCite, a three-stage approach to identify and repair such stale references: (1) detecting unresolved function-form symbols -- symbols in comments that appear to reference functions but for which no matching function can be found in the current codebase, (2) tracing the evolution history of each unresolved symbol through the Git history, and (3) generating LLM-based repair suggestions grounded in the evolution history and current code context. On Linux kernel v6.18-rc1, ReCite detects 869 stale references with generated repair suggestions. A manual evaluation on 200 sampled repairs shows that 178 (89.0%) provide useful repair guidance, with 85 (42.5%) directly applicable. Of our 75 submitted patches, 50 have been accepted. We also empirically study all unresolved function-form symbols.

</details>


### [91] [Can LLMs Test Terminal User Interfaces?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03743)
*Chao Peng,Ruida Hu,Ajitha Rajan,Tegawendé F Bissyandé,Jacques Klein,Cuiyun Gao*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 对 TUI 应用的自动化测试进行系统研究，发现 LLM 引导与随机探索各有优劣，且行覆盖率不能有效预测崩溃发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TUI 应用广泛但缺乏专用测试方法，现有测试主要忽略界面交互，需要系统化的基准和评估来推动改进。

Method: 调查 197 个实际 TUI 应用，构建跨四个框架的无头基准测试，利用 Docker 镜像插桩，在相同时间预算下比较四个 LLM 与随机探索，记录覆盖率、状态和崩溃。

Result: 无模型全面占优；随机基线因高吞吐量在前崩溃发现上有优势，但 LLM 每次交互更高效且能触达输入门控缺陷；自动推导启动输入收益最大；行覆盖率与崩溃发现弱相关。

Conclusion: 自动化 TUI 测试可行但仍需深入研究，随机基线和启动输入处理是关键，工具已开源。

中文摘要：终端用户界面（TUI）将 GUI 的有状态、面向屏幕的行为与终端部署相结合，现在在开发者工具中很常见。然而，它们缺乏专门的测试方法论。我们调查了 197 个现实世界的 TUI 应用：只有 12% 的测试代码涉及界面，其中 45% 的测试从不发送输入，而是检查静态帧。我们将这些应用转化为一个无头基准测试，涵盖 ratatui/Rust、bubbletea/Go、textual/Python 和 ink/TypeScript，并将每个应用打包为带插桩的 Docker 镜像。我们记录了可靠的行覆盖率和控件覆盖率、渲染的终端状态以及崩溃。在相同的墙钟时间预算下，我们比较了四个前沿 LLM 与随机探索。没有模型占主导地位。随机探索是一个强时间预算基线，但其崩溃优势来自更高的吞吐量：每次交互，LLM 引导更高效，并且独特地能到达输入门控故障。自动推导启动输入带来了最大的实际收益，使原本无法启动的应用得以运行。行覆盖率对崩溃发现的预测能力很差，削弱了其作为测试有效性代理指标的作用。自动化 TUI 测试是可行的，但远未解决，诚实的基线比模型选择更重要。我们发布了覆盖率工具 tuicov（https://github.com/tui-testing/tuicov）和测试框架 tuibot（https://github.com/tui-testing/tuibot）。

Original abstract: Terminal User Interfaces (TUIs) combine the stateful, screen-oriented behaviour of GUIs with terminal deployment and are now common in developer tools. Yet they lack a dedicated testing methodology. We survey 197 real-world TUI applications: only 12% of test code exercises the interface, and 45% of those tests never send input, checking a static frame instead. We turn these applications into a headless benchmark spanning ratatui/Rust, bubbletea/Go, textual/Python, and ink/TypeScript, packaging each as an instrumented Docker image. We record line and widget coverage where reliable, rendered terminal states, and crashes. Under equal wall-clock budgets, we compare four frontier LLMs with random exploration. No model dominates. Random is a strong time-budgeted baseline, but its crash advantage comes from higher throughput: per interaction, LLM guidance is more efficient and uniquely reaches input-gated faults. Automatically deriving launch inputs yields the largest practical gain, enabling applications that otherwise never start. Line coverage poorly predicts crash discovery, weakening it as a proxy for test effectiveness. Automated TUI testing is feasible but far from solved, and honest baselines matter more than model choice. We release the coverage tool tuicov at https://github.com/tui-testing/tuicov and the testing framework tuibot at https://github.com/tui-testing/tuibot.

</details>
